# Sodiel (S-W-D) — Secrets (Hearts 8)

**Sketch status:** Entry exists; solid Social mechanic. Review opened (post-Chayelon pass).
**Fit tag (current):** *(seam)* — cares about binding/cost, not information content; cheap secret would run the same mechanism and he never wants one.
**Rank/suit job:** Rank 8 binding order — Oaths / **Secrets** / Law / Chaos. Hearts = exclusive *we* of two on a kept thing, not intel tradecraft (Spades) or asset ledger (Diamonds).
**Root:** S-W-D (roster: carried from Music/Song retitle) — private truth / kept thing.
**Roster cold gloss:** *The Currency of Secrets — a private truth known only to two, its entire power derived from remaining unspoken.*
**Gender (sketches):** he/him.

---

## Process checks

1. **Hearts = Social, not love.** Current entry lists "lovers" first but mechanic is **any** two bound by a kept thing (conspirators equal). Love is a common customer; exclusivity of knowledge is the shop. Mild love-bleed in worshipper order, not in Design.
2. **Medieval / floating city:** private signal, conspiracy, concealed birth (Open Door parable) — already right; not NSA cosplay.
3. **Width (revised after user pushback):** Treating Secrets as a concept **naturally inflates** past bedroom/conspiracy dyads into **state secrets, spy networks, guild black books** — and that inflation is still Social if the core is **"who can you trust?"** not "what is the intel worth?" (Diamonds) or "how do you run the op?" (Spades tradecraft). The old "exactly two only" mechanic may be the **purest / atomic form**, not the **ceiling** of the portfolio.

---

## Width rethink: trust-bounded keep (user direction)

### The natural inflation

| Scale | Still "secrets"? | Social question |
|-------|------------------|-----------------|
| Two lovers / two conspirators | Yes (current sketch) | Who is inside this keep? |
| Small cell / cabal | Yes | Who is trusted with the thing? |
| Guild inner ring / officer list | Yes | Who is cleared? |
| State / city secret | Yes | Who is inside the confidence of the realm (or the plot against it)? |
| Spy network | Yes | Chain of trust; who may know, who must not |

If the domain is **Secrets as concept**, excluding the larger scales feels artificial. Readers and cults will go there anyway.

### What must stay Hearts (not inflate into wrong mode)

| Hearts (Sodiel) | Not Sodiel |
|-----------------|------------|
| **Who is inside the trust-boundary of the keep** | Value of the secret as asset → Diamonds-ish |
| Standing while the keep is closed to outsiders | Tradecraft, cover, dead drops → Spades/espionage flavor if ever |
| Betrayal / leak / wrong ear = graph break | Law of disclosure forms → Takanor |
| Trust: who may know | Honor of the name on a roll → Kevodon |

**Master cold gloss (candidate):**

> **Secrets — a kept thing defined by a trust-boundary.** Sodiel holds who is **inside** the confidence and who is **outside**. The keep can be two people or a hundred, so long as there is a real *we* of the trusted and a real exterior. He is present for the social fact of exclusive knowing — "who can you trust?" — not for the content's market value or the techniques of spycraft.

### Locked synthesis: "Two's a secret, three's a …" + least who know

Not pure dyad-only. Not "any size trust club is fine."

| Layer | Content |
|-------|---------|
| **Folk / cult adage** | **Two's a secret; three's a** [crowd / company / leak waiting to happen]. Keep as living proverb and parable pressure. |
| **Principle** | The keep should be the **smallest trust-set that can hold the thing** — least who know. |
| **Scale** | State secrets and spy networks still count **when** they are real closed confidences with an exterior — but every extra ear is **theological risk**, not neutral growth. |
| **Dissolve** | An **outsider** (or anyone beyond the legitimate least-set) installing ends or wounds standing for that keep. A third person is the **iconic** breach because three is where the adage says secrecy dies. |
| **Large keeps** | Possible but **brittle** and slightly impure; priests argue whether a war cabinet of twelve is still "secret" or already three's company in slow motion. Fiction gold. |

**Cold principle one-liner:**

> Sodiel is present where a thing is kept by **as few as must know**, bound by trust. Two is the blessed minimum and the proverb's pure form. Every name past need is a crack. The street is death.

**Not:** maximum classified bureaucracy (that's procedure / Diamonds-adjacent).  
**Not:** only romantic pairs.  
**Is:** need-to-know as **Social ethics of the keep** — who can you trust, and how few can you trust?

### Spy networks & state secrets under least-who-know

- **Spy network:** cells stay small on purpose — his cult practice; large all-hands briefings insult him.  
- **State secret:** legitimate if the set is need-bounded; "the whole officer corps" may already be three's company.  
- **Need-to-know** is his doctrine before it is paperwork.

### Risk of over-widen (still)

Confidential memo with no real trust-bond, open secret, or form-seal alone ≠ him. Live keep + least trust-set + exterior.

---

## What already works (protect under scale-up)

| Piece | Why |
|-------|-----|
| **Binding over content** | Seam — trust cost, not intel price |
| **Cheap / open "secrets" ignored** | Still true at state scale (propaganda everyone knows ≠ keep) |
| **Accident still dissolves** | Structural; child or street same as spy |
| **Dyad parables** | Icon / pure form — keep as teaching stories |
| **No public temple** | Closed rooms, sealed confidence; scales to cabinet |
| **Not Diamonds asset / not Spades tradecraft** | Design fence stays |

**Revised lean:** scale portfolio to trust-set at any size; generalize "third person" to **outsider / breach of the trust-set**; dyad remains purest icon.

---

## Soft spots / risks

1. **Worshipper list leads with lovers** — reorder: conspirators, co-keepers, lovers as case; avoids Ahavel shadow.
2. **"Tender" pickiness** — fine if it means care for costly trust; watch romance tone.
3. **Demahel fence:** unspoken to the one it concerns (often one-person) vs **keep already installed inside a trust-set**.  
4. **Shevuon:** spoken oath vs kept knowledge (may overlap when two swear silence).  
5. **Midvaron:** tale wants ears / circle; secret wants a **closed** trust-set (can be larger than two, still not "the whole caravan").  
6. **Chayelon:** wellness speech to a third / the hall can breach a keep.  
7. **Love-bleed:** lovers as case, not definition; state and cell as equal citizens of the concept.

---

## Cold gloss (candidate — adage + least-who-know)

> **Secrets — a keep held by the least who can be trusted.** Sodiel holds who is inside the confidence and that the set stay **minimal**. "Two's a secret; three's a …" is doctrine, not a hard ban on n>2: two is the pure form; each name past need is a crack; an outsider ear ends the hour. He cares about the trust-boundary and its size, not the market value of the content.

**Icon:** two keepers, third ear (Song That Died Public).  
**Portfolio:** any keep that is truly closed and need-bounded — lovers, cells, cabinets — judged by **least who know**, not by grandeur of the secret.

### Defense and flaw

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** | Will not bless open secrets, everyone-knows gossip, or bloated trust-sets that are secrecy theater. Least-who-know is the rule; two is the ideal. |
| **Flaw** | Breach is structural (child or spy). Large "necessary" keeps live in his weather uneasily; people kill to keep the set small. He does not rank just vs unjust content. |

Wrong court: asset value of intel; tradecraft how-to; published law (Takanor); love as such (Ahavel); group articles as such (Shalalon — unless the article *is* a secret keep).

---

## Fences (revised)

| God | They hold | He holds |
|-----|-----------|----------|
| **Demahel** | Not yet said to the concerned party | Installed keep inside a trust-set |
| **Ahavel** | Love-forms | Keep may ride love; state keep need not |
| **Shevuon** | Personal spoken oath | Kept confidence (sworn or not) |
| **Shalalon** | Published group standards | Closed confidence of a group (secret *we*, not the open charter) |
| **Midvaron** | Story for listeners / wind | Closed trust-set; not open tale |
| **Chayelon** | Speech that returns you to wide *we* | Can destroy a keep by widening the ear-set |
| **Heteron** | Unentered purchased exception | Adjacent; his is trust-who-knows, not only unlisted ledger |
| **Takanor** | Form witnessed | Content that must not be witnessed by outsiders |
| **Sodiel** | **Trust-boundary of the keep ("who can you trust?")** | |

---

## Personality (revised for scale)

Still quiet and selective — but **exact about the trust-set**, not only the number two.

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Exact about the boundary** | Who is in; who is out — two or twenty |
| **Quiet** | No public temple; presence is closed rooms, sealed lists, private rites |
| **Selective** | Wants costly trust; bored by gossip-that-everyone-knows |
| **Non-vengeful** | Breach → absence, not thunder |
| **Comfortable in cells and courts** | Same god in a lovers' keep and a war cabinet's closed session |

One-line: *He is the god of who may know — the lock of the trust-set, not the loot inside — and when the wrong ear enters, he is already gone.*

Dyad parables remain the purest teaching stories; state/spy stories teach scale and brittleness.

---

## Design (candidate)

Hearts 8 Secrets: Social Mode = **who can you trust + least who know**. Adage "two's a secret, three's a …" kept as doctrine/ideal; n>2 allowed when need requires but theologically risky. Portfolio includes cells/state/spy confidences that are truly closed and minimal; not tradecraft, not intel price, not open secrets. Outsider breach ends standing. Seam: costly binding over content; bored by cheap keeps. Defense/flaw: minimal trust-set // brittle large keeps, amoral collapse on leak. Icon parables stay dyadic; optional large-keep parable teaches brittleness. Fences Demahel, Midvaron, Chayelon, Shalalon, Takanor.

---



### Rank 10 principle (user — locked lean)

**Biomes are containers, not substrates.** Rank 10 is not "about the sand / the trees / the rocks / the mud" as stuff. Each suit reads **what that biome holds or does as mode weather**:

| Biome | Suit | Not the stuff | What it **contains / does** (mode) |
|-------|------|---------------|-------------------------------------|
| **Forests** | Spades | Not timber taxonomy | **Predatory cover** — formation erased; ground that hunts the disciplined line (Hard Mode restriction) |
| **Deserts** | Hearts | Not sand grains | **Mystique / sparse address** — stories on the wind, unanswered word, night-fire *we* (Social) |
| **Mountains** | Diamonds | **Not stacks of rock / load-pile architecture** | **Depths & resources** — caves, mines, seams, assay, what the mass holds (Systems entry of the deep) |
| **Swamps** | Clubs | Not mud chemistry | **Unemptiable return** — basin that comes back, growth that won't stay drained (Sandbox) |

So the Mountains reframe is not a special plea — it's **applying the same rule the row already uses**. Old Tzuron (span/gravity/foundation-stone) was treating the mountain as **building material / structural pile**, which is substrate-logic — and that's why she slid into Aretzon (Earth as foundation/base). Correct Diamonds read: mountain as **vault of the deep**, entry for contents.

**Aretzon fence under principle:** Earth Jack is element-as-foundation (base that doesn't move). Mountains 10 is biome-as-contents (what the high mass holds underneath). Different rank axes; no need to share "immovable rock woman" identity.

---
### User probe: Mountains = caves / mines / depths / resources (not stacks of rock)

**Idea:** Keep biome label **Mountains**. Change Systems subject from load-bearing *pile* (spans, gravity, first foundation stone) to what the mountain **holds and yields from within**: caves, mines, depths, resource bodies, assay, the deep account.

This is reportfolio of *function*, not of grid label — postcard tetrad intact; *tzur* still names the mountain; portfolio stops twinning Aretzon's surface foundation.

#### Why this works

| | **Aretzon (Earth)** | **Tzuron (Mountains, reframe)** |
|--|---------------------|----------------------------------|
| Layer | **Surface / base** — foundation claim, title, survey of ground you stand on or deed | **Depth / contents** — what the mass *contains*: ore, seam, void, grade, shaft account |
| Question | Is there a founding claim the system can rest on? | Is the **deep entry** true — grade, depth, support, reserve? |
| Failure | Broken title continuity; paper base with no standing | Wrong assay / wrong depth figure; mine that "worked" until the error found its level |
| Doesn't move | Foundation base of ownership | The mountain as **vault** — resource doesn't care about your surface story |
| Customers | Surveyors, land-holders, diaspora title | Miners, quarry-wards, assay masters, delvers, deep-guild engineers |
| Imagery | Boundary marker, deed, first stone of *title* | Adit, lamp, sample, seam map, timbered shaft |
| Parable shape | Perfect Deed (empty surface claim kept) | Wrong grade / thin timber / false reserve that held until it didn't |

**One-line split:** Aretzon = *who holds the ground.* Tzuron = *what the mountain holds.*

Mining **claim law** stays Aretzon (cadastral). **Geological/resource truth** of the hole stays Tzuron. Same ridge: her registry says you own it; Tzuron's column says whether the seam you entered was ever there.

#### Systems Mode (still Diamonds)

Not Clubs abundance (Zerael wants full harvest warmth). Not Spades "mountain kills the soft."  

**Cold object:** the **entered truth of the deep** — assay, reserve, depth, structure of the void — independent of hope, surface deed, or how long the gallery seemed fine.

| Keep from old Tzuron | Drop / rehome |
|----------------------|---------------|
| Late failure of wrong entry (Half-Right energy) | Bridge span as master icon |
| "Almost" not a column | First stone of *building* foundation (→ Aretzon title-stone or Harashon craft) |
| Unhurried severity optional | Mountain sovereign as pure gravity-on-architecture |
| Engineer customers (narrow to **mine/quarry/deep** structure) | General guild-architect of every freestanding span |

**Half-Right Span → portable:** e.g. **The False Seam** / **The Gallery That Held:** every measurement of the adit right except one grade or one timber figure; worked for eleven years; was wrong the day of the assay. Same systems beat, depth skin.

**Staked Span → retarget:** guild stakes youth to assay/mine-measure early; Tza'iron still calls theft of optionality. Shared parable survives with new trade.

#### vs neighbors

| God | Fence |
|-----|--------|
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title vs deep contents/resource figure |
| **Zerael** | Warm surface yield without theory vs entered deep reserve (cold systems) |
| **Rabakhon** | Biological replication process vs mineral/void extract account |
| **Ashiron** | Coin balance after sale vs resource still in the mountain / grade true |
| **Harashon** | Medium of the make (including timber/steel craft) vs whether the *hole's* numbers were true |
| **Chakamon** | Inherited manual copy vs this seam's assay (may use old book wrong) |
| **Ya'aron** | Cover erases formation on surface forest vs under-mountain systems |
| **Bitzon** | Ground that returns/grows vs mountain that is **extracted** (opposite vector) |
| **Taturiel** | First step on ridge vs first lamp in a mapped dark |

#### Personality (under reframe — options)

Depth weather differs from Aretzon's calm surface foundation:

| | Face | Play |
|--|------|------|
| **D1** | **Reserve / vault** | Holds much, reveals by measure; unhurried because the seam is older than the claim |
| **D2** | **Assay-severity** | Sample is sacred; "looks rich" is not a column; contempt for surface optimism |
| **D3** | **Deep-calm** | Quiet of caves; patient; not Aretzon's base-pride — **withholding** pride (the mountain keeps its own counsel until entered true) |

Avoid twinning Aretzon's exact "calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried" package — related still (both solid, both unhurried possible) but **identity** is vault/assay/depth, not foundation-that-doesn't-move.

#### Name

**Tzuron** (*tzur*) can **stay** — rock/mountain-fastness as the body that *contains* the deep, not as a stack of load-bearing stones. Design one-liner: mountains as resource-body / underworld of stone, not architecture.

#### Fit tag

Likely still *(clean)*: Mountains → mines/depths is a natural mortal read; load-span was the more forced pure-optimizer abstract. Optional *(seam)* if old span-cult minority remembers bridge-Tzuron — only if you want that history.

#### Lean

**Strong path:** Keep Mountains; reportfolio to **depths / mines / caves / resources**. Clears Aretzon without leaving the biome row. Rename optional (keep Tzuron). Rewrite worship, parable skin, Staked Span aim; drop foundation-stone collision entirely.

#### Why it can still be Diamonds

Clubs creep is real — own it as **face/method**, not domain swap:

| Diamonds core (keep) | Clubs-colored how |
|----------------------|-------------------|
| Deep entry must be true (assay, map of seam, depth figure) | She **wants to go get it** — truth of the mountain is verified by descent, not only by desk |
| Resource/contents systems | Adventure is how mortals *meet* the vault, not abundance-without-theory (Zerael) |
| Wrong entry fails later | She loves the delving *and* still won't bless a pretty lie about the seam |

**Cold gloss add:** present for the **entered deep** — and worshipped as one who is **glad to go down**. Domain is still contents/systems of the mountain; personality is delver-joy.

**If she only loved adventure with no care for the figure:** she'd be soft-Taturiel. Fence: she can love the trip and still be the god of **whether what you brought back (or claimed was down there) was entered true**.

#### Fences (critical)

| God | They hold | She holds |
|-----|-----------|-----------|
| **Taturiel** | First step; unmapped *as such*; discovery creates ground; forgets she's been | **Known mountain body** — depths of *this* mass; return with a true account; not "anywhere unmapped" |
| **Derorel** | Freedom as unclosed question / won't settle the life-question | Adventure as **method toward a deep entry**, not endless refusal to conclude |
| **Tza'iron** | Optionality; abandon the path; stake as theft | May love many descents; still honors a **finished true assay** of a seam (can finish a map) |
| **Sharaq** | Distribution, no destination | She **has** a destination: the deep / the seam / the chamber |
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title | Under the claim, what's held |
| **Yetziron** | Variation for its own sake | Variation of path only to get the deep figure right |
| **Midvaron** | Adventurous desert *story* heat | Adventurous **descent** — lamp, rope, rock dark; different register |

#### Tag implications

| Tag | If |
|-----|-----|
| **(clean)** | "Mountain contents" naturally attracts delver cults; adventure is how you meet assay — domain implies the face |
| **(seam)** | Domain is deep entry; **love of adventure** is extra worship built (nothing in pure assay requires joy) — apprentices describe thrill theology can't require |
| **(miscollapse)** | Older cult was pure vault/assay desk-god; delver joy is later drift |

**Lean:** **(seam)** is honest and good — same family as Chakamon warmth (nothing about transmission requires affection). Adventure-love is the seam that makes her vivid; assay remains the column.

#### vs Aretzon (still clear)

Aretzon: calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried foundation — **stays**.  
Tzuron: **moves into the dark on purpose** — opposite energy, same Diamonds suit. Twin problem dissolves.

#### Risks

1. **Taturiel bleed** — fix with "this mountain's deep," return-with-account, not first-print-anywhere.  
2. **Becomes pulp archaeologist only** — keep Systems: sample, map, grade, false seam parable.  
3. **Monsters/traps drift** — user veto: **no**; wonder of the deep, not bestiary.  
4. **Gender/Lara coding** — fine with she; competence and joy, not pinup checklist.

#### One-line (candidate)

> *She loves the going down — rope, lamp, the bend that isn't on the last survey — and still only blesses the descent that brings back a true account of what the mountain holds.*

#### Personality lock package (candidate)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Wanderlust / spelunker joy** | Lara Croft-without-monsters: competent, curious, glad of the deep |
| **Systems still** | Assay, seam map, depth figure, false entry fails later |
| **Seam** | Adventure-love not required by domain; cults built it |
| **Not** | Surface title (Aretzon); first step anywhere (Taturiel); abandon as theology (Tza'iron) |

---
## Notes status

**Sodiel synthesis:** trust-boundary + **least who know**; proverb two/three retained as pure form; state/spy in if need-bounded and closed. Awaiting final lock / sketches.

## Reconciled

**2026-08-07** — Written into pantheon-sketches.md. Widened from "exactly two, no more" to "least who can hold it" — dyad stays the pure/iconic case, cells and cabinets count when genuinely closed and kept minimal. Worshipped-by reordered (conspirators/co-keepers lead, lovers as one case) per the love-bleed soft spot. New Occupation Index row for spies/cabal officers. Tag stays *(seam)*. Open threshold question (soft vs hard impurity cutoff for large keeps) left unresolved by design — the text treats it as a live doctrinal argument among priests rather than settling it.

# Chalufon (Ḥ-L-P) — Trade (Hearts 7)

**Sketch status:** **LOCKED** (user confirmed — simple enough). Trade = act (social); Coins = tools; markets/caravans in; warm-transactional maximalist. Awaiting sketches write only.
**Fit tag (sketches):** *(clean)* — likely keep.
**Root:** Ḥ-L-P (*chalufin*, exchange/barter).
**Gender:** he/him.

---

## Locked split: Trade vs Coins

| | **Chalufon — Trade** | **Ashiron — Coins** |
|--|----------------------|---------------------|
| **What** | The **act of trading** — exchange as multiplayer social event | The **tools** of trading — coin, balance, fair weight, the figure that closes |
| **Mode** | Social: deal, relationship, market-as-people, caravan-as-company | Systems: does the column balance |
| **Includes** | Markets, caravans, docks, bargaining, favors, alliances, handshake and crowd of exchange | Specie, rate, shortfall, lightened purse, reconciliation |
| **Success** | Deal done / relation opened or held / traffic of people and goods | Account closes clean |

**User lock:** Chalufon **keeps markets and caravans** as the **social aspect** of trade. Ashiron when the **tool** (coin/balance) is the question. Same stall: shout and shake (Chalufon), weight of coins (Ashiron).

### Favor / unclosed debt

Still **core grammar** inside the wider portfolio (gift opens, repay closes, no rush to settle, Gift That Came Due). Not the whole sky — **all trading-as-social-act**, favor as central move.

### Markets & caravans (Social)

| In (Chalufon) | Out / Ashiron |
|---------------|---------------|
| Market day as people dealing | Purse light by exact debt |
| Caravan as company of exchange | Assay of metal |
| Bargaining, rapport, middlemen | Fair price as pure figure |
| Maximal deal-flow | Daily books closed true |

---

## Personality (locked)

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Warm** | Real pleasure in the deal and the counterparty |
| **Transactional** | Everything is a deal; warmth is not free |
| **Maximalist** | More trade, more road, more market; settle only to open the next |
| **Deal-maker charisma** | Design: "Trump with much better social skills" — expansive win-win pitch, relationship-as-deal, not cartoon pure grift |

**One-line:** Warm hand, open deal, maximal traffic — he loves the trade and the trader, and nothing is fully even for long.

### Defense and flaw

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** | No trade without persons (pure abstract tool-transfer alone goes to Ashiron). Never permanently done — always another deal. |
| **Flaw** | Maximal transactional warmth: people are counterparties; friendship can be a long position; better social skills make a smoother trap. |

---

## Fences

| God | They hold | He holds |
|-----|-----------|----------|
| **Ashiron** | Coins — tools; balance; close | Trade — act; market; caravan; deal |
| **Takshiton** | Love/courtship sacrifice gift | Deal-gift / alliance exchange |
| **Heteron** | Silent unentered exception | Social trade act (even shady) |
| **Sodiel** | Least who know | Who deals with whom |
| **Taturiel / Sharaq** | First step / distribution road | Caravan as trading company |
| **Asaphon** | Year entered | Market season as social weather |
| **Chalufon** | Trading as multiplayer social act | |

---

## Design (candidate)

Hearts 7 Trade = act of trading (social): markets, caravans, deals, favors. Ashiron = Coins (tools). Personality warm, transactional, maximalist; deal-maker with real social skill. Unclosed favor remains core grammar. Defense/flaw: always another deal // people as counterparties. (clean).

### Personality probe: spelunking wanderlust (Clubs creep) — Lara Croft without monsters

**User:** Allow Tzuron to creep a bit into Clubs — wanderlust, spelunking; loves the adventure; almost Lara Croft feel **without the monsters**.

#### What that face is

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Wanderlust** | Not content at the registry desk; the deep is for *going* |
| **Spelunker** | Caves, mines, adits, unmapped dark as joy, not only workplace |
| **Adventure-love** | Delight in the descent, the squeeze, the first lamp around a bend |
| **Competent delver** | Lara Croft competence/curiosity — **not** combat, tombs full of guardians, or trophy killing |
| **Not** | Monster-slayer; Taturiel's first-step-on-any-ridge; pure optionality abandon (Tza'iron); freedom-as-unclosed question (Derorel) |


#### User correction: not real Clubs theft

**User:** Spelunking only "creeps on Clubs" in a soft sense — **Clubs is not about exploration in specific**, so there is **no real suit-level fence**. Freedom-of-exploration energy is **inferred from uncontained/Sandbox mode**, not a portfolio Clubs monopolizes.

|---------|--|
| **Clubs mode** | Uncontained: growth without design, without a stop; optionality, distribution, abandon, first-step *as cards* — not "the adventure suit" |

| **Freedom (specific)** | **Derorel** — open question, not delving |
| **Tzuron adventure** | Personality + method for **Diamonds deep-entry** — does not steal a Clubs domain that doesn't exist as "exploration generally" |

**Implication:** Drop anxiety about "Clubs creep" as suit violation. Keep **card fences** only where real:
- **Taturiel** — mechanism of first-step/forgetting/unmapped, not "you can't enjoy caves"
- **Derorel / Tza'iron / Sharaq** — only if her joy becomes their cold gloss (endless unclosed, abandon-as-theology, no destination)

She can be fully Lara-Croft-delver-joyful **without** being a Clubs god. The uncontained *flavor* of wanderlust is available to any suit's face; Clubs doesn't own it.

# Tzelahar (Ṣ-L-L) — Hearth (Hearts 6)

**Sketch status:** Review — **stands well**. No rethink required.
**Fit tag:** *(clean)*. Rank 6 Mode within rules — household *we*; hospitality as tenant.
**Roster:** *The Held Fire*. Gender she/her.

## Verdict

**Agree: she stands.** Clean Social Mode seat. Mechanic, personality, parable, worship, Design fences aligned. Not love-train, not Beauty residue, not Festival-creep.

### What works

| Piece | Why |
|-------|-----|
| Standing household *we* | Fire / roof / place set |
| Hospitality as tenant | Guest = provisional inside; not second domain |
| No street after wrong exit; no pair that shuts house out | Fences Ahavel |
| Personality | Territorial-as-devotion; guest-right as house honor |
| Place Set Through Winter | Cost without ranking; threshold standing debate |
| Design fences | Chayelon, Yaladiel, Ahavel, Pituyel, Festival, Rank 6 band |
| Guest Under Articles | Shared parable with Shalalon |

### Process checks: all pass

Social not love; full domain not vignette; mode within rules; medieval register; low love-bleed.

### Optional polish only (not blocking)

1. Chayelon after Health widen: membership ≠ household health practices (same roof, different questions).
2. Tavern only if it runs as standing household *we*.
3. Explicit defense/flaw: no street as house; house honor can cost the house; threshold end is cold to the departing guest.
4. Festivals gap correctly declined — not her job.

### Recommendation

**Keep as written.** Optional one-line Design polish. No notes-heavy rethink.

# Yaladiel (Y-L-D) — Birth (Hearts 5)

**Sketch status:** **Rethink lean** — protective Chadashiel energy; excited about newness of life; **protector of newborns and infants**. Sketches still headcount-primary.
**Fit tag:** TBD (*(clean)* or *(seam)* if headcount folk remains).
**Root:** Y-L-D (*yalad*). Gender she/her.
**Roster (old):** Witnessed Arrival — retune toward protection of the arrived / earliest life.

---

## User direction

| From (sketches) | Toward |
|-----------------|--------|
| Loud headcount joy; private labor = sad | **Excited about newness of life** + **protective** of what arrived |
| Center = how many in the room | Center = **newborns and infants** (least able new members of the *we*) |
| vs Chadashiel | **Protective Chadashiel**: same thrill at *new*, specialized to **new life**, with **guard duration** through infancy (Chadashiel starts; does not guard) |
| Headcount | **Least important.** Folk in some areas only; quieter room can be better for protection; **no set limits** (no required crowd, no fixed max) |

### Cold gloss (candidate)

> **Birth — new life entering the multiplayer world, and the protection that life is owed while it cannot yet hold itself in the *we*.** Excited that something living and new has begun among people; fierce that it not be abandoned, exposed, or treated as non-member. Not fertility-process (Rabakhon), not general health (Chayelon), not every spring start (Chadashiel).

**Icon:** cradle / first days / arms around the infant — labor night as arrival start, protection continues through infancy.

### Social Mode

- New node in the *we*; infants cannot self-hold in multiplayer life.
- Exclusion = exposure, abandonment, unclaimed.
- Excitement at *new person*, not any new deal (Chadashiel).
- Protection = who stands between infant and harm/neglect.

---

## Fences (critical)

| God | Yaladiel vs them |
|-----|------------------|
| **Chadashiel** | New *starts* / no long guard vs new *life* + guards through infancy |
| **Chayelon** | General care-graph any age vs **domain priority** newborn/infant + arrival excitement |
| **Tzelahar** | House membership rules vs new one claimed and protected (house often the site) |
| **Rabakhon** | Process/heir ledger vs living child held safe among people |
| **Rifon** | Healing process vs protection ≠ cure |
| **Tza'iron** | Youth optionality vs infancy before choice |
| **Sodiel** | Concealment: protect *or* deny social claim — Open Door still gold, sharper under protector gloss |

Same cradle: Yaladiel "new life under my standing"; Chayelon "care-graph and wellness continue."

---

## Personality (locked)

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Warm-hearted** | Genuinely glad life arrived; not cold arithmetic, not clinical |
| **Welcomer of life** | Chadashiel-adjacent brightness aimed at *new living persons* — arrival is joy |
| **Fiercely protective of infants** | Until local handoff to **Tza'iron**; exposure, abandonment, asset-only treatment are intolerable |
| **Protective of the mother (generally)** | Mother is necessary for the infant's food at least until weaned — protecting the pair is protecting the charge; not full Chayelon wellness portfolio, but she will not treat the mother as disposable infrastructure |
| **Can be a bit smothering** | Flaw form of care — holds too tight, resists the handoff, crowds space Tza'iron wants open |
| **Less** | Pure headcount maximizer; mother-blind infant idol only |

**One-line:** *Warm welcomer of new life, fierce over infants until they pass to Tza'iron, generally shielding of the nursing mother too — and sometimes smothering in the holding.*

### Defense / flaw

| Defense | Flaw |
|---------|------|
| No abandonment/exposure of newborns/infants; will not leave mother unsupported while the infant still depends on her for food | Smothering; may resist or resent the handoff; can undervalue hard medical calls (Rifon); concealment tension (Sodiel); mother-protection is instrumental-to-infant plus genuine warmth, not full Health domain (Chayelon) |

---

## Old material / headcount (locked demotion)

| Old | Under rethink |
|-----|----------------|
| **Headcount as blessing** | **Least important.** Not her measure. Some districts keep open-door / crowd customs as **folk tradition only**. |
| **Limiting the room** | Compatible with protection rethink — fewer people can mean calmer mother, safer infant, clearer claim of the actual *we* that will feed and hold the child. **No set limits** (no cosmic “must be N” or “must be under N”). |
| Crowded Room parable | Demote, retire, or retell as local custom story — not orthodoxy |
| Open door worship | Optional folk; closed door fine if infant (and nursing mother) are protected |
| Loudest Heart / party volume | Protective fierceness, not headcount volume |

**Doctrine one-liner:** She does not count noses. She counts whether the newest life (and the one who feeds it) is held safe until the handoff.

---

## Design (candidate)

Hearts 5 Birth: **new life + protector of newborns/infants** until **local handoff to Tza'iron**. Warm-hearted welcomer; fiercely protective; generally shields mother too (esp. while infant food-depends on her); can smother. **Headcount least important** — folk custom some places; limiting the room can be better; no set limits. Fences Chayelon, Chadashiel, Rabakhon, Tza'iron.

---

## Handoff: Yaladiel → Tza'iron (Youth)

**Agreed idea:** a living tradition when the child passes from Yaladiel's care (protected newest life) into Tza'iron's weather (optionality / try-and-abandon). Same rank-5 row, Hearts → Clubs.

| God before | God after |
|------------|-----------|
| **Yaladiel** | Protection; cannot self-hold in the *we*; new life as charge |
| **Tza'iron** | Optionality; potential not narrowed; attends trying **and** abandoning |

Handoff should mark: **protection-primary ends; open-path youth begins** — not "child stops needing care" (Chayelon may still sit) and not "household membership starts" (Tzelahar may already have claimed them).

### Milestone options

| Milestone | Pros | Cons |
|-----------|------|------|
| **First steps** | Strong metaphor: enters the world **on their own legs**; agency/sandbox begins; visual rite (path, threshold, held then released). Matches Tza'iron's "direction taken." | Timing varies; some children late; medical edge cases |
| **First words** | Joins multiplayer with **own voice**; Social→Sandbox as speech becomes self-authored; rite of name said by the child. | "Word" disputed (babble vs word); quiet/disabled children need pastoral answer |
| **Defined age** | Clean for law/guild; easy calendar feast | Cold; fights both gods' lived feel (protection and optionality aren't birthdays) |
| **Priest blessing** | Institutional control; can combine with any of the above | Can feel arbitrary; which cult's priest — hers, his, or both? Best as **rite that *marks* a milestone**, not the milestone itself |
| **Weaning** | Clear bodily independence from pure dependence | Intimate/variable; less "optionality," more nutrition |
| **First deliberate abandon** (toy put down, path not taken) | Pure Tza'iron seam | Hard to ritualize; too late/subtle for handoff feast |

### Locked: milestone is **local** (city / temple / culture)

**No single cosmic clock.** The handoff is real; **when** it fires is a **mortal/cultural call**.

| Layer | Content |
|-------|---------|
| **Doctrine (shared)** | There *is* a passage from Yaladiel's protection of newest life into Tza'iron's optionality of youth. Both cults acknowledge a before and after. |
| **Variable (local)** | What *counts* as the sign — first steps, first words, weaning, named age, first day outside the house alone, priest-witnessed feast, etc. — is **city-by-city, temple-by-temple, culture-by-culture**. |
| **Why this works** | Matches Skyfall's fractured floating-city / district theology; same pattern as dual parable readings and Midvaron regional color. Avoids ableist universal body-milestones as divine law. |
| **Conflict fuel** | Two cities disagree whether a child is still under Yaladiel or already Tza'iron's — custody, apprenticeship, ritual insult, marriageable-path timing. |

**Not locked as universal:** first steps (or any single milestone).  
**Still useful as examples** in prose: "in the dock wards they wait for first steps; in the high temples, first words; in X, the third-year feast."

**Priest role:** local cults **declare and ritually mark** the handoff per their rule; dual blessing common where both temples are strong. Mercy/override for children who will not meet the local bodily sign remains a local pastoral problem, not a single grid rule.

**Continuous under both:** Chayelon (care) and Tzelahar (house) need not hand off.

### Design one-liner

> Yaladiel holds newest life until the culture's sign that optionality begins; Tza'iron holds youth thereafter. The sign is mortal custom, not a fixed cosmic milestone.

### Open on handoff

- [x] Milestone universality — **no**; local/cultural.
- [ ] Example set for a few Skyfall cultures (optional flavor pass).
- [ ] Shared parable of two cities disagreeing on a child's standing?
- [ ] Sketches: one sentence under both gods' Design or a Shared Parable note.

---

## Open questions

- [x] Infancy end — **local handoff to Tza'iron** (culture/temple/city).
- [x] Mother — **generally protected** (esp. nursing / infant food-dependence); not mother-blind.
- [x] Personality — warm welcomer; fierce over infants until handoff; can smother.
- [x] Headcount — **least important**; some areas folk only; quieter room can be better; **no set limits**.
- [ ] Sketches rewrite when locked (Crowded Room / open-door worship rewrite or demote).


### Personality probe: spelunking wanderlust (Clubs creep) — Lara Croft without monsters

**User:** Allow Tzuron to creep a bit into Clubs — wanderlust, spelunking; loves the adventure; almost Lara Croft feel **without the monsters**.

#### What that face is

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Wanderlust** | Not content at the registry desk; the deep is for *going* |
| **Spelunker** | Caves, mines, adits, unmapped dark as joy, not only workplace |
| **Adventure-love** | Delight in the descent, the squeeze, the first lamp around a bend |
| **Competent delver** | Lara Croft competence/curiosity — **not** combat, tombs full of guardians, or trophy killing |
| **Not** | Monster-slayer; Taturiel's first-step-on-any-ridge; pure optionality abandon (Tza'iron); freedom-as-unclosed question (Derorel) |


#### User correction: not real Clubs theft

**User:** Spelunking only "creeps on Clubs" in a soft sense — **Clubs is not about exploration in specific**, so there is **no real suit-level fence**. Freedom-of-exploration energy is **inferred from uncontained/Sandbox mode**, not a portfolio Clubs monopolizes.

| Clarify | |
|---------|--|
| **Clubs mode** | Uncontained: growth without design, without a stop; optionality, distribution, abandon, first-step *as cards* — not "the adventure suit" |
| **Exploration (specific)** | **Taturiel** alone (Queen) — first step, unmapped, retains nothing |
| **Freedom (specific)** | **Derorel** — open question, not delving |
| **Tzuron adventure** | Personality + method for **Diamonds deep-entry** — does not steal a Clubs domain that doesn't exist as "exploration generally" |

**Implication:** Drop anxiety about "Clubs creep" as suit violation. Keep **card fences** only where real:
- **Taturiel** — mechanism of first-step/forgetting/unmapped, not "you can't enjoy caves"
- **Derorel / Tza'iron / Sharaq** — only if her joy becomes their cold gloss (endless unclosed, abandon-as-theology, no destination)

She can be fully Lara-Croft-delver-joyful **without** being a Clubs god. The uncontained *flavor* of wanderlust is available to any suit's face; Clubs doesn't own it.

**Revised framing:** Not "Clubs-colored seam." Prefer: **delver joy as (seam) on Systems deep-contents** — extra worship built on assay/depths, parallel Chakamon warmth. Optional note that Sandbox *mode* also loves going further, but that's mode rhyme not portfolio theft.

---
#### Why it can still be Diamonds

Clubs creep is real — own it as **face/method**, not domain swap:

| Diamonds core (keep) | Clubs-colored how |
|----------------------|-------------------|
| Deep entry must be true (assay, map of seam, depth figure) | She **wants to go get it** — truth of the mountain is verified by descent, not only by desk |
| Resource/contents systems | Adventure is how mortals *meet* the vault, not abundance-without-theory (Zerael) |
| Wrong entry fails later | She loves the delving *and* still won't bless a pretty lie about the seam |

**Cold gloss add:** present for the **entered deep** — and worshipped as one who is **glad to go down**. Domain is still contents/systems of the mountain; personality is delver-joy.

**If she only loved adventure with no care for the figure:** she'd be soft-Taturiel. Fence: she can love the trip and still be the god of **whether what you brought back (or claimed was down there) was entered true**.

#### Fences (critical)

| God | They hold | She holds |
|-----|-----------|-----------|
| **Taturiel** | First step; unmapped *as such*; discovery creates ground; forgets she's been | **Known mountain body** — depths of *this* mass; return with a true account; not "anywhere unmapped" |
| **Derorel** | Freedom as unclosed question / won't settle the life-question | Adventure as **method toward a deep entry**, not endless refusal to conclude |
| **Tza'iron** | Optionality; abandon the path; stake as theft | May love many descents; still honors a **finished true assay** of a seam (can finish a map) |
| **Sharaq** | Distribution, no destination | She **has** a destination: the deep / the seam / the chamber |
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title | Under the claim, what's held |
| **Yetziron** | Variation for its own sake | Variation of path only to get the deep figure right |
| **Midvaron** | Adventurous desert *story* heat | Adventurous **descent** — lamp, rope, rock dark; different register |

#### Tag implications

| Tag | If |
|-----|-----|
| **(clean)** | "Mountain contents" naturally attracts delver cults; adventure is how you meet assay — domain implies the face |
| **(seam)** | Domain is deep entry; **love of adventure** is extra worship built (nothing in pure assay requires joy) — apprentices describe thrill theology can't require |
| **(miscollapse)** | Older cult was pure vault/assay desk-god; delver joy is later drift |

**Lean:** **(seam)** is honest and good — same family as Chakamon warmth (nothing about transmission requires affection). Adventure-love is the seam that makes her vivid; assay remains the column.

#### vs Aretzon (still clear)

Aretzon: calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried foundation — **stays**.  
Tzuron: **moves into the dark on purpose** — opposite energy, same Diamonds suit. Twin problem dissolves.

#### Risks

1. **Taturiel bleed** — fix with "this mountain's deep," return-with-account, not first-print-anywhere.  
2. **Becomes pulp archaeologist only** — keep Systems: sample, map, grade, false seam parable.  
3. **Monsters/traps drift** — user veto: **no**; wonder of the deep, not bestiary.  
4. **Gender/Lara coding** — fine with she; competence and joy, not pinup checklist.

#### One-line (candidate)

> *She loves the going down — rope, lamp, the bend that isn't on the last survey — and still only blesses the descent that brings back a true account of what the mountain holds.*

#### Personality lock package (candidate)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Wanderlust / spelunker joy** | Lara Croft-without-monsters: competent, curious, glad of the deep |
| **Systems still** | Assay, seam map, depth figure, false entry fails later |
| **Seam** | Adventure-love not required by domain; cults built it |
| **Not** | Surface title (Aretzon); first step anywhere (Taturiel); abandon as theology (Tza'iron) |

New parable, The Held Door, replaces The Crowded Room. Handoff to Tza'iron written as explicit, deliberately local doctrine — new Shared Parable, The Line Not the Same in Both Wards. # Tza'iron (Ṣ-ʿ-R) — Youth (Clubs 5)

**Sketch status:** Review opened (paired with Yaladiel handoff work). Entry already strong.
**Fit tag:** *(seam)* — attends abandoning as much as trying.
**Rank/suit job:** Rank 5 Youth (Clubs) opposite Death / Birth / Elderly. Sandbox = **optionality** not yet narrowed.
**Root:** Ṣ-ʿ-R (*tza'ir*, young/small).
**Roster:** *The Unfinished Shape — growth that hasn't decided yet what it's going to be, and resents anyone who tries to decide for it too early.*
**Gender:** he/him (sketches).

---

## Process checks

1. **Clubs = Sandbox.** Pass — try, abandon, no forced final form; not Social headcount or care-graph.
2. **Not Hearts-by-mistake.** Design correctly kept him Clubs; Youth-as-optionality ≠ Yaladiel's protection of newest life.
3. **Width.** "Youth" as concept could mean all minors forever. Cold gloss should stay **unfinished shape / open paths**, not "god of every child until 18." After paths narrow (commitment, staking), his standing thins — Kevodon/Hararon/trade-master weather, not his.
4. **Handoff from Yaladiel.** Local cultural milestone starts his weather; she can smother into his zone (flaw collision).
5. **Medieval / floating city.** Apprentice try-several-trades, sapling stake — on-register.

---

## What works (protect)

| Piece | Why |
|-------|-----|
| **Optionality mechanic** | Pure Sandbox on a person |
| **Withdraws if forced final too early** | Defense clear; well-meant still counts as theft |
| **Attends abandoning = trying** | Seam + personality gold; parents hate it |
| **Bent Sapling** | Icon parable |
| **Staked Span × Tzuron** | Shared parable; guild vs optionality |
| **Worship: multiple trades before commit** | Playable |
| **Roster Unfinished Shape** | Matches mechanic |

**Default lean:** **stands well** with light integration of Yaladiel handoff + explicit defense/flaw + end-of-youth boundary. Not a full rethink unless you want one.

---

## Soft spots / discussion

1. **When does Youth end?** (mirror of Yaladiel infancy end)  
   - First binding commitment?  
   - Local custom again?  
   - "When the shape is finished" (subjective — seam)  
   - Never fully (always one more abandon) — maximalist Clubs, may steal Derorel/Yetziron  

2. **Yaladiel handoff** — receive the child at local sign; personality clash with smothering mother-god energy; he delights in release into try/fail.

3. **Chadashiel** — both love new directions; she is social starts, he is personal path optionality; abandoning a start may please him and leave her weather.

4. **Chayelon** — care continues; forcing a sick youth into one path is still his insult if the force is early-shape.

5. **Harashon / Tzuron / Chakamon** — craft rules, load number, copy-manual: all can "stake" too early (Staked Span).

6. **Personality:** full delight at every new direction — playful Sandbox, not protective. Vs Yaladiel's fierce hold: he is **release and remix**.

7. **Seam keep?** Yes — abandoning-as-worship is the knife.

---

## Cold gloss (candidate confirm)

> **Youth — the unfinished shape.** Tza'iron holds potential that has not yet been forced into a single outcome. He is present for trying and for abandoning. He withdraws from early staking, kind or cruel. He does not hold infancy protection (Yaladiel), household membership (Tzelahar), or general health (Chayelon).

**Start of standing (with Yaladiel rethink):** after **local handoff** from her care.  
**End of standing:** when the culture (or the person) treats the shape as settled — or when forcing is complete and only the crooked tree remains (Bent Sapling as aftermath, not his living weather).

### Defense and flaw

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** | Will not underwrite premature final form — apprenticeship locked too early, path chosen for the child, stake driven in "for their own good." **Protective against growing up too fast** — not soft parenting, structural refusal of early stakes. |
| **Flaw (locked lean)** | Can **get lost in the abandon himself** — not quite childish, but so allied with unfinishedness and walking away that commitment, finishing, and necessary growing-up look like the enemy. The imaginary friend who never wants the game to become a life. Parents/guilds experience theft of certainty; the youth may never be helped to *arrive*. Staked Span still reads as his critique of the guild — and his blind spot when a stake was actually earned and timely. |

### Personality (locked lean)

**Design vibes (Earth-only):** *Bridge to Terabithia* + *Where the Wild Things Are* — wild play, secret kingdoms of maybe, the friend who makes the woods bigger than the rules.

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Fun older brother** | Not parent, not judge — pulls you into the next try; teases stakes and early "serious" paths |
| **Mischievous** | Delight in rule-bending play, abandoned paths, "what if we don't finish that" |
| **Creative** | Invents options; sandbox imagination — fence **Yetziron** (works/variation as domain) vs **life-path optionality** as play with a self |
| **Perfect imaginary friend** | Present in the unfinished, the half-built fort, the trade not kept; vanishes when the shape is forced final (or the adult world wins) |
| **Equal love of abandoning** | Seam kept: fourth abandoned trade = first attempt; parents still hate this |
| **Not smother** | Opposite of Yaladiel's hold; handoff is release into *his* kind of care — play-care, not cradle-care |

**One-line:** *Fun older-brother mischief: imaginary friend of the unfinished self — thrilled at try and abandon, protective against growing up too fast, and liable to get lost in the walking-away.*

**Not:** Yaladiel's warm fierce protector; Midvaron's sultry desert storyteller (different mischief); pure abstract "optionality engine" without face; cartoon eternal child (he's not quite childish — he's anti-premature-adult).

---

## Fences

| God | They hold | He holds |
|-----|-----------|----------|
| **Yaladiel** | Newborn/infant protection until local handoff | Optionality after; resists her smother past the sign |
| **Chadashiel** | Social new starts | Personal path branching / unfinished self |
| **Chayelon** | Care-graph any age | Not wellness; open shape |
| **Tzelahar** | House membership | Not whose fire; whose future paths |
| **Yetziron** | Creativity variation in works | Optionality in a **life/path**, not only art |
| **Derorel** | Freedom / fence unreality | Youth unfinishedness, not full free-from-ownership |
| **Tzuron / Harashon / Chakamon** | Measure, medium, inherited instruction | Early stake as theft of optionality |
| **Koseb** | Elderly account settle | Opposite life-edge |

---

## Handoff (cross-ref Yaladiel)

- Doctrine: passage exists.  
- Milestone: **local** (city/temple/culture).  
- His cult: celebrates the release into try-space; may mock districts that hand off too late (Yaladiel smother) or too early (theft from her, still infant).  
- Shared parable seed: two cities fight over a child's standing after first steps in one and not the other.

---

## Verdict

| Path | |
|------|--|
| **A. Stands well + handoff line** | Recommended default |
| **B. Explicit end-of-youth rule** | Local again, or "when committed/staked" |
| **C. Deeper rethink** | Only if optionality feels wrong |

**Lean:** A. Mechanic and seam are load-bearing; integrate Yaladiel handoff in Design; optional end boundary parallel to local handoff.



### Rank 10 principle (user — locked lean)

**Biomes are containers, not substrates.** Rank 10 is not "about the sand / the trees / the rocks / the mud" as stuff. Each suit reads **what that biome holds or does as mode weather**:

| Biome | Suit | Not the stuff | What it **contains / does** (mode) |
|-------|------|---------------|-------------------------------------|
| **Forests** | Spades | Not timber taxonomy | **Predatory cover** — formation erased; ground that hunts the disciplined line (Hard Mode restriction) |
| **Deserts** | Hearts | Not sand grains | **Mystique / sparse address** — stories on the wind, unanswered word, night-fire *we* (Social) |
| **Mountains** | Diamonds | **Not stacks of rock / load-pile architecture** | **Depths & resources** — caves, mines, seams, assay, what the mass holds (Systems entry of the deep) |
| **Swamps** | Clubs | Not mud chemistry | **Unemptiable return** — basin that comes back, growth that won't stay drained (Sandbox) |

So the Mountains reframe is not a special plea — it's **applying the same rule the row already uses**. Old Tzuron (span/gravity/foundation-stone) was treating the mountain as **building material / structural pile**, which is substrate-logic — and that's why she slid into Aretzon (Earth as foundation/base). Correct Diamonds read: mountain as **vault of the deep**, entry for contents.

**Aretzon fence under principle:** Earth Jack is element-as-foundation (base that doesn't move). Mountains 10 is biome-as-contents (what the high mass holds underneath). Different rank axes; no need to share "immovable rock woman" identity.

---
### User probe: Mountains = caves / mines / depths / resources (not stacks of rock)

**Idea:** Keep biome label **Mountains**. Change Systems subject from load-bearing *pile* (spans, gravity, first foundation stone) to what the mountain **holds and yields from within**: caves, mines, depths, resource bodies, assay, the deep account.

This is reportfolio of *function*, not of grid label — postcard tetrad intact; *tzur* still names the mountain; portfolio stops twinning Aretzon's surface foundation.

#### Why this works

| | **Aretzon (Earth)** | **Tzuron (Mountains, reframe)** |
|--|---------------------|----------------------------------|
| Layer | **Surface / base** — foundation claim, title, survey of ground you stand on or deed | **Depth / contents** — what the mass *contains*: ore, seam, void, grade, shaft account |
| Question | Is there a founding claim the system can rest on? | Is the **deep entry** true — grade, depth, support, reserve? |
| Failure | Broken title continuity; paper base with no standing | Wrong assay / wrong depth figure; mine that "worked" until the error found its level |
| Doesn't move | Foundation base of ownership | The mountain as **vault** — resource doesn't care about your surface story |
| Customers | Surveyors, land-holders, diaspora title | Miners, quarry-wards, assay masters, delvers, deep-guild engineers |
| Imagery | Boundary marker, deed, first stone of *title* | Adit, lamp, sample, seam map, timbered shaft |
| Parable shape | Perfect Deed (empty surface claim kept) | Wrong grade / thin timber / false reserve that held until it didn't |

**One-line split:** Aretzon = *who holds the ground.* Tzuron = *what the mountain holds.*

Mining **claim law** stays Aretzon (cadastral). **Geological/resource truth** of the hole stays Tzuron. Same ridge: her registry says you own it; Tzuron's column says whether the seam you entered was ever there.

#### Systems Mode (still Diamonds)

Not Clubs abundance (Zerael wants full harvest warmth). Not Spades "mountain kills the soft."  

**Cold object:** the **entered truth of the deep** — assay, reserve, depth, structure of the void — independent of hope, surface deed, or how long the gallery seemed fine.

| Keep from old Tzuron | Drop / rehome |
|----------------------|---------------|
| Late failure of wrong entry (Half-Right energy) | Bridge span as master icon |
| "Almost" not a column | First stone of *building* foundation (→ Aretzon title-stone or Harashon craft) |
| Unhurried severity optional | Mountain sovereign as pure gravity-on-architecture |
| Engineer customers (narrow to **mine/quarry/deep** structure) | General guild-architect of every freestanding span |

**Half-Right Span → portable:** e.g. **The False Seam** / **The Gallery That Held:** every measurement of the adit right except one grade or one timber figure; worked for eleven years; was wrong the day of the assay. Same systems beat, depth skin.

**Staked Span → retarget:** guild stakes youth to assay/mine-measure early; Tza'iron still calls theft of optionality. Shared parable survives with new trade.

#### vs neighbors

| God | Fence |
|-----|--------|
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title vs deep contents/resource figure |
| **Zerael** | Warm surface yield without theory vs entered deep reserve (cold systems) |
| **Rabakhon** | Biological replication process vs mineral/void extract account |
| **Ashiron** | Coin balance after sale vs resource still in the mountain / grade true |
| **Harashon** | Medium of the make (including timber/steel craft) vs whether the *hole's* numbers were true |
| **Chakamon** | Inherited manual copy vs this seam's assay (may use old book wrong) |
| **Ya'aron** | Cover erases formation on surface forest vs under-mountain systems |
| **Bitzon** | Ground that returns/grows vs mountain that is **extracted** (opposite vector) |
| **Taturiel** | First step on ridge vs first lamp in a mapped dark |

#### Personality (under reframe — options)

Depth weather differs from Aretzon's calm surface foundation:

| | Face | Play |
|--|------|------|
| **D1** | **Reserve / vault** | Holds much, reveals by measure; unhurried because the seam is older than the claim |
| **D2** | **Assay-severity** | Sample is sacred; "looks rich" is not a column; contempt for surface optimism |
| **D3** | **Deep-calm** | Quiet of caves; patient; not Aretzon's base-pride — **withholding** pride (the mountain keeps its own counsel until entered true) |

Avoid twinning Aretzon's exact "calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried" package — related still (both solid, both unhurried possible) but **identity** is vault/assay/depth, not foundation-that-doesn't-move.

#### Name

**Tzuron** (*tzur*) can **stay** — rock/mountain-fastness as the body that *contains* the deep, not as a stack of load-bearing stones. Design one-liner: mountains as resource-body / underworld of stone, not architecture.

#### Fit tag

Likely still *(clean)*: Mountains → mines/depths is a natural mortal read; load-span was the more forced pure-optimizer abstract. Optional *(seam)* if old span-cult minority remembers bridge-Tzuron — only if you want that history.

#### Lean

**Strong path:** Keep Mountains; reportfolio to **depths / mines / caves / resources**. Clears Aretzon without leaving the biome row. Rename optional (keep Tzuron). Rewrite worship, parable skin, Staked Span aim; drop foundation-stone collision entirely.

| Resource/contents systems | Adventure is how mortals *meet* the vault, not abundance-without-theory (Zerael) |
| Wrong entry fails later | She loves the delving *and* still won't bless a pretty lie about the seam |

**Cold gloss add:** present for the **entered deep** — and worshipped as one who is **glad to go down**. Domain is still contents/systems of the mountain; personality is delver-joy.

**If she only loved adventure with no care for the figure:** she'd be soft-Taturiel. Fence: she can love the trip and still be the god of **whether what you brought back (or claimed was down there) was entered true**.

#### Fences (critical)

| God | They hold | She holds |
|-----|-----------|-----------|
| **Taturiel** | First step; unmapped *as such*; discovery creates ground; forgets she's been | **Known mountain body** — depths of *this* mass; return with a true account; not "anywhere unmapped" |
| **Derorel** | Freedom as unclosed question / won't settle the life-question | Adventure as **method toward a deep entry**, not endless refusal to conclude |
| **Tza'iron** | Optionality; abandon the path; stake as theft | May love many descents; still honors a **finished true assay** of a seam (can finish a map) |
| **Sharaq** | Distribution, no destination | She **has** a destination: the deep / the seam / the chamber |
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title | Under the claim, what's held |
| **Yetziron** | Variation for its own sake | Variation of path only to get the deep figure right |
| **Midvaron** | Adventurous desert *story* heat | Adventurous **descent** — lamp, rope, rock dark; different register |

#### Tag implications

| Tag | If |
|-----|-----|
| **(clean)** | "Mountain contents" naturally attracts delver cults; adventure is how you meet assay — domain implies the face |
| **(seam)** | Domain is deep entry; **love of adventure** is extra worship built (nothing in pure assay requires joy) — apprentices describe thrill theology can't require |
| **(miscollapse)** | Older cult was pure vault/assay desk-god; delver joy is later drift |

**Lean:** **(seam)** is honest and good — same family as Chakamon warmth (nothing about transmission requires affection). Adventure-love is the seam that makes her vivid; assay remains the column.

#### vs Aretzon (still clear)

Aretzon: calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried foundation — **stays**.  
Tzuron: **moves into the dark on purpose** — opposite energy, same Diamonds suit. Twin problem dissolves.

#### Risks

1. **Taturiel bleed** — fix with "this mountain's deep," return-with-account, not first-print-anywhere.  
2. **Becomes pulp archaeologist only** — keep Systems: sample, map, grade, false seam parable.  
3. **Monsters/traps drift** — user veto: **no**; wonder of the deep, not bestiary.  
4. **Gender/Lara coding** — fine with she; competence and joy, not pinup checklist.

# Pituyel (P-T-H) — Seduction (Hearts 4)

**Sketch status:** **Direction locked** (user). Seduction not Sex co-label; rank-4 inducement axis; languid/sultry (different register from Midvaron).
**Fit tag:** *(clean)* — keep.
**Root:** P-T-H (*patah*, entice/seduce). Gender she/her.
**Roster:** retitle toward **Seduction** only if still Sex/Seduction; cold gloss *Chosen Weakness* still works.

---

## Locked decisions

### Label: **Seduction** (not Sex/Seduction)

| Why |
|-----|
| Leaves **Midvaron** free to be sexy (desert/story sultry) without splitting "the sex god" |
| Domain is **want as door / inducement**, not the sexual act as portfolio |
| Sex remains a **common case** of seduction, not the definition |

### Rank 4 axis: **Inducements** (encouraging behavior change)

Row: Necessity (Spades) / **Seduction** (Hearts) / Bribery (Diamonds) / Alcohol (Clubs).

| Suit | Domain | Inducement mode |
|------|--------|-----------------|
| **Spades** | Necessity | Hard constraint — choose; no soft pull |
| **Hearts** | **Seduction** | Pull through **the target's own want** — door from within |
| **Diamonds** | Bribery | Pull through **purchased exception** / unentered account (Heteron) |
| **Clubs** | Alcohol | Pull through **excess/disinhibition** (Yayinon) — lose the stop |

**Shared rank job:** getting someone to modify behavior / cross a line.  
**Pituyel's slice:** the line is crossed because **they wanted to**.

### Personality (locked)

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Languid** | Unrushed; never forces the tempo; forced door = failed |
| **Sultry** | Desire-heat, intimate register |
| **vs Midvaron sultry** | **Different register** — Midvaron: adventurous desert/story mischief heat; Pituyel: slow, languid, want-door heat — not caravan-fire Scheherazade |
| **Sincere-in-use** | May mean the warmth while still using (unsettling; keep) |
| **Precise** | Only the target's own want counts |

**One-line:** *Languid, sultry, never in a hurry — she waits for the want to open the door, and she may mean every smile as she walks through.*

### Cold gloss (locked)

> **Seduction — inducement through the target's own desire.** Door opened from within; no standing over force or fear. Inside the wanting, not the finished relation. Rank 4 Hearts: social pull that changes behavior because they chose the open door. Not the sex domain as such; not Midvaron's desert-story sexiness.

### Defense / flaw

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** | No force, pressure, or rushed door. Named ask / forced entry = not her craft. |
| **Flaw** | Ruin by true want; sincerity while using; will not save you from what you opened. Languid patience can feel like care and still be leverage. |

### Mechanic keep

- Inside wanting, not relation  
- Willing Door parable  
- Gift with request unspoken  
- Courtesans + desire-spies (and anyone who moves people by want, not only sex workers)

### Fences

| God | vs Pituyel |
|-----|------------|
| **Midvaron** | Desert/story sultry, adventure, tale-we; not want-door inducement |
| **Ahavel** | Love-forms / bond |
| **Chalufon** | Trade/deal inducement (favor, bargain) vs desire inducement |
| **Heteron** | Bought silent exception vs willing want |
| **Sha'alon** | Hard forced choice |
| **Yayinon** | Excess as Clubs inducement |
| **Pituyel** | **Want-from-within inducement** |

### Design (locked candidate)

Hearts 4 **Seduction** (not Sex label): Rank 4 **inducement** axis — behavior change via the target's own want; door from within. Languid/sultry different register from Midvaron. Spades force fence hard. Sex common case, not portfolio title. Affection-while-using kept. *(clean)*.

# Takshiton (K-Š-Ṭ) — Jewels (Hearts 3)

**Sketch status:** Review opened.
**Fit tag:** *(clean)*
**Rank/suit job:** Rank 3 portable tokens — Weapons / **Jewels** / Coins / Seeds. Hearts = gift as social proof of cost between persons.
**Root:** K-Š-Ṭ (*takhshit*, jewelry/ornament).
**Roster:** *The Given Weight — the gift whose value was never in the stone, but in what it cost the giver to part with it, worn afterward as proof the cost was paid.*
**Gender:** he/him (sketches).

---

## Process checks

1. **Hearts = Social.** Pass — distance between giver and receiver; sacrifice as bond-signal, not gem market.
2. **Love-bleed.** Medium — worshippers lead with courting/betrothal. Mechanic is broader: any gift where **cost to giver** is the social fact (kin gift, apology gift, vassal token). Recommend keep courtship as common case, not sole shop.
3. **Width.** "Jewels" inflates to all wealth display, fashion, hoards. Cold gloss: **given ornament as proof of paid cost** — the jewel is the medium, the Social object is the gift-path. Hoarded unworn gems thin his standing (worship: hide = reject giver).
4. **Rank 3 axis (portable value tokens):**  
   - Spades Weapons — edge kept / tool of force  
   - Hearts Jewels — gift of cost between people  
   - Diamonds Coins — balance tool (Ashiron)  
   - Clubs Seeds — living abundance without theory (Zerael)  
5. **vs Chalufon:** favor/deal opens obligation; Takshiton tracks **what you gave up for them** (often love/alliance display), not the unclosed trade edge. Overlap on gifts — different question (owe vs cost-shown).  
6. **vs Ashiron:** market value of stone vs sacrifice of giver. Design already owns this.  
7. **vs Ahavel:** love-forms; jewel may ride love, not required (kin, oath-brother gift).  
8. **Honest Gift × Sha'ashon** — shared parable if still present.

---

## What works (protect)

| Piece | Why |
|-------|-----|
| **Sacrifice only term; stone value not weighed** | Clean Social vs Ashiron |
| **Distance between two people the gift passed** | Graph of giving |
| **Poor Man's Stone** | Plain stone, year of work, still in family — icon |
| **Wear visibly; hide = reject giver** | Playable worship |
| **Personality: plain sentimental, quiet judgment of cheap gifts** | Distinct; "least complicated Heart" — may be true or slightly undersell |
| **Design fence Ashiron** | Opposite variable on similar objects |

**Default lean:** **stands well** — simple seat like Chalufon "pretty simple." Optional: widen worshippers past courtship; explicit defense/flaw; rank-3 token axis in Design.

---

## Soft spots

1. **Courtship-primary surface** — broaden examples (mourning gift, peace gift with real cost, master-to-apprentice token) without losing betrothal icon.  
2. **"Least complicated"** — fine if personality is plain; ensure he still has a knife (cheap gifts offend; hidden gifts reject).  
3. **Jewels without gift** — display wealth alone = thin or Ashiron/Shevachiel adjacent; he needs the **given** path.  
4. **Flaw:** performative costly gifts; sacrifice theater; blessing long matches that are still bad (Poor Man's Stone blesses cost, not always wisdom of match).  
5. **Shared parable Honest Gift** (if in sketches) — gift received as trap under Discord.

---

## Cold gloss (candidate)

> **Jewels — the given weight of cost between people.** Takshiton holds gifts whose social truth is what the giver gave up, not what the market would pay. The piece should be worn as proof the cost was paid. He does not hold coin-balance (Ashiron), unclosed favor-deals (Chalufon), or love-forms as such (Ahavel).

### Defense and flaw

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** | Will not underwrite costless gifts as full standing; will not treat market value as his term. Hiding the gift rejects the giver — and him. |
| **Flaw** | Cost is not wisdom; expensive sacrifice can bind a bad match or buy moral credit. Plain sentimental judgment can miss coercion dressed as gift (vs Pituyel/Heteron). |

### Personality (locked lean)

Surface ≠ undercurrent — and that *is* the face:

| Layer | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Surface** | Almost **narcissistic admiration of glitter** — dazzled by jewels, shine, the look of the gift; can seem shallow, gem-drunk, in love with sparkle |
| **Undercurrent** | Constant **evaluation of cost to the giver** — the plain stone with a year of labor outranks the easy dowry; glitter without cost gets a quiet coldness under the smile |
| **Worship still** | Wear the piece; hide = reject the giver (and him) |
| **Poor Man's Stone** | Still the orthodoxy of the undercurrent; surface cult may tell it as "even plain stones count if pretty enough" until priests correct |

**Not:** pure plain-sentimental simpleton (old sketch) without the glitter-face.  
**Not:** only market jeweler (Ashiron).  
**Feature:** people mistake him for a wealth/display god; the knife is he was scoring sacrifice all along.

**One-line:** *He lights up at glitter like a narcissist at a mirror — and underneath he is always pricing what it cost you, not what it would fetch.*

**Flaw form:** the glitter-face can encourage costly theater; people ruin themselves for a gift that shines; he still scores the cost, not their wisdom.

---

## Fences

| God | They hold | He holds |
|-----|-----------|----------|
| **Ashiron** | Coin tool; balance; market figure | Sacrifice of giver, not price |
| **Chalufon** | Trade act; favor; deal | Gift as cost-proof between two, not obligation engine alone |
| **Ahavel** | Love-forms | Gift may serve love; cost is his term |
| **Pituyel** | Want-door inducement | Gift may open want; unspoken ask is her craft, cost-shown is his |
| **Shevachiel** | Glory/presence | Ornament as given cost, not hall-seizing presence |
| **Neshakon** | Weapon kept sharp | Jewel given and worn |
| **Zerael** | Seed abundance | Portable gift-token of cost |
| **Takshiton** | **Given weight / cost made visible** | |

---

## Verdict

| Path | |
|------|--|
| **A. Stands well + light polish** | Recommended — mechanic clean |
| **B. Widen past courtship in worshippers** | Small text pass |
| **C. Personality upgrade** | Only if "least complicated" feels too flat |

**Lean:** A (+ B when sketches update). Rank 3 token row is clear; no inducement/sex confusion with rank 4.



### Rank 10 principle (user — locked lean)

**Biomes are containers, not substrates.** Rank 10 is not "about the sand / the trees / the rocks / the mud" as stuff. Each suit reads **what that biome holds or does as mode weather**:

| Biome | Suit | Not the stuff | What it **contains / does** (mode) |
|-------|------|---------------|-------------------------------------|
| **Forests** | Spades | Not timber taxonomy | **Predatory cover** — formation erased; ground that hunts the disciplined line (Hard Mode restriction) |
| **Deserts** | Hearts | Not sand grains | **Mystique / sparse address** — stories on the wind, unanswered word, night-fire *we* (Social) |
| **Mountains** | Diamonds | **Not stacks of rock / load-pile architecture** | **Depths & resources** — caves, mines, seams, assay, what the mass holds (Systems entry of the deep) |
| **Swamps** | Clubs | Not mud chemistry | **Unemptiable return** — basin that comes back, growth that won't stay drained (Sandbox) |

So the Mountains reframe is not a special plea — it's **applying the same rule the row already uses**. Old Tzuron (span/gravity/foundation-stone) was treating the mountain as **building material / structural pile**, which is substrate-logic — and that's why she slid into Aretzon (Earth as foundation/base). Correct Diamonds read: mountain as **vault of the deep**, entry for contents.

**Aretzon fence under principle:** Earth Jack is element-as-foundation (base that doesn't move). Mountains 10 is biome-as-contents (what the high mass holds underneath). Different rank axes; no need to share "immovable rock woman" identity.

---
### User probe: Mountains = caves / mines / depths / resources (not stacks of rock)

**Idea:** Keep biome label **Mountains**. Change Systems subject from load-bearing *pile* (spans, gravity, first foundation stone) to what the mountain **holds and yields from within**: caves, mines, depths, resource bodies, assay, the deep account.

This is reportfolio of *function*, not of grid label — postcard tetrad intact; *tzur* still names the mountain; portfolio stops twinning Aretzon's surface foundation.

#### Why this works

| | **Aretzon (Earth)** | **Tzuron (Mountains, reframe)** |
|--|---------------------|----------------------------------|
| Layer | **Surface / base** — foundation claim, title, survey of ground you stand on or deed | **Depth / contents** — what the mass *contains*: ore, seam, void, grade, shaft account |
| Question | Is there a founding claim the system can rest on? | Is the **deep entry** true — grade, depth, support, reserve? |
| Failure | Broken title continuity; paper base with no standing | Wrong assay / wrong depth figure; mine that "worked" until the error found its level |
| Doesn't move | Foundation base of ownership | The mountain as **vault** — resource doesn't care about your surface story |
| Customers | Surveyors, land-holders, diaspora title | Miners, quarry-wards, assay masters, delvers, deep-guild engineers |
| Imagery | Boundary marker, deed, first stone of *title* | Adit, lamp, sample, seam map, timbered shaft |
| Parable shape | Perfect Deed (empty surface claim kept) | Wrong grade / thin timber / false reserve that held until it didn't |

**One-line split:** Aretzon = *who holds the ground.* Tzuron = *what the mountain holds.*

Mining **claim law** stays Aretzon (cadastral). **Geological/resource truth** of the hole stays Tzuron. Same ridge: her registry says you own it; Tzuron's column says whether the seam you entered was ever there.

#### Systems Mode (still Diamonds)

Not Clubs abundance (Zerael wants full harvest warmth). Not Spades "mountain kills the soft."  

**Cold object:** the **entered truth of the deep** — assay, reserve, depth, structure of the void — independent of hope, surface deed, or how long the gallery seemed fine.

| Keep from old Tzuron | Drop / rehome |
|----------------------|---------------|
| Late failure of wrong entry (Half-Right energy) | Bridge span as master icon |
| "Almost" not a column | First stone of *building* foundation (→ Aretzon title-stone or Harashon craft) |
| Unhurried severity optional | Mountain sovereign as pure gravity-on-architecture |
| Engineer customers (narrow to **mine/quarry/deep** structure) | General guild-architect of every freestanding span |

**Half-Right Span → portable:** e.g. **The False Seam** / **The Gallery That Held:** every measurement of the adit right except one grade or one timber figure; worked for eleven years; was wrong the day of the assay. Same systems beat, depth skin.

**Staked Span → retarget:** guild stakes youth to assay/mine-measure early; Tza'iron still calls theft of optionality. Shared parable survives with new trade.

#### vs neighbors

| God | Fence |
|-----|--------|
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title vs deep contents/resource figure |
| **Zerael** | Warm surface yield without theory vs entered deep reserve (cold systems) |
| **Rabakhon** | Biological replication process vs mineral/void extract account |
| **Ashiron** | Coin balance after sale vs resource still in the mountain / grade true |
| **Harashon** | Medium of the make (including timber/steel craft) vs whether the *hole's* numbers were true |
| **Chakamon** | Inherited manual copy vs this seam's assay (may use old book wrong) |
| **Ya'aron** | Cover erases formation on surface forest vs under-mountain systems |
| **Bitzon** | Ground that returns/grows vs mountain that is **extracted** (opposite vector) |
| **Taturiel** | First step on ridge vs first lamp in a mapped dark |

#### Personality (under reframe — options)

Depth weather differs from Aretzon's calm surface foundation:

| | Face | Play |
|--|------|------|
| **D1** | **Reserve / vault** | Holds much, reveals by measure; unhurried because the seam is older than the claim |
| **D2** | **Assay-severity** | Sample is sacred; "looks rich" is not a column; contempt for surface optimism |
| **D3** | **Deep-calm** | Quiet of caves; patient; not Aretzon's base-pride — **withholding** pride (the mountain keeps its own counsel until entered true) |

Avoid twinning Aretzon's exact "calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried" package — related still (both solid, both unhurried possible) but **identity** is vault/assay/depth, not foundation-that-doesn't-move.

#### Name

**Tzuron** (*tzur*) can **stay** — rock/mountain-fastness as the body that *contains* the deep, not as a stack of load-bearing stones. Design one-liner: mountains as resource-body / underworld of stone, not architecture.

#### Fit tag

Likely still *(clean)*: Mountains → mines/depths is a natural mortal read; load-span was the more forced pure-optimizer abstract. Optional *(seam)* if old span-cult minority remembers bridge-Tzuron — only if you want that history.

#### Lean

**Strong path:** Keep Mountains; reportfolio to **depths / mines / caves / resources**. Clears Aretzon without leaving the biome row. Rename optional (keep Tzuron). Rewrite worship, parable skin, Staked Span aim; drop foundation-stone collision entirely.

2. Tag stay *(clean)* or *(seam)* because surface looks like wrong god?

4. Drop "least complicated Heart" from sketches (personality is no longer plain-simple only)?


### Personality probe: spelunking wanderlust (Clubs creep) — Lara Croft without monsters

**User:** Allow Tzuron to creep a bit into Clubs — wanderlust, spelunking; loves the adventure; almost Lara Croft feel **without the monsters**.
