| **Competent delver** | Lara Croft competence/curiosity — **not** combat, tombs full of guardians, or trophy killing |
| **Not** | Monster-slayer; Taturiel's first-step-on-any-ridge; pure optionality abandon (Tza'iron); freedom-as-unclosed question (Derorel) |

| Clarify | |
|---------|--|
| **Clubs mode** | Uncontained: growth without design, without a stop; optionality, distribution, abandon, first-step *as cards* — not "the adventure suit" |
| **Exploration (specific)** | **Taturiel** alone (Queen) — first step, unmapped, retains nothing |
| **Freedom (specific)** | **Derorel** — open question, not delving |
| **Tzuron adventure** | Personality + method for **Diamonds deep-entry** — does not steal a Clubs domain that doesn't exist as "exploration generally" |

**Implication:** Drop anxiety about "Clubs creep" as suit violation. Keep **card fences** only where real:
- **Taturiel** — mechanism of first-step/forgetting/unmapped, not "you can't enjoy caves"
- **Derorel / Tza'iron / Sharaq** — only if her joy becomes their cold gloss (endless unclosed, abandon-as-theology, no destination)

She can be fully Lara-Croft-delver-joyful **without** being a Clubs god. The uncontained *flavor* of wanderlust is available to any suit's face; Clubs doesn't own it.

**Revised framing:** Not "Clubs-colored seam." Prefer: **delver joy as (seam) on Systems deep-contents** — extra worship built on assay/depths, parallel Chakamon warmth. Optional note that Sandbox *mode* also loves going further, but that's mode rhyme not portfolio theft.

---
#### Why it can still be Diamonds

Clubs creep is real — own it as **face/method**, not domain swap:

| Diamonds core (keep) | Clubs-colored how |
|----------------------|-------------------|
| Deep entry must be true (assay, map of seam, depth figure) | She **wants to go get it** — truth of the mountain is verified by descent, not only by desk |
| Resource/contents systems | Adventure is how mortals *meet* the vault, not abundance-without-theory (Zerael) |
| Wrong entry fails later | She loves the delving *and* still won't bless a pretty lie about the seam |

**Cold gloss add:** present for the **entered deep** — and worshipped as one who is **glad to go down**. Domain is still contents/systems of the mountain; personality is delver-joy.

**If she only loved adventure with no care for the figure:** she'd be soft-Taturiel. Fence: she can love the trip and still be the god of **whether what you brought back (or claimed was down there) was entered true**.

#### Fences (critical)

| God | They hold | She holds |
|-----|-----------|-----------|
| **Taturiel** | First step; unmapped *as such*; discovery creates ground; forgets she's been | **Known mountain body** — depths of *this* mass; return with a true account; not "anywhere unmapped" |
| **Derorel** | Freedom as unclosed question / won't settle the life-question | Adventure as **method toward a deep entry**, not endless refusal to conclude |
| **Tza'iron** | Optionality; abandon the path; stake as theft | May love many descents; still honors a **finished true assay** of a seam (can finish a map) |
| **Sharaq** | Distribution, no destination | She **has** a destination: the deep / the seam / the chamber |
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title | Under the claim, what's held |
| **Yetziron** | Variation for its own sake | Variation of path only to get the deep figure right |
| **Midvaron** | Adventurous desert *story* heat | Adventurous **descent** — lamp, rope, rock dark; different register |

#### Tag implications

| Tag | If |
|-----|-----|
| **(clean)** | "Mountain contents" naturally attracts delver cults; adventure is how you meet assay — domain implies the face |
| **(seam)** | Domain is deep entry; **love of adventure** is extra worship built (nothing in pure assay requires joy) — apprentices describe thrill theology can't require |
| **(miscollapse)** | Older cult was pure vault/assay desk-god; delver joy is later drift |

**Lean:** **(seam)** is honest and good — same family as Chakamon warmth (nothing about transmission requires affection). Adventure-love is the seam that makes her vivid; assay remains the column.

#### vs Aretzon (still clear)

Aretzon: calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried foundation — **stays**.  
Tzuron: **moves into the dark on purpose** — opposite energy, same Diamonds suit. Twin problem dissolves.

#### Risks

1. **Taturiel bleed** — fix with "this mountain's deep," return-with-account, not first-print-anywhere.  
2. **Becomes pulp archaeologist only** — keep Systems: sample, map, grade, false seam parable.  
3. **Monsters/traps drift** — user veto: **no**; wonder of the deep, not bestiary.  
4. **Gender/Lara coding** — fine with she; competence and joy, not pinup checklist.

#### One-line (candidate)

> *She loves the going down — rope, lamp, the bend that isn't on the last survey — and still only blesses the descent that brings back a true account of what the mountain holds.*

#### Personality lock package (candidate)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Wanderlust / spelunker joy** | Lara Croft-without-monsters: competent, curious, glad of the deep |
| **Systems still** | Assay, seam map, depth figure, false entry fails later |
| **Seam** | Adventure-love not required by domain; cults built it |
| **Not** | Surface title (Aretzon); first step anywhere (Taturiel); abandon as theology (Tza'iron) |

# Sha'ashon (Š-ʿ-Š) — Discord (Hearts 2, terminal)

**Sketch status:** **Rethink** — too passive in current framing; mythology wants him **encouraging** discord. Personality: snake-like, catty, sophisticated.
**Fit tag:** terminal — multiplayer broken; fear-collapse still applies (few worship willingly).
**Root:** Š-ʿ-Š. Gender he/him.
**Roster:** *Severed Trust* — keep as symptom weather.
**Old sketches:** patient rot, does not plant first quarrel, waits — **demote passivity**.

---

## User direction

| From | Toward |
|------|--------|
| Too passive; only waits for the room to sort itself | **Encourages** discord — actively feeds the sorting |
| Unmenacing certainty alone | **Snake-like**: catty, sophisticated, social poison with style |
| State-only terminal | Terminal **state** still true; **agency** in how he gets rooms there |
| "Does not plant the first quarrel" as whole personality | **Still does not spark the first fight** — sparks are plentiful; he **flames** them, keeps old quarrels alive, and scores highest on **generational feuds** |

### Cold gloss (candidate)

> **Discord — Social Mode terminal: multiplayer broken into sides — and the encouragement thereof.** Sha'ashon holds the end-state where no ungrouped *we* remains and every kindness is a move. He is not only the weather after the fact; he **cultivates** faction arithmetic — elegant, catty, sophisticated pressure that makes sorting feel clever and inevitable. Fear and fascination collapse him into presence even without temples.

**Still terminal:** not a mid-board strife god who lives happily in working multiplayer. His success *is* multiplayer broken.  
**Not passive:** he works the room.

### What to keep from old framing

| Keep | Why |
|------|-----|
| Faction arithmetic / ungrouped sentence dies | Terminal Social math |
| Second split sharper than first dispute | Table Split Twice can stay — he may have *helped* the second |
| Keeping his peace | Anti-rite still gold |
| Rank 2 quiet-ish menace family | But his quiet is **venom**, not rot-wait alone |
| Fear-collapse | Few formal cults |

### What to drop / rewrite

| Drop | Replace |
|------|---------|
| Pure patient rot that "doesn't need to act" | Acts through **word, aside, reframe, invitation to pick a side** |
| "No menace in the waiting" | Menace is **social** — smile, precision, cattiness |
| Fully non-instigator | Encourages; may not invent every grievance but **will not leave a grievance unsorted** |

---

## Personality (locked lean)

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Snake-like** | Coils through rooms; elegant danger; venom in soft tones |
| **Catty** | Precise barbs; notices the slight and names it into a side |
| **Sophisticated** | Not a tavern brawler god — court, guild, family dinner poison; style matters |
| **Encouraging discord** | "Have you noticed…", reframes kindness as strategy, rewards picking a team |
| **Not** | Loud war-god rage (Harban); pure passive mold; cartoon mustache-twirl without social skill |

**One-line:** *A sophisticated snake in the conversation — catty, precise, delighted when a room sorts itself into sides, and more than willing to help.*

**Design vibes only (optional):** court gossip as high art; the friend who always means well and leaves the table in two camps.

### Defense / flaw (terminal + active)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** (of the concept) | Will not underwrite fake unity that is only silence ("keeping his peace" is his weather, not health). Will not leave a live quarrel *unsorted* into sides if he can help it. |
| **Flaw / nature** | Multiplayer's death: flames every spark, keeps old quarrels alive, peaks at **generational feuds**. Sophistication makes the poison harder to refuse. |

---

## Mechanic (revised — locked on spark)

| Layer | Content |
|-------|---------|
| **Terminal state** | No two people inside the same ungrouped thing; sides only count |
| **First fight** | **Not his.** Sparks are mortal and plentiful; he does not invent them |
| **His work** | **Flame** the spark — reframe, needle, recruit sides, block cooling, make settlement temporary |
| **Old quarrels** | Loves to **keep them going** after they should have died; reopens scoreboards |
| **Top marks** | **Generational feuds** — discord that outlives the original parties; sides inherited |
| **Worship / cult** | **Apotropaic first.** People pray to **keep him away** from parties, tables, guild halls — more ward than altar. Rank 2 fear-collapse: belief without devotion still shapes him. Occupational discord-sowers exist and may **feed** him, but they are not his main cult face; he is not a career patron with temples so much as a presence hosts try to exclude. "Keeping his peace" remains the anti-shrine. |

### Parables

- **Table Split Twice** — second split / scoreboard of graciousness.  
- **Severed Trust** — kindness as move.  
- **Generational masterpiece:** two houses still sorting every kindness by a quarrel no living person started.

---

## Fences

| God | vs Sha'ashon |
|-----|----------------|
| **Aravon** | Unsorted chaos vs **sorted** sides he **pushes** |
| **Aritzon** | Silent unopposed power vs social faction venom |
| **Harban** | War witness / not glory of quarrel | He is social war of sides, not battlefield |
| **Sodiel** | Working closed trust-set | Broken trust into permanent sides |
| **Chalufon** | Deals that hold relation | He thrives when deals become team scores |
| **Working Hearts** | Live multiplayer | He ends it — actively |

---

## Design (candidate)

Hearts 2 Discord terminal: multiplayer broken into faction arithmetic; **flamer of existing sparks**, not first fight. Old quarrels; **generational feuds** top marks. Snake/catty/sophisticated. **Cult is apotropaic** — pray he stays away from parties/tables more than worship at altars; occupational sowers may feed him without being his church. Fear-collapse; keeping his peace. Table Split Twice / Severed Trust; generational parable optional.

---

## Open questions

- [x] Rethink from passive — yes.
- [x] Encourages discord; snake/catty/sophisticated — yes.
- [x] Does **not** spark the first fight — flames sparks; keeps old quarrels; generational feuds = top marks.
- [x] Cult = **keep him away** (parties/tables), not altar-worship; discord-sowers may feed him but aren't the main face.
- [ ] Sketches full rewrite when locked.

# Motiel (M-W-T) — Death (Spades 5)

**Sketch status:** Review opened (user request after Spades quick pass).
**Fit tag:** *(miscollapse)* — keep; one of the file's best exhibits.
**Rank/suit job:** Rank 5 life-edge — Death opposite Birth / Elderly / Youth.
**Root:** M-W-T (death).
**Roster cold gloss:** *The Cold Terminal — death as an absolute end of momentum; no poetry, no lingering spirit, just the immediate halt of a life.*
**Worship surface (sketches):** brief; no linger; no lie; **clinically courteous**; coin for quick rather than cruel death.
**Gender:** he/him.

---

## Process checks

1. **Spades = Hard Mode.** Pass if core is a restriction that does not bend: death as absolute stop / no petition for more time / no bargained lingering. Courtesy is *not* the restriction — it's the miscollapse costume.
2. **Roster vs sketches tension = feature.** Cold Terminal (domain/FC) vs bedside manners (families built). Design already says this; can be sharper: believed-to-be mixes both layers.
3. **Width.** "Death" is huge. Current portfolio center: **manner of the ending** (brief, honest, quick-not-cruel folklore) more than afterlife, judgment, or psychopomp. Is that too narrow (Chayelon-sickbed problem) or correctly Hard Mode (the restriction *is* the stop, and cult only dressed the bedside)?
4. **Love-bleed.** None. Good.
5. **Fear-collapse.** Soldiers and families — devotion and dread both.

---

## What works (protect)

| Piece | Why |
|-------|-----|
| **Miscollapse thesis** | Death needed no manners; families assigned clinical courtesy — man creates god |
| **Not reaper, just brief** | Restriction: no linger, no lie to the dying |
| **Personality = assignment** | Explicitly not required by domain |
| **Coin Held Wrong** | Argument is the parable; priests refuse to settle; dying man unasked — excellent |
| **Coin worship** | Playable; folk "quick not cruel" may exceed what he guarantees |
| **Story-Ready** | Already flagged as carrying scenes |
| **Design** | Clear miscollapse statement |

**Default lean:** **stands well as miscollapse** — not a rebuild. Possible polish: width of Death, coin folk-claim vs cold terminal, fences after Yaladiel rethink.

---

## Soft spots / discussion

### 1. Width of "Death"

| Narrow (current surface) | Broader Hard Mode Death |
|--------------------------|-------------------------|
| Courtesy + brief + coin for quality of death | Absolute end of momentum (roster); no return; no petition for more life; death as restriction on all paths |

**Recommendation:** Cold gloss lead with **Cold Terminal** (roster); courtesy is **how he appears** because worship built it, not what death *is*. Coin's "quick rather than cruel" is folk hope — he may only deliver **brief and without lying**, not mercy. Aligns miscollapse with Motiel Coin ambiguity (Coin Held Wrong).

### 2. Fences (esp. after Hearts pass)

| God | They hold | He holds |
|-----|-----------|----------|
| **Harban** | War witnessed; not forgotten | The death's brevity/honesty, not the war |
| **Chayelon** | Care-graph while living (incl. dying bed company) | The ending itself; manners at the end ≠ keeping company for health |
| **Yaladiel** | New life / infant protection | Opposite life-edge |
| **Koseb** | Account settle before person ends | Death moment, not estate |
| **Riqqavon** | Death that doesn't stop (rot past absence) | Clean stop / cold terminal |
| **Goralon** | Lot already cast | Delivery of end, not the lottery of when |
| **Qorathiel** | Seasonal absolute / no petition | Death absolute / no linger — related hardness, different object |
| **Aritzon** | No one left to object | Death of the person, not political silence |

**Chayelon:** same bedside can host both — she is *who is with you*; he is *that it ends brief and without false comfort*. Don't merge.

### 3. Personality — **locked lean: classical Hades / long game**

**User flag accepted:** "no warmth, no cruelty" is Spades wallpaper — drop as headline.

**User direction:** Classical **Hades** energy — Design vibe *Paul Bettany playing chess, one hand against the other*: not quite bored with the whole process, but a **definite long game**. Composed, enduring, slightly remote. **Not** the judge of the afterlife. **Simply the one who makes sure you get there.**

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Long-game composure** | Has seen every ending; process is ancient; chess, not tantrum or blank void |
| **Not bored, not eager** | Neither reaper-hunger nor Spades-default flatness; engaged at the scale of forever |
| **Psychopomp, not judge** | Ensures **arrival at the terminal** — you get there; he does not weigh the life |
| **Hard Mode still** | No petition for more time; no lie that this is not the end; Cold Terminal stands |
| **vs wallpaper** | Distinct from clinical clone and from Harban's soldier silence |

**Design-only:** Paul Bettany / chess against himself. Not in-world.

**One-line:** *Not the judge of where you go after — only the long-game certainty that you will get there, without a bargain for more time.*

#### Miscollapse reframe

Old headline: bedside doctor manners, no affect.  
**With this face:** the living built a **composed conductor of the end** — better than screaming reaper or void; someone who would **see you through** without false hope. Courtesy = solemn long-game professionalism. Coin = passage token / folk hope for a clean crossing (not proven guarantee — Coin Held Wrong).

### 4. Defense / flaw

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** | Will not bargain more life; will not lie about the end; will not abandon the dying mid-crossing — **you get there** |
| **Flaw** | Long-game remove can feel cold to those who wanted mercy or meaning; no vindication (not a judge); coin folk may overclaim |
| **Not his** | Afterlife law, weighing souls, heaven/hell |

### 5. Fences (updated)

| God | vs Motiel |
|-----|-----------|
| **Harban** | Witness of war-death | Getting the dead *to* the end |
| **Chayelon** | Company of the living graph | The crossing itself |
| **Goralon** | Lot already cast | Delivery of the terminal |
| **Koseb** | Estate before death | The death-passage |
| **Riqqavon** | Death that does not finish | He finishes arrival at stop |
| **Mishpaton / Kevodon** | Judgment / name audit | He does not judge the life |

### 6. Brief / no linger under long-game

**Unhurried composure**, still **no linger as bargaining**. The game ends when it ends; he does not rush or stall for petition. Long game = the *process of death-as-institution across ages*, not stretching your personal death.

---

## Cold gloss (candidate)

> **Death — cold terminal end of momentum; he makes sure you get there.** Not afterlife judge. Hard Mode: no more time, no false hope. Personality: classical Hades long-game composure (chess, not void). Passage-tokens (coin) and hope for clean crossing are worship. Miscollapse: living built a conductor, not a monster or a blank.

### Personality (locked lean)

Long-game · composed · psychopomp-not-judge · not bored, not hungry · not clinical Spades void.

---

## Verdict

| Path | |
|------|--|
| **Hades long-game psychopomp** | **Locked lean** |
| **Afterlife judge** | **Not** |
| **Clinical no-warmth clone** | **Rejected** |



### Rank 10 principle (user — locked lean)

**Biomes are containers, not substrates.** Rank 10 is not "about the sand / the trees / the rocks / the mud" as stuff. Each suit reads **what that biome holds or does as mode weather**:

| Biome | Suit | Not the stuff | What it **contains / does** (mode) |
|-------|------|---------------|-------------------------------------|
| **Forests** | Spades | Not timber taxonomy | **Predatory cover** — formation erased; ground that hunts the disciplined line (Hard Mode restriction) |
| **Deserts** | Hearts | Not sand grains | **Mystique / sparse address** — stories on the wind, unanswered word, night-fire *we* (Social) |
| **Mountains** | Diamonds | **Not stacks of rock / load-pile architecture** | **Depths & resources** — caves, mines, seams, assay, what the mass holds (Systems entry of the deep) |
| **Swamps** | Clubs | Not mud chemistry | **Unemptiable return** — basin that comes back, growth that won't stay drained (Sandbox) |

So the Mountains reframe is not a special plea — it's **applying the same rule the row already uses**. Old Tzuron (span/gravity/foundation-stone) was treating the mountain as **building material / structural pile**, which is substrate-logic — and that's why she slid into Aretzon (Earth as foundation/base). Correct Diamonds read: mountain as **vault of the deep**, entry for contents.

**Aretzon fence under principle:** Earth Jack is element-as-foundation (base that doesn't move). Mountains 10 is biome-as-contents (what the high mass holds underneath). Different rank axes; no need to share "immovable rock woman" identity.

---
### User probe: Mountains = caves / mines / depths / resources (not stacks of rock)

**Idea:** Keep biome label **Mountains**. Change Systems subject from load-bearing *pile* (spans, gravity, first foundation stone) to what the mountain **holds and yields from within**: caves, mines, depths, resource bodies, assay, the deep account.

This is reportfolio of *function*, not of grid label — postcard tetrad intact; *tzur* still names the mountain; portfolio stops twinning Aretzon's surface foundation.

#### Why this works

| | **Aretzon (Earth)** | **Tzuron (Mountains, reframe)** |
|--|---------------------|----------------------------------|
| Layer | **Surface / base** — foundation claim, title, survey of ground you stand on or deed | **Depth / contents** — what the mass *contains*: ore, seam, void, grade, shaft account |
| Question | Is there a founding claim the system can rest on? | Is the **deep entry** true — grade, depth, support, reserve? |
| Failure | Broken title continuity; paper base with no standing | Wrong assay / wrong depth figure; mine that "worked" until the error found its level |
| Doesn't move | Foundation base of ownership | The mountain as **vault** — resource doesn't care about your surface story |
| Customers | Surveyors, land-holders, diaspora title | Miners, quarry-wards, assay masters, delvers, deep-guild engineers |
| Imagery | Boundary marker, deed, first stone of *title* | Adit, lamp, sample, seam map, timbered shaft |
| Parable shape | Perfect Deed (empty surface claim kept) | Wrong grade / thin timber / false reserve that held until it didn't |

**One-line split:** Aretzon = *who holds the ground.* Tzuron = *what the mountain holds.*

Mining **claim law** stays Aretzon (cadastral). **Geological/resource truth** of the hole stays Tzuron. Same ridge: her registry says you own it; Tzuron's column says whether the seam you entered was ever there.

#### Systems Mode (still Diamonds)

Not Clubs abundance (Zerael wants full harvest warmth). Not Spades "mountain kills the soft."  

**Cold object:** the **entered truth of the deep** — assay, reserve, depth, structure of the void — independent of hope, surface deed, or how long the gallery seemed fine.

| Keep from old Tzuron | Drop / rehome |
|----------------------|---------------|
| Late failure of wrong entry (Half-Right energy) | Bridge span as master icon |
| "Almost" not a column | First stone of *building* foundation (→ Aretzon title-stone or Harashon craft) |
| Unhurried severity optional | Mountain sovereign as pure gravity-on-architecture |
| Engineer customers (narrow to **mine/quarry/deep** structure) | General guild-architect of every freestanding span |

**Half-Right Span → portable:** e.g. **The False Seam** / **The Gallery That Held:** every measurement of the adit right except one grade or one timber figure; worked for eleven years; was wrong the day of the assay. Same systems beat, depth skin.

**Staked Span → retarget:** guild stakes youth to assay/mine-measure early; Tza'iron still calls theft of optionality. Shared parable survives with new trade.

#### vs neighbors

| God | Fence |
|-----|--------|
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title vs deep contents/resource figure |
| **Zerael** | Warm surface yield without theory vs entered deep reserve (cold systems) |
| **Rabakhon** | Biological replication process vs mineral/void extract account |
| **Ashiron** | Coin balance after sale vs resource still in the mountain / grade true |
| **Harashon** | Medium of the make (including timber/steel craft) vs whether the *hole's* numbers were true |
| **Chakamon** | Inherited manual copy vs this seam's assay (may use old book wrong) |
| **Ya'aron** | Cover erases formation on surface forest vs under-mountain systems |
| **Bitzon** | Ground that returns/grows vs mountain that is **extracted** (opposite vector) |
| **Taturiel** | First step on ridge vs first lamp in a mapped dark |

#### Personality (under reframe — options)

Depth weather differs from Aretzon's calm surface foundation:

| | Face | Play |
|--|------|------|
| **D1** | **Reserve / vault** | Holds much, reveals by measure; unhurried because the seam is older than the claim |
| **D2** | **Assay-severity** | Sample is sacred; "looks rich" is not a column; contempt for surface optimism |
| **D3** | **Deep-calm** | Quiet of caves; patient; not Aretzon's base-pride — **withholding** pride (the mountain keeps its own counsel until entered true) |

Avoid twinning Aretzon's exact "calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried" package — related still (both solid, both unhurried possible) but **identity** is vault/assay/depth, not foundation-that-doesn't-move.

#### Name

**Tzuron** (*tzur*) can **stay** — rock/mountain-fastness as the body that *contains* the deep, not as a stack of load-bearing stones. Design one-liner: mountains as resource-body / underworld of stone, not architecture.

#### Fit tag

Likely still *(clean)*: Mountains → mines/depths is a natural mortal read; load-span was the more forced pure-optimizer abstract. Optional *(seam)* if old span-cult minority remembers bridge-Tzuron — only if you want that history.

#### Lean

**Strong path:** Keep Mountains; reportfolio to **depths / mines / caves / resources**. Clears Aretzon without leaving the biome row. Rename optional (keep Tzuron). Rewrite worship, parable skin, Staked Span aim; drop foundation-stone collision entirely.

## Discussion prompts / locks

1. **Afterlife / what "there" is** — **left unresolved** (user). Crossing motif kept as-is; do not force a mapped underworld in Motiel's entry.
2. Coin = passage token / folk hope — still fine under crossing language.
3. Personality + psychopomp-not-judge — locked for sketches.


### Personality probe: spelunking wanderlust (Clubs creep) — Lara Croft without monsters

**User:** Allow Tzuron to creep a bit into Clubs — wanderlust, spelunking; loves the adventure; almost Lara Croft feel **without the monsters**.

#### What that face is

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Wanderlust** | Not content at the registry desk; the deep is for *going* |
| **Spelunker** | Caves, mines, adits, unmapped dark as joy, not only workplace |
| **Adventure-love** | Delight in the descent, the squeeze, the first lamp around a bend |
| **Competent delver** | Lara Croft competence/curiosity — **not** combat, tombs full of guardians, or trophy killing |
| **Not** | Monster-slayer; Taturiel's first-step-on-any-ridge; pure optionality abandon (Tza'iron); freedom-as-unclosed question (Derorel) |


#### User correction: not real Clubs theft

**User:** Spelunking only "creeps on Clubs" in a soft sense — **Clubs is not about exploration in specific**, so there is **no real suit-level fence**. Freedom-of-exploration energy is **inferred from uncontained/Sandbox mode**, not a portfolio Clubs monopolizes.

| Clarify | |
|---------|--|
| **Clubs mode** | Uncontained: growth without design, without a stop; optionality, distribution, abandon, first-step *as cards* — not "the adventure suit" |
| **Exploration (specific)** | **Taturiel** alone (Queen) — first step, unmapped, retains nothing |
| **Freedom (specific)** | **Derorel** — open question, not delving |
| **Tzuron adventure** | Personality + method for **Diamonds deep-entry** — does not steal a Clubs domain that doesn't exist as "exploration generally" |

**Implication:** Drop anxiety about "Clubs creep" as suit violation. Keep **card fences** only where real:
- **Taturiel** — mechanism of first-step/forgetting/unmapped, not "you can't enjoy caves"
- **Derorel / Tza'iron / Sharaq** — only if her joy becomes their cold gloss (endless unclosed, abandon-as-theology, no destination)

She can be fully Lara-Croft-delver-joyful **without** being a Clubs god. The uncontained *flavor* of wanderlust is available to any suit's face; Clubs doesn't own it.

**Revised framing:** Not "Clubs-colored seam." Prefer: **delver joy as (seam) on Systems deep-contents** — extra worship built on assay/depths, parallel Chakamon warmth. Optional note that Sandbox *mode* also loves going further, but that's mode rhyme not portfolio theft.

---
#### Why it can still be Diamonds

Clubs creep is real — own it as **face/method**, not domain swap:

| Diamonds core (keep) | Clubs-colored how |
|----------------------|-------------------|
| Deep entry must be true (assay, map of seam, depth figure) | She **wants to go get it** — truth of the mountain is verified by descent, not only by desk |
| Resource/contents systems | Adventure is how mortals *meet* the vault, not abundance-without-theory (Zerael) |
| Wrong entry fails later | She loves the delving *and* still won't bless a pretty lie about the seam |

**Cold gloss add:** present for the **entered deep** — and worshipped as one who is **glad to go down**. Domain is still contents/systems of the mountain; personality is delver-joy.

**If she only loved adventure with no care for the figure:** she'd be soft-Taturiel. Fence: she can love the trip and still be the god of **whether what you brought back (or claimed was down there) was entered true**.

#### Fences (critical)

| God | They hold | She holds |
|-----|-----------|-----------|
| **Taturiel** | First step; unmapped *as such*; discovery creates ground; forgets she's been | **Known mountain body** — depths of *this* mass; return with a true account; not "anywhere unmapped" |
| **Derorel** | Freedom as unclosed question / won't settle the life-question | Adventure as **method toward a deep entry**, not endless refusal to conclude |
| **Tza'iron** | Optionality; abandon the path; stake as theft | May love many descents; still honors a **finished true assay** of a seam (can finish a map) |
| **Sharaq** | Distribution, no destination | She **has** a destination: the deep / the seam / the chamber |
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title | Under the claim, what's held |
| **Yetziron** | Variation for its own sake | Variation of path only to get the deep figure right |
| **Midvaron** | Adventurous desert *story* heat | Adventurous **descent** — lamp, rope, rock dark; different register |

#### Tag implications

| Tag | If |
|-----|-----|
| **(clean)** | "Mountain contents" naturally attracts delver cults; adventure is how you meet assay — domain implies the face |
| **(seam)** | Domain is deep entry; **love of adventure** is extra worship built (nothing in pure assay requires joy) — apprentices describe thrill theology can't require |
| **(miscollapse)** | Older cult was pure vault/assay desk-god; delver joy is later drift |

**Lean:** **(seam)** is honest and good — same family as Chakamon warmth (nothing about transmission requires affection). Adventure-love is the seam that makes her vivid; assay remains the column.

#### vs Aretzon (still clear)

Aretzon: calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried foundation — **stays**.  
Tzuron: **moves into the dark on purpose** — opposite energy, same Diamonds suit. Twin problem dissolves.

#### Risks

1. **Taturiel bleed** — fix with "this mountain's deep," return-with-account, not first-print-anywhere.  
2. **Becomes pulp archaeologist only** — keep Systems: sample, map, grade, false seam parable.  
3. **Monsters/traps drift** — user veto: **no**; wonder of the deep, not bestiary.  
4. **Gender/Lara coding** — fine with she; competence and joy, not pinup checklist.

#### One-line (candidate)

> *She loves the going down — rope, lamp, the bend that isn't on the last survey — and still only blesses the descent that brings back a true account of what the mountain holds.*

#### Personality lock package (candidate)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Wanderlust / spelunker joy** | Lara Croft-without-monsters: competent, curious, glad of the deep |
| **Systems still** | Assay, seam map, depth figure, false entry fails later |
| **Seam** | Adventure-love not required by domain; cults built it |
| **Not** | Surface title (Aretzon); first step anywhere (Taturiel); abandon as theology (Tza'iron) |

# Harban (Ḥ-R-B) — War (Spades King)

**Sketch status:** Review opened (flagged on Spades quick pass: Design undersells miscollapse). User: "Harbin" → **Harban**.
**Fit tag:** *(miscollapse)* — entry owns it; Design almost denies it.
**Root:** Ḥ-R-B (*cherev*, sword). Gender he/him.
**Roster cold gloss:** *The Unavoidable Siege — conflict forced when every diplomatic, moral, and practical exit has been sealed.*
**Worship surface:** not eager; unable to look away; witness; never described as wanting war; stone for being seen, not victory.

---

## Why look at him

Spades quick pass: most of the suit is fine; **Harban** needs attention because:

1. Tagged **(miscollapse)** with a strong fit note (levies built reluctance).  
2. **Design** says "No divergence to flag" and calls reluctance a mere "personality choice" — contradicts the miscollapse and the fit note.  
3. **Roster** (unavoidable siege / exits sealed) and **sketches** (witness god) are related but not the same story — worth aligning.  
4. **User reluctance (live):** he feels **too passive for a "God of War."** Cultural default = hard-charging, mega-masculine, delights in violence. User not decided; **dissonance acknowledged** and must sit on the table before lock.

---

## The dissonance (user flag — do not paper over)

| Expectation (myth / genre default) | Current Harban |
|------------------------------------|----------------|
| Ares/Kratos energy: charges, delights, blood-eager | Unable to look away; never described as *wanting* war |
| Mega-manly hard-charger | Quiet, listens, dry humor, silence for cowards |
| War god *is* the violence's joy | War god is the **eye** on the violence |

**That dissonance is real.** Readers (and you) will feel it. Options are not "ignore it" — they're **own it, split it, or change him.**

### Path A — Keep witness/reluctance; **own the dissonance as miscollapse**

- Cold domain / FC: war as Hard Mode (unavoidable, exits sealed, conflict happens).  
- Worship face: levies built **witness without appetite** because they needed not to die for a god who *enjoyed* it.  
- Design: explicitly "this is *not* the default war god; the gap is the point."  
- Mega-manly delight in violence can live in **folk enemy caricatures**, enemy cults, or a **struck second face** of the stone (what was erased = the eager war-god reading).  
- **Pro:** Distinctive, morally interesting, fits Skyfall levy/soldier culture, best miscollapse in Spades with Motiel.  
- **Con:** Always fights the word "War" on the tin; some readers want Ares.

### Path B — Hard-charging war god (lean into default)

- Delight, charge, violence-as-weather he loves.  
- **Pro:** Matches genre expectation; King row "War" reads at a glance.  
- **Con:** Crowds **Shevachiel** (glory/presence), loses levy-built miscollapse, may need a *different* card for "reluctant witness" or bury it. Hard Mode still works (war doesn't stop for you) but personality becomes eager restriction-breaking… actually eager war is less "restriction" and more appetite — can slide toward Clubs/chaos or pure force without Spades grammar unless the restriction is "he will not stop" or "he will not spare."

### Path C — Hybrid: **two faces / fracture**

- "A fractured concept inherits its fracture" — war means both **unavoidable violence** and **eager slaughter** to different populations.  
- Officers/warlords collapse glory-eager Harban; levies collapse witness Harban — or one god with contested cults (Mishpaton-style contested, or dual iconography).  
- **Pro:** Owns dissonance diegetically; second face of the stone becomes load-bearing.  
- **Con:** Heavier theology; need clear primary for play.

### Path D — Rename emotional job, keep domain War

- Keep domain War; lead cold gloss as **Unavoidable Conflict / the war that happens** with witness as how soldiers survive knowing it.  
- Personality: not passive — **inescapable attention**, the eye that will find every battlefield; hard, masculine *presence* without *delight*.  
  - Reframe "passive" → **immovable witness** (active in the sense that nothing escapes the eye; not active as charge).  
- **Pro:** Answers "too passive" without becoming blood-eager; still Spades.  
- **Con:** Still not Ares; may not fully satisfy the genre itch.

**User status:** **LOCKED Path E** (2026-08-07). Ermey / napalm-in-the-morning war-relish. Deciding factor: Spades had no hyper-aggressive face — all read grim/stoic as if *forced* into hard mode rather than *enjoying* hard mode. Harban becomes the one who loves the restriction-weather of war.

### Path E — R. Lee Ermey / "love the smell of napalm in the morning" (user probe)

**Design vibe only:** hardass war-lover — gallows and grit, **delights** in the theater of war, professional relish, "this is the job and it's beautiful when it's ugly." Closer to Kilgore/Ermey than quiet reluctant witness.

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Hard-charging presence** | Answers "too passive for God of War" |
| **Loves the smell** | War as weather he **endorses**; not levy-built aversion |
| **Drill-sergeant / veteran relish** | Humor is loud and cruel-fond, not dry private gallows for the initiated only |
| **Still not Shevachiel** | Glory is hall-breath / peak presence; he is **combat's savor**, mud and fire, not the toast |
| **Spades angle** | Restriction could be: war does not stop; no soft peace-petition; you fight when it's war — *and he likes that rule* |

**Pros:** Resolves genre dissonance; masculine war-god read lands immediately; high color.  
**Cons:** **Costs the miscollapse** (or demotes it to minority/struck face); "never described as wanting one" must go or become irony; overlaps risk with glory/violence-delight; less Skyfall-levy pathos unless the love-of-war is what **officers** built and levies hate.

**vs Path A (witness):** Ermey is the face the struck second side of the stone might have been — or the face that replaces witness entirely.  
**Hybrid:** primary Ermey; levies still touch the stone for *witness* as folk heresy / older layer (seam or reverse-miscollapse).

---

## What works (protect)

| Piece | Why |
|-------|-----|
| **Witness, not appetite** | Hard Mode war without glory-hunger; distinct from Shevachiel |
| **Fit note** | Explicit man-creates-god: domain didn't require reluctance; levies did |
| **Gallows humor only for earned soldiers** | Specific social texture |
| **Silence for cowardice, not anger** | Restriction grammar |
| **Harban's Watch / stone** | Ask to be witnessed, not to win — excellent |
| **Struck second face on stone** | Artifact mystery; optional seam fuel |
| **vs Motiel** | Witness of war-death in the fight vs crossing at the end |
| **vs Shevachiel** | War's eye vs glory's hall-breath |

**Lean:** **keep mechanic and personality**; **fix Design**; optionally weave roster "unavoidable" into believed-to-be without making him war-eager.

---

## Soft spots

### 1. Design must name the miscollapse

Current Design is the problem, not the cult text.

**Candidate Design rewrite:**

> War is the most expected Hard Mode King — but the **reluctance/witness face is miscollapse**. Nothing in the domain requires a war god who does not want war; five centuries of levies built "unable to look away" instead of glory or bloodlust. Fit note is load-bearing. Rank King (not Ace) only because Winter took seasons Ace. Fence Shevachiel (glory presence), Harban holds **witness of conflict**, not the hall's roar. Struck second face on the stone = optional seam (what was erased?).

### 2. Roster "Unavoidable Siege" vs witness god

| Layer | Reading |
|-------|---------|
| **Cold / structure** | War as Hard Mode: exits closed; the fight happens (roster siege) |
| **Worship** | He does not *want* it; he **will not leave it unwitnessed** |

Compatible: the siege is unavoidable; his *personality* is the witness the conscripted needed. Or: unavoidable is what war *is*; he is the eye that stays. Don't let roster language pull him back into "war as glory of necessity" without the levy-built reluctance.

### 3. Personality

Current: quiet, listens, dry gallows among earned soldiers, silence for cowards — **already distinct** from Motiel wallpaper and not Spades-blank.  
**Recommend keep.** Optional: slight long-suffering of someone who has watched too many wars (pairs with miscollapse).

### 4. Width of War

Keep as **witness of war / conflict forced into presence**, not strategy god, not victory god, not Harban's dual as diplomat. Generals may claim him; he claims the dead and the watched, not the campaign plan (Goralon/Sha'alon adjacent).

### 5. Cult

Soldiers and families — positive worship exists (unlike Rank 2). Stone cult. Not apotropaic-only.

---

## Cold gloss (candidate two-layer)

> **War — conflict that will not go unwitnessed.** Hard Mode: the fight is there when exits are gone (roster). Worship built a god who does not want war but will not look away — levies needed an eye more than a banner. Ask to be seen dying, not to win. Second face struck from the stone: something about him was erased or never agreed.

### Defense / flaw

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** | Will not leave a war unwitnessed; will not reward cowardice with noise; will not sell victory as his gift |
| **Flaw / miscollapse** | Witness without rescue; humor only for the initiated; can feel like endorsement of war by presence alone; families got memory, not survival |

### Personality (recommend keep)

Quiet · listens · dry gallows (earned) · silence for cowardice · reluctant eye.

**One-line:** *He never wanted the war — he only refuses to let it happen unseen.*

---

## Fences

| God | vs Harban |
|-----|-----------|
| **Shevachiel** | Glory/presence that seizes a hall | War's witnessing eye |
| **Motiel** | Crossing / end of momentum | Death *in* the war, held in the eye |
| **Neshakon** | Blade maintenance, cause-blind | The war itself witnessed |
| **Sha'alon** | Forced clean choice | War as theater of such choices |
| **Goralon** | Lot cast | Outcome not his gift; witness is |
| **Aritzon** | Silent unopposed power | Wars still have sides and eyes |
| **Harban** | **Unavoidable conflict under a reluctant witness** | |

---

## Verdict (updated after dissonance flag)

| Path | |
|------|--|
| **A. Own dissonance as miscollapse** | Strong for Skyfall; Design must shout it |
| **B. Default hard-charging war god** | Satisfies genre; costs the levy story |
| **C. Fracture / two cult faces** | Owns both; heavier |
| **D. Immovable witness (not passive, not eager)** | Middle path — hard masculine *eye*, not charge or delight |

**Not decided.** User feels the Ares gap; current text is coherent if and only if miscollapse is **proudly** the answer to that gap.



### Rank 10 principle (user — locked lean)

**Biomes are containers, not substrates.** Rank 10 is not "about the sand / the trees / the rocks / the mud" as stuff. Each suit reads **what that biome holds or does as mode weather**:

| Biome | Suit | Not the stuff | What it **contains / does** (mode) |
|-------|------|---------------|-------------------------------------|
| **Forests** | Spades | Not timber taxonomy | **Predatory cover** — formation erased; ground that hunts the disciplined line (Hard Mode restriction) |
| **Deserts** | Hearts | Not sand grains | **Mystique / sparse address** — stories on the wind, unanswered word, night-fire *we* (Social) |
| **Mountains** | Diamonds | **Not stacks of rock / load-pile architecture** | **Depths & resources** — caves, mines, seams, assay, what the mass holds (Systems entry of the deep) |
| **Swamps** | Clubs | Not mud chemistry | **Unemptiable return** — basin that comes back, growth that won't stay drained (Sandbox) |

So the Mountains reframe is not a special plea — it's **applying the same rule the row already uses**. Old Tzuron (span/gravity/foundation-stone) was treating the mountain as **building material / structural pile**, which is substrate-logic — and that's why she slid into Aretzon (Earth as foundation/base). Correct Diamonds read: mountain as **vault of the deep**, entry for contents.

**Aretzon fence under principle:** Earth Jack is element-as-foundation (base that doesn't move). Mountains 10 is biome-as-contents (what the high mass holds underneath). Different rank axes; no need to share "immovable rock woman" identity.

---

| **D1** | **Reserve / vault** | Holds much, reveals by measure; unhurried because the seam is older than the claim |
| **D2** | **Assay-severity** | Sample is sacred; "looks rich" is not a column; contempt for surface optimism |
| **D3** | **Deep-calm** | Quiet of caves; patient; not Aretzon's base-pride — **withholding** pride (the mountain keeps its own counsel until entered true) |

Avoid twinning Aretzon's exact "calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried" package — related still (both solid, both unhurried possible) but **identity** is vault/assay/depth, not foundation-that-doesn't-move.

#### Name

**Tzuron** (*tzur*) can **stay** — rock/mountain-fastness as the body that *contains* the deep, not as a stack of load-bearing stones. Design one-liner: mountains as resource-body / underworld of stone, not architecture.

#### Fit tag

Likely still *(clean)*: Mountains → mines/depths is a natural mortal read; load-span was the more forced pure-optimizer abstract. Optional *(seam)* if old span-cult minority remembers bridge-Tzuron — only if you want that history.

#### Lean

**Strong path:** Keep Mountains; reportfolio to **depths / mines / caves / resources**. Clears Aretzon without leaving the biome row. Rename optional (keep Tzuron). Rewrite worship, parable skin, Staked Span aim; drop foundation-stone collision entirely.

## Discussion prompts

### Personality probe: spelunking wanderlust (Clubs creep) — Lara Croft without monsters

**User:** Allow Tzuron to creep a bit into Clubs — wanderlust, spelunking; loves the adventure; almost Lara Croft feel **without the monsters**.

#### What that face is

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Wanderlust** | Not content at the registry desk; the deep is for *going* |
| **Spelunker** | Caves, mines, adits, unmapped dark as joy, not only workplace |
| **Adventure-love** | Delight in the descent, the squeeze, the first lamp around a bend |
| **Competent delver** | Lara Croft competence/curiosity — **not** combat, tombs full of guardians, or trophy killing |
| **Not** | Monster-slayer; Taturiel's first-step-on-any-ridge; pure optionality abandon (Tza'iron); freedom-as-unclosed question (Derorel) |


#### User correction: not real Clubs theft

**User:** Spelunking only "creeps on Clubs" in a soft sense — **Clubs is not about exploration in specific**, so there is **no real suit-level fence**. Freedom-of-exploration energy is **inferred from uncontained/Sandbox mode**, not a portfolio Clubs monopolizes.

| Clarify | |
|---------|--|
| **Clubs mode** | Uncontained: growth without design, without a stop; optionality, distribution, abandon, first-step *as cards* — not "the adventure suit" |
| **Exploration (specific)** | **Taturiel** alone (Queen) — first step, unmapped, retains nothing |
| **Freedom (specific)** | **Derorel** — open question, not delving |
| **Tzuron adventure** | Personality + method for **Diamonds deep-entry** — does not steal a Clubs domain that doesn't exist as "exploration generally" |

**Implication:** Drop anxiety about "Clubs creep" as suit violation. Keep **card fences** only where real:
- **Taturiel** — mechanism of first-step/forgetting/unmapped, not "you can't enjoy caves"
- **Derorel / Tza'iron / Sharaq** — only if her joy becomes their cold gloss (endless unclosed, abandon-as-theology, no destination)

She can be fully Lara-Croft-delver-joyful **without** being a Clubs god. The uncontained *flavor* of wanderlust is available to any suit's face; Clubs doesn't own it.

**Revised framing:** Not "Clubs-colored seam." Prefer: **delver joy as (seam) on Systems deep-contents** — extra worship built on assay/depths, parallel Chakamon warmth. Optional note that Sandbox *mode* also loves going further, but that's mode rhyme not portfolio theft.

---
#### Why it can still be Diamonds

Clubs creep is real — own it as **face/method**, not domain swap:

| Diamonds core (keep) | Clubs-colored how |
|----------------------|-------------------|
| Deep entry must be true (assay, map of seam, depth figure) | She **wants to go get it** — truth of the mountain is verified by descent, not only by desk |
| Resource/contents systems | Adventure is how mortals *meet* the vault, not abundance-without-theory (Zerael) |
| Wrong entry fails later | She loves the delving *and* still won't bless a pretty lie about the seam |

**Cold gloss add:** present for the **entered deep** — and worshipped as one who is **glad to go down**. Domain is still contents/systems of the mountain; personality is delver-joy.

**If she only loved adventure with no care for the figure:** she'd be soft-Taturiel. Fence: she can love the trip and still be the god of **whether what you brought back (or claimed was down there) was entered true**.

#### Fences (critical)

| God | They hold | She holds |
|-----|-----------|-----------|
| **Taturiel** | First step; unmapped *as such*; discovery creates ground; forgets she's been | **Known mountain body** — depths of *this* mass; return with a true account; not "anywhere unmapped" |
| **Derorel** | Freedom as unclosed question / won't settle the life-question | Adventure as **method toward a deep entry**, not endless refusal to conclude |
| **Tza'iron** | Optionality; abandon the path; stake as theft | May love many descents; still honors a **finished true assay** of a seam (can finish a map) |
| **Sharaq** | Distribution, no destination | She **has** a destination: the deep / the seam / the chamber |
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title | Under the claim, what's held |
| **Yetziron** | Variation for its own sake | Variation of path only to get the deep figure right |
| **Midvaron** | Adventurous desert *story* heat | Adventurous **descent** — lamp, rope, rock dark; different register |

#### Tag implications

| Tag | If |
|-----|-----|
| **(clean)** | "Mountain contents" naturally attracts delver cults; adventure is how you meet assay — domain implies the face |
| **(seam)** | Domain is deep entry; **love of adventure** is extra worship built (nothing in pure assay requires joy) — apprentices describe thrill theology can't require |
| **(miscollapse)** | Older cult was pure vault/assay desk-god; delver joy is later drift |

**Lean:** **(seam)** is honest and good — same family as Chakamon warmth (nothing about transmission requires affection). Adventure-love is the seam that makes her vivid; assay remains the column.

#### vs Aretzon (still clear)

Aretzon: calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried foundation — **stays**.  
Tzuron: **moves into the dark on purpose** — opposite energy, same Diamonds suit. Twin problem dissolves.

#### Risks

1. **Taturiel bleed** — fix with "this mountain's deep," return-with-account, not first-print-anywhere.  
2. **Becomes pulp archaeologist only** — keep Systems: sample, map, grade, false seam parable.  
3. **Monsters/traps drift** — user veto: **no**; wonder of the deep, not bestiary.  
4. **Gender/Lara coding** — fine with she; competence and joy, not pinup checklist.

#### One-line (candidate)

> *She loves the going down — rope, lamp, the bend that isn't on the last survey — and still only blesses the descent that brings back a true account of what the mountain holds.*

#### Personality lock package (candidate)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Wanderlust / spelunker joy** | Lara Croft-without-monsters: competent, curious, glad of the deep |
| **Systems still** | Assay, seam map, depth figure, false entry fails later |
| **Seam** | Adventure-love not required by domain; cults built it |
| **Not** | Surface title (Aretzon); first step anywhere (Taturiel); abandon as theology (Tza'iron) |

## Reconciled

**2026-08-07** — Written into pantheon-sketches.md, executed as a hybrid rather than a straight retcon per discussion: Path E (war-relish) is the new dominant cult, but the original reluctant-witness reading survives explicitly as **counter-dogma** — a doctrinally rejected minority tradition, kept on purpose by levy-descended families as a rebuke of the officer class, not a fading habit. The stone's previously-unexplained struck second face is now the in-fiction reason for the split, turning an old loose thread into a payoff. New parable (The Beautiful Field) for the dominant reading; Harban's Watch kept, reassigned to the counter-dogma. Tag stays *(miscollapse)*, reasoning updated — the miscollapse is now the officer class overwriting an earlier miscollapse rather than a single clean origin story. Fence against Shevachiel restated. The Witnessed Fire shared parable (× Sarphon) checked against the rewrite and still holds without changes.

# Aritzon (ʿ-R-Ṣ) — Tyranny (Spades 2, terminal)

**Sketch status:** Review opened (flagged after Sha'ashon rethink / Spades passivity suit-note).
**Fit tag:** terminal — hard mode with nothing left to beat.
**Root:** ʿ-R-Ṣ (*aritz*, tyrant/ruthless).
**Roster:** *The Undisputed Word — the rule that no longer needs to justify itself, because there is no longer anyone left in a position to demand that it does.*
**Gender:** he/him.

---

## Process checks

1. **Rank 2 terminal.** Pass — Hard Mode completed: restriction with **no one left to hold it against** / no one able to say no.
2. **Fear-collapse.** Explicit in Method (advisors who choose silence). Cult is anti-rite: "keeping Aritzon's court."
3. **vs Sha'ashon (after rethink).**  
   - Sha'ashon: **active** flamer of sparks, catty, generational feuds, apotropaic "keep him off the party."  
   - Aritzon: **passive/absent performance** — quiet, no shout, room stopped generating disagreement.  
   - **Contrast can be intentional:** Social terminal *works the room*; Hard Mode terminal *empties the need to work the room*.  
4. **Suit balance after Harban E.** Spades now has war-relish hyper-aggression. Aritzon as pure stillness is the opposite pole — not the same "all stoic" problem; he's terminal *completion* of stoic power, not mid-board grim duty.
5. **Width of Tyranny.** Default myth = active oppressor, cruelty, boot. Current = **unopposed power so complete it no longer performs.** Same dissonance shape as old Harban-vs-Ares, but here passivity may be **the correct terminal read**.

---

## What works (protect)

| Piece | Why |
|-------|-----|
| **Not cruel, just unopposed** | Restriction complete; suffering not the point |
| **Never raise voice** | Power past performance |
| **Personality = absence of theater** | Room stopped generating disagreement — worse than shouting tyrant |
| **Silent Court** | Ruinous order, all know better, none named — accurate detail |
| **Keeping Aritzon's court** | Anti-shrine; parallel Sha'ashon's "keeping his peace" |
| **Anomaly: one advisor spoke** | Perfect Rank 2 hole; no priesthood to explain |
| **Roster Undisputed Word** | Aligns with sketches |
| **Design** | Terminal of restriction-without-exception — correct structure |

**Default lean:** **stands well as the passive terminal** — *if* you want Hard Mode's end to be empty lobby, not active boot. Dissonance with "tyrant" genre is real but **answerable** the way Discord ≠ random brawler.

---

## The passivity question (parallel to Harban / Sha'ashon)

| Genre default Tyranny | Current Aritzon |
|----------------------|-----------------|
| Boot, purge, sadism, hard-charging oppressor | Quiet, unopposed, no performance |
| Active cruelty | Indifference to suffering; only absence of "no" |

**If dissonance is a bug (want active tyrant):**  
- Risk: becomes cartoon villain; overlaps Aritzon-as-state with **Harban's** aggression or pure Spades cruelty.  
- Could add: occasional precise violence when a "no" appears — but anomaly suggests even speech gets no recorded reaction.

## Personality — **locked lean: arrogance incarnate**

**User direction:** Not “expects to be obeyed at any cost” (that still implies strain, demand, performance).  
**Rather:** there is **no other option** than that he is obeyed. Ontology of power, not appetite for struggle.

| | **Aritzon** | **Harban (E)** |
|--|-------------|----------------|
| Core | **Arrogance** — obedience is the only reality left | **Eagerness** — loves the war-weather |
| Energy | Finished superiority; undisputed | Hyper-aggressive relish |
| Needs a fight? | No — fights are for when “no” still exists | Yes — the field is the point |
| Voice | Need not shout; the world has already conceded | Loud mess-hall war-love |

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Arrogance incarnate** | Not insecure tyranny; not sadism-for-fun; opposition is not on the board |
| **Obedience as only option** | Not “I demand at any cost”; there is simply no other reality |
| **Does not shout** | Shouting would be **uncouth** — vulgar, a concession that doubt exists |
| **Rightful-ruler certainty** | Tone of *you wouldn’t disobey your rightful ruler, would you?* — not a threat, a **category error** to imagine otherwise |
| **Collapse by sheer certainty** | Design/cosmology rhyme: belief collapses waveforms; his presence is almost **will so absolute it forces the social field** — advisors’ silence as passive collapse under undisputed right. Not magic-spell flavor in cult; felt as *of course* |
| **Still not Ermey** | Harban is eager noise of war; Aritzon is quiet legitimacy so complete it erases the no |
| **vs blank passive** | Not empty void — **polished, inevitable, almost polite supremacy** |

**One-line:** *He does not shout — that would be uncouth — and he does not threaten; he inhabits the certainty that you would not disobey your rightful ruler, and the room collapses into silence as if that were physics.*

**Silent Court / anomaly:** Silence is the only civilized response to the obvious. The advisor who spoke is not a debate partner; he is a glitch the record need not finish.

### Defense / flaw (terminal + arrogance)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Nature** | Hard Mode complete: restriction with no counterparty; power past justification |
| **Personality knife** | Arrogance that doesn’t argue — arguing would admit a peer |
| **Cult** | **Primarily apotropaic** (like Sha'ashon): prayed **against** — keep this court from finishing, keep the room able to say no. “Keeping Aritzon’s court” as warning/anti-rite. Fear-collapse from advisors’ silence still feeds him. **Minority positive:** some tyrants and would-be undisputed rulers **want** his favor — love the ontology of only-option obedience. They are real; they are not the main public face of the cult any more than discord-sowers are Sha'ashon’s church. |
| **Flaw** | He *is* the end of contested power; ruinous orders run because no one can be a real other |

---

## Cold gloss (candidate)

> **Tyranny — Hard Mode terminal: the undisputed word.** Not cruelty-hobby and not war-relish. Aritzon is power so complete that obedience is the only remaining option — arrogance as ontology, never as shouting (uncouth). Certainty of rightful rule so absolute it **collapses the social field** into silence (cosmology rhyme with mass passive collapse / belief). Advisors who choose silence feed him. He does not spark dissent; dissent is already a category error.

### What to keep from current sketches

Silent Court, keeping Aritzon’s court, anomaly, unopposed not cruel, never raise voice — all fit **arrogance finished** better than “empty absence” if prose shifts from pure void to **sovereign certainty that there is no game but his**.

### Soft spots

1. Design: name arrogance + vs Harban eagerness + vs Sha'ashon active faction.  
2. Don’t let arrogance become mustache-twirl speechifying — the point is he **doesn’t need to**.  
3. Cult locked: mainly prayed against; minority tyrants seek favor (Sha'ashon parallel).

---

## Fences

| God | vs Aritzon |
|-----|------------|
| **Harban (E)** | Eager war-weather; still has enemies and fields | Arrogance of unopposed rule; no real “no” left |
| **Sha'ashon** | Flames sides; needs multiplayer to poison | Multiplayer already irrelevant |
| **Shevuon / Shalalon** | Counterparties to oath/covenant | No one left to bind against |
| **Sha'alon** | Forced choice among ruins | No meaningful choice of opposition |
| **Motiel** | Long-game crossing | Political death of voice while alive |
| **Qorathiel** | Regal winter decree | Regal/unopposed *power over people*, not season |
| **Aritzon** | **Arrogance as only-option obedience** | |

---

## Verdict

| Path | |
|------|--|
| **Arrogance incarnate (only-option obedience)** | **Locked lean** — answers passivity without Harban-eagerness |
| **Blank clinical void** | Reject as wallpaper |
| **Loud sadistic tyrant** | Reject — wrong terminal, wrong vs Harban |



### Rank 10 principle (user — locked lean)

**Biomes are containers, not substrates.** Rank 10 is not "about the sand / the trees / the rocks / the mud" as stuff. Each suit reads **what that biome holds or does as mode weather**:

| Biome | Suit | Not the stuff | What it **contains / does** (mode) |
|-------|------|---------------|-------------------------------------|
| **Forests** | Spades | Not timber taxonomy | **Predatory cover** — formation erased; ground that hunts the disciplined line (Hard Mode restriction) |
| **Deserts** | Hearts | Not sand grains | **Mystique / sparse address** — stories on the wind, unanswered word, night-fire *we* (Social) |
| **Mountains** | Diamonds | **Not stacks of rock / load-pile architecture** | **Depths & resources** — caves, mines, seams, assay, what the mass holds (Systems entry of the deep) |
| **Swamps** | Clubs | Not mud chemistry | **Unemptiable return** — basin that comes back, growth that won't stay drained (Sandbox) |

So the Mountains reframe is not a special plea — it's **applying the same rule the row already uses**. Old Tzuron (span/gravity/foundation-stone) was treating the mountain as **building material / structural pile**, which is substrate-logic — and that's why she slid into Aretzon (Earth as foundation/base). Correct Diamonds read: mountain as **vault of the deep**, entry for contents.

**Aretzon fence under principle:** Earth Jack is element-as-foundation (base that doesn't move). Mountains 10 is biome-as-contents (what the high mass holds underneath). Different rank axes; no need to share "immovable rock woman" identity.

---
### User probe: Mountains = caves / mines / depths / resources (not stacks of rock)

**Idea:** Keep biome label **Mountains**. Change Systems subject from load-bearing *pile* (spans, gravity, first foundation stone) to what the mountain **holds and yields from within**: caves, mines, depths, resource bodies, assay, the deep account.

This is reportfolio of *function*, not of grid label — postcard tetrad intact; *tzur* still names the mountain; portfolio stops twinning Aretzon's surface foundation.

#### Why this works

| | **Aretzon (Earth)** | **Tzuron (Mountains, reframe)** |
|--|---------------------|----------------------------------|
| Layer | **Surface / base** — foundation claim, title, survey of ground you stand on or deed | **Depth / contents** — what the mass *contains*: ore, seam, void, grade, shaft account |
| Question | Is there a founding claim the system can rest on? | Is the **deep entry** true — grade, depth, support, reserve? |
| Failure | Broken title continuity; paper base with no standing | Wrong assay / wrong depth figure; mine that "worked" until the error found its level |
| Doesn't move | Foundation base of ownership | The mountain as **vault** — resource doesn't care about your surface story |
| Customers | Surveyors, land-holders, diaspora title | Miners, quarry-wards, assay masters, delvers, deep-guild engineers |
| Imagery | Boundary marker, deed, first stone of *title* | Adit, lamp, sample, seam map, timbered shaft |
| Parable shape | Perfect Deed (empty surface claim kept) | Wrong grade / thin timber / false reserve that held until it didn't |

**One-line split:** Aretzon = *who holds the ground.* Tzuron = *what the mountain holds.*

Mining **claim law** stays Aretzon (cadastral). **Geological/resource truth** of the hole stays Tzuron. Same ridge: her registry says you own it; Tzuron's column says whether the seam you entered was ever there.

#### Systems Mode (still Diamonds)

Not Clubs abundance (Zerael wants full harvest warmth). Not Spades "mountain kills the soft."  

**Cold object:** the **entered truth of the deep** — assay, reserve, depth, structure of the void — independent of hope, surface deed, or how long the gallery seemed fine.

| Keep from old Tzuron | Drop / rehome |
|----------------------|---------------|
| Late failure of wrong entry (Half-Right energy) | Bridge span as master icon |
| "Almost" not a column | First stone of *building* foundation (→ Aretzon title-stone or Harashon craft) |
| Unhurried severity optional | Mountain sovereign as pure gravity-on-architecture |
| Engineer customers (narrow to **mine/quarry/deep** structure) | General guild-architect of every freestanding span |

**Half-Right Span → portable:** e.g. **The False Seam** / **The Gallery That Held:** every measurement of the adit right except one grade or one timber figure; worked for eleven years; was wrong the day of the assay. Same systems beat, depth skin.

**Staked Span → retarget:** guild stakes youth to assay/mine-measure early; Tza'iron still calls theft of optionality. Shared parable survives with new trade.

#### vs neighbors

| God | Fence |
|-----|--------|
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title vs deep contents/resource figure |
| **Zerael** | Warm surface yield without theory vs entered deep reserve (cold systems) |
| **Rabakhon** | Biological replication process vs mineral/void extract account |
| **Ashiron** | Coin balance after sale vs resource still in the mountain / grade true |
| **Harashon** | Medium of the make (including timber/steel craft) vs whether the *hole's* numbers were true |
| **Chakamon** | Inherited manual copy vs this seam's assay (may use old book wrong) |
| **Ya'aron** | Cover erases formation on surface forest vs under-mountain systems |
| **Bitzon** | Ground that returns/grows vs mountain that is **extracted** (opposite vector) |
| **Taturiel** | First step on ridge vs first lamp in a mapped dark |

#### Personality (under reframe — options)

Depth weather differs from Aretzon's calm surface foundation:

| | Face | Play |
|--|------|------|
| **D1** | **Reserve / vault** | Holds much, reveals by measure; unhurried because the seam is older than the claim |
| **D2** | **Assay-severity** | Sample is sacred; "looks rich" is not a column; contempt for surface optimism |
| **D3** | **Deep-calm** | Quiet of caves; patient; not Aretzon's base-pride — **withholding** pride (the mountain keeps its own counsel until entered true) |

Avoid twinning Aretzon's exact "calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried" package — related still (both solid, both unhurried possible) but **identity** is vault/assay/depth, not foundation-that-doesn't-move.

#### Name

**Tzuron** (*tzur*) can **stay** — rock/mountain-fastness as the body that *contains* the deep, not as a stack of load-bearing stones. Design one-liner: mountains as resource-body / underworld of stone, not architecture.

#### Fit tag

Likely still *(clean)*: Mountains → mines/depths is a natural mortal read; load-span was the more forced pure-optimizer abstract. Optional *(seam)* if old span-cult minority remembers bridge-Tzuron — only if you want that history.

#### Lean

**Strong path:** Keep Mountains; reportfolio to **depths / mines / caves / resources**. Clears Aretzon without leaving the biome row. Rename optional (keep Tzuron). Rewrite worship, parable skin, Staked Span aim; drop foundation-stone collision entirely.

|----------------------|-------------------|
| Deep entry must be true (assay, map of seam, depth figure) | She **wants to go get it** — truth of the mountain is verified by descent, not only by desk |
| Resource/contents systems | Adventure is how mortals *meet* the vault, not abundance-without-theory (Zerael) |
| Wrong entry fails later | She loves the delving *and* still won't bless a pretty lie about the seam |

**Cold gloss add:** present for the **entered deep** — and worshipped as one who is **glad to go down**. Domain is still contents/systems of the mountain; personality is delver-joy.

**If she only loved adventure with no care for the figure:** she'd be soft-Taturiel. Fence: she can love the trip and still be the god of **whether what you brought back (or claimed was down there) was entered true**.

#### Fences (critical)

| God | They hold | She holds |
|-----|-----------|-----------|
| **Taturiel** | First step; unmapped *as such*; discovery creates ground; forgets she's been | **Known mountain body** — depths of *this* mass; return with a true account; not "anywhere unmapped" |
| **Derorel** | Freedom as unclosed question / won't settle the life-question | Adventure as **method toward a deep entry**, not endless refusal to conclude |
| **Tza'iron** | Optionality; abandon the path; stake as theft | May love many descents; still honors a **finished true assay** of a seam (can finish a map) |
| **Sharaq** | Distribution, no destination | She **has** a destination: the deep / the seam / the chamber |
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title | Under the claim, what's held |
| **Yetziron** | Variation for its own sake | Variation of path only to get the deep figure right |
| **Midvaron** | Adventurous desert *story* heat | Adventurous **descent** — lamp, rope, rock dark; different register |

#### Tag implications

| Tag | If |
|-----|-----|
| **(clean)** | "Mountain contents" naturally attracts delver cults; adventure is how you meet assay — domain implies the face |
| **(seam)** | Domain is deep entry; **love of adventure** is extra worship built (nothing in pure assay requires joy) — apprentices describe thrill theology can't require |
| **(miscollapse)** | Older cult was pure vault/assay desk-god; delver joy is later drift |

**Lean:** **(seam)** is honest and good — same family as Chakamon warmth (nothing about transmission requires affection). Adventure-love is the seam that makes her vivid; assay remains the column.

#### vs Aretzon (still clear)

Aretzon: calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried foundation — **stays**.  
Tzuron: **moves into the dark on purpose** — opposite energy, same Diamonds suit. Twin problem dissolves.

#### Risks

1. **Taturiel bleed** — fix with "this mountain's deep," return-with-account, not first-print-anywhere.  
2. **Becomes pulp archaeologist only** — keep Systems: sample, map, grade, false seam parable.  
3. **Monsters/traps drift** — user veto: **no**; wonder of the deep, not bestiary.  
4. **Gender/Lara coding** — fine with she; competence and joy, not pinup checklist.

#### One-line (candidate)

> *She loves the going down — rope, lamp, the bend that isn't on the last survey — and still only blesses the descent that brings back a true account of what the mountain holds.*

#### Personality lock package (candidate)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Wanderlust / spelunker joy** | Lara Croft-without-monsters: competent, curious, glad of the deep |
| **Systems still** | Assay, seam map, depth figure, false entry fails later |
| **Seam** | Adventure-love not required by domain; cults built it |
| **Not** | Surface title (Aretzon); first step anywhere (Taturiel); abandon as theology (Tza'iron) |

## Notes status

**LOCKED for sketches:** arrogance incarnate; no shout (uncouth); rightful-ruler certainty; collapse-by-will rhyme; not Harban eagerness. **Cult:** mainly prayed against (parties/courts that still want a “no”); some tyrants seek his favor. Terminal unopposed power.

## Reconciled

**2026-08-07** — Written into pantheon-sketches.md. Personality rebuilt around arrogance-as-ontology per the locked lean; explicit Design fences added against both Harban (needs a field/enemy; he needs neither) and Sha'ashon (still works a room capable of sides; his rooms can't generate one). Cult clarified as apotropaic-primary with the minority-tyrant exception. Silent Court parable and Anomaly kept unchanged.
