# Asaphon (ʾ-S-P) — Autumn (Diamonds Ace)

**Sketch status:** Reimagine — essence of autumn, not harvest-tally only.
**Gender:** **he** (season 2–2 with Qayitzon male).
**Fit tag:** TBD under reimagine (seam of savor-addition may stay or move).

---

## Season essence (user — locked direction)

| Ace | Season | Essence |
|-----|--------|---------|
| **Chadashiel** | Spring | **New** |
| **Qayitzon** | Summer | **Growth unchecked** |
| **Asaphon** | Autumn | **Closing out and preparing for rest** |
| **Qorathiel** | Winter | **Still regal** — rest/absolute as settled weather |

**User:** Current entry is all tallies and harvests — that’s an **aspect**, not the essence of autumn.  
If Spring = new and Summer = growth unchecked, Autumn = **closing out + preparing for rest** (so Winter can be still).

### Essence vs aspect

| Essence (primary) | Aspects / expressions (secondary) |
|-------------------|-----------------------------------|
| Closing out processes | Year-end ledger, finishing the count |
| Preparing for rest | Storing, settling, winding down work, putting affairs in order before stillness |
| Diamonds mode | How that close is **entered / completed / made legible as done** — not mere mood |

Tallies and harvests remain **valid worship and parable ground** (Ruinous Year Closed Clean still works) but as **one way** Systems Mode performs “the period is finished and ready for rest,” not the whole god.

### Cold gloss (candidate)

> **Autumn — closing out and preparing for rest.** Asaphon holds the completion of open processes so stillness can follow: the books closed, the work wound down, the account of the period made whole enough to stop. He does not hold growth (Qayitzon/Zerael), onset (Chadashiel), or rest itself as absolute weather (Qorathiel). Harvest tallies are a common face of the close, not the essence.

### Diamonds still

Systems Mode: “done” is real when the **entry/process is complete** — not when people feel sleepy. Incomplete close (good year never finished counting; work abandoned mid-wind-down without a settled state) fails him. Honest completion of a bad period can still satisfy (finalizer without comfort).

### Personality (locked)

**User image:** whistling while he checks off items on a list - work-oriented, checklist-focused, content at the work.

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Defined by the checklist** | Outside the list he is a **bit of a blank slate** - little personality beyond the task stream |
| **Just wants the tasks done** | Not drama, not depth-chat; open item to closed item |
| **Whistling / content at work** | Mild satisfaction in ticking boxes, not charisma or storm |
| **Can be boring to be around** | Feature, not bug - not every god is exciting or earth-shaking |
| **vs clinical pile** | Not cold cruelty or deep absorption in theory - **plain workman of the close** |
| **vs Qayitzon** | Never finishes; Asaphon only lights up (slightly) when finishing |
| **vs Qorathiel** | Regal still presence; he is ordinary closure labor before rest |

**One-line:** *Whistling through the checklist, blank outside it - he just wants the tasks done, and he can be boring company; not every god has to shake the earth.*

**Seam:** Systems Mode need not produce a vivid character; contentment-at-closing is almost the only color on an otherwise thin self.
### What demotes / keeps

| Old emphasis | Under reimagine |
|--------------|-----------------|
| Only guild-auditors / year total | Widen: anyone closing a season of work, settling before winter, finalizing a process |
| Harvest as whole domain | Harvest as aspect of close-out |
| Ruinous Year Closed Clean | Keep — pure close without kind yield |
| Full ledger at frost | Keep as major rite; not only rite |
| “Blessing unexplained” | Keep — close is the blessing, not surplus |

### Fences

| God | They hold | He holds |
|-----|-----------|----------|
| **Chadashiel** | New / open | Close / prepare to stop |
| **Qayitzon** | Unchecked growth; never notices finished | Requires finished / wind-down |
| **Qorathiel** | Rest already absolute; still regal | Preparing for rest; the close *before* stillness |
| **Zerael** | Abundance without theory | Close of a period, not endless yield |
| **Ashiron** | Daily coin close | Seasonal / process close-out |
| **Koseb** | Estate before death | Living cycle’s close toward rest |
| **Kevodon** | Name audit across life | Period finalized |
| **Asaphon** | **Close-out + prepare for rest (systems-complete)** | |

### Defense / flaw (candidate)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** | Will not underwrite endless open process when the season of closing has come; incomplete books / unfinished wind-down fail him |
| **Flaw** | May demand close when people aren’t ready; “rest” prepared as entry can feel cold; honest ruin closed still “blessed” while living hurt |

---

## Open questions

- [x] Male + season 2–2 with Qayitzon.
- [x] Essence: closing out + preparing for rest (tally/harvest = aspect).
- [x] Personality: whistling checklist; work-oriented content closer.
- [x] Harvest/tally = aspect (no fixed further detail required for lock).
- [ ] Sketches rewrite.


### Personality probe: spelunking wanderlust (Clubs creep) — Lara Croft without monsters

**User:** Allow Tzuron to creep a bit into Clubs — wanderlust, spelunking; loves the adventure; almost Lara Croft feel **without the monsters**.

#### What that face is

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Wanderlust** | Not content at the registry desk; the deep is for *going* |
| **Spelunker** | Caves, mines, adits, unmapped dark as joy, not only workplace |
| **Adventure-love** | Delight in the descent, the squeeze, the first lamp around a bend |
| **Competent delver** | Lara Croft competence/curiosity — **not** combat, tombs full of guardians, or trophy killing |
| **Not** | Monster-slayer; Taturiel's first-step-on-any-ridge; pure optionality abandon (Tza'iron); freedom-as-unclosed question (Derorel) |


#### User correction: not real Clubs theft

**User:** Spelunking only "creeps on Clubs" in a soft sense — **Clubs is not about exploration in specific**, so there is **no real suit-level fence**. Freedom-of-exploration energy is **inferred from uncontained/Sandbox mode**, not a portfolio Clubs monopolizes.

| Clarify | |
|---------|--|
| **Clubs mode** | Uncontained: growth without design, without a stop; optionality, distribution, abandon, first-step *as cards* — not "the adventure suit" |
| **Exploration (specific)** | **Taturiel** alone (Queen) — first step, unmapped, retains nothing |
| **Freedom (specific)** | **Derorel** — open question, not delving |
| **Tzuron adventure** | Personality + method for **Diamonds deep-entry** — does not steal a Clubs domain that doesn't exist as "exploration generally" |

**Implication:** Drop anxiety about "Clubs creep" as suit violation. Keep **card fences** only where real:
- **Taturiel** — mechanism of first-step/forgetting/unmapped, not "you can't enjoy caves"
- **Derorel / Tza'iron / Sharaq** — only if her joy becomes their cold gloss (endless unclosed, abandon-as-theology, no destination)

She can be fully Lara-Croft-delver-joyful **without** being a Clubs god. The uncontained *flavor* of wanderlust is available to any suit's face; Clubs doesn't own it.

**Revised framing:** Not "Clubs-colored seam." Prefer: **delver joy as (seam) on Systems deep-contents** — extra worship built on assay/depths, parallel Chakamon warmth. Optional note that Sandbox *mode* also loves going further, but that's mode rhyme not portfolio theft.

---

# Kevodon (K-B-D) — Honor (Diamonds King)

**Sketch status:** Review opened (Diamonds King; clinical-cluster candidate from suit review).
**Fit tag:** *(miscollapse)* — honor without "was he loved"; minority text remembers warmer Kevodon.
**Root:** K-B-D (*kavod*, honor/weight).
**Roster:** *The Weighed Name — reputation not felt, only recorded: ledger of conduct courts/heirs consult whether or not you're in the room.*
**Gender:** he/him.

---

## Process checks

1. **Diamonds = Systems.** Pass — name as audited record; love not a field.
2. **King row:** War (Harban E relish) / Glory (Shevachiel presence) / **Honor** (recorded weight) / Freedom (Derorel). Clean: felt glory vs entered honor.
3. **Miscollapse load-bearing.** Domain need not be cold; magistrates built audit-honor. Minority: cared whether well-conducted man died with someone at his side — reverse of empty house as blessing.
4. **Register risk (suit review).** Personality is **default Diamond auditor** — thorough, fair, blind to love. Strong mechanic; face may blend with Rabakhon/Rifon/Koseb clinical family.
5. **vs Spades wallpaper issue.** Different: not grim stoic force — cold *ledger*. Same problem class: **one dialect**.

---

## What works (protect)

| Piece | Why |
|-------|-----|
| **Name survives audit; loved not a field** | Pure systems honor |
| **Well-Kept Name** | Perfect record, empty house — guild blessing vs old texts as what he was meant to prevent |
| **Unamendable guild-roll entry** | Careless original is the sin of amendment |
| **Miscollapse explicit** | Design good (unlike old Harban Design) |
| **Fence Shevachiel** | Recorded name vs live presence that seizes breath |
| **Fence Ahavel/Chayelon** | Love and company not in his columns |

**Default lean:** mechanic + miscollapse **keep**. Decision: own cold auditor as the *point* of miscollapse, or give him a **distinct counting-pleasure / face** so he isn't soft-Takanor.

---

## Soft spots / discussion

### 1. Personality (locked)

**User:** stuffy **seneschal / herald** reciting the rolls of honor and deeds — key word: **pretentious**.

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Pretentious** | Performs the weight of honor; ceremony and diction that insist the roll *matters* as high culture of reputation |
| **Herald of deeds** | Voice of the record — announces what the ledger holds, not what the heart held |
| **Recites the rolls** | Honor is *spoken entry* as much as written; ceremony of names and conduct |
| **Blind to love-column** | Will recite a perfect empty life without noting the empty house (or notes only what's on the roll) |
| **vs clinical pile** | Not doctor/engineer absorption — **court officer of the name** |
| **vs Shevachiel** | Herald of *record*, not of the hall's breath in the moment |
| **vs Harban** | Deeds as entered standing, not war-weather relish |
| **Miscollapse** | Magistrates/guilds built honor-as-roll-call; older texts wanted someone at the deathbed the herald does not mention |

**One-line:** *A pretentious seneschal-herald of the rolls — he will recite your deeds with full ceremony until the name is weighed, and never ask who loved you.*

**Flaw form:** Perfect recitation of a hollow life; ceremony without companionship; people live for the roll he will read.
### 2. Width of Honor

Keep as **recorded standing / weighed name**, not battlefield honor (Harban), not glory (Shevachiel), not oath-keeping (Shevuon). Amendment forbidden is strong and specific.

### 3. Well-Kept Name tension

Guild: empty house + perfect roll = highest blessing.  
Old texts: that outcome is what honor was invoked to *prevent*.  
**Do not reconcile** — miscollapse gold. Optional: minority still prays the old way (someone at the deathbed).

### 4. vs Asaphon

Asaphon: period closes, content whistle.  
Kevodon: life-name never amended, weightier, no whistle — different scale and mood.

---

## Cold gloss (candidate)

> **Honor — the weighed name that survives audit.** Not whether you were loved or glorious in the room; whether the record of conduct holds for heirs and courts. Miscallapse: magistrates stripped affection from honor; older texts remember a god who cared if a good man died alone. Worship: enter the name once; do not amend.

### Defense / flaw

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** | Will not treat love or popularity as honor-fields; will not bless careless amendment of the roll |
| **Flaw** | Perfect name + empty house; honor without companionship; people optimize the ledger over the life |

### Personality options for lock

| | |
|--|--|
| **A** | Cold thorough auditor (current) — miscollapse as wallpaper risk |
| **C lean** | **Weight/gravitas** — *kavod* as heaviness; slow, solemn, the name matters as mass; still blind to love-column |
| **B+C** | Gravitas + quiet pride when a name holds unamended for forty years |

**One-line (C-lean):** *He does not ask if you were loved — only whether the name still weighs what it claimed, unamended, when you are gone.*

---

## Fences

| God | vs Kevodon |
|-----|------------|
| **Shevachiel** | Live glory-presence | Recorded honor after the room empties |
| **Harban** | War-weather relish | Name's conduct weight, not field savor |
| **Shevuon** | Personal oath collected | Life-roll of standing, not single swear |
| **Shalalon** | Group standards held | Individual name audit |
| **Asaphon** | Period books closed | Name across a life |
| **Koseb** | Estate account before death | Reputation account that outlives |
| **Ashiron** | Coin balance | Conduct weight |
| **Ahavel / Chayelon** | Love / company | Not fields he keeps |
| **Kevodon** | **Weighed name / honor as audit** | |

## Verdict

| Path | |
|------|--|
| **A. Keep as written** | Valid; Design already owns miscollapse |
| **B. Personality: gravitas/weight** | Helps Diamonds register; still cold to love |
| **C. Minority counter-dogma louder** | Harban-style dual cult for "died with someone" |

**LOCKED lean:** stuffy seneschal/herald of the rolls; miscollapse + Well-Kept Name keep; not clinical blank.



### Rank 10 principle (user — locked lean)

**Biomes are containers, not substrates.** Rank 10 is not "about the sand / the trees / the rocks / the mud" as stuff. Each suit reads **what that biome holds or does as mode weather**:

| Biome | Suit | Not the stuff | What it **contains / does** (mode) |
|-------|------|---------------|-------------------------------------|
| **Forests** | Spades | Not timber taxonomy | **Predatory cover** — formation erased; ground that hunts the disciplined line (Hard Mode restriction) |
| **Deserts** | Hearts | Not sand grains | **Mystique / sparse address** — stories on the wind, unanswered word, night-fire *we* (Social) |
| **Mountains** | Diamonds | **Not stacks of rock / load-pile architecture** | **Depths & resources** — caves, mines, seams, assay, what the mass holds (Systems entry of the deep) |
| **Swamps** | Clubs | Not mud chemistry | **Unemptiable return** — basin that comes back, growth that won't stay drained (Sandbox) |

So the Mountains reframe is not a special plea — it's **applying the same rule the row already uses**. Old Tzuron (span/gravity/foundation-stone) was treating the mountain as **building material / structural pile**, which is substrate-logic — and that's why she slid into Aretzon (Earth as foundation/base). Correct Diamonds read: mountain as **vault of the deep**, entry for contents.

**Aretzon fence under principle:** Earth Jack is element-as-foundation (base that doesn't move). Mountains 10 is biome-as-contents (what the high mass holds underneath). Different rank axes; no need to share "immovable rock woman" identity.

---
### User probe: Mountains = caves / mines / depths / resources (not stacks of rock)

**Idea:** Keep biome label **Mountains**. Change Systems subject from load-bearing *pile* (spans, gravity, first foundation stone) to what the mountain **holds and yields from within**: caves, mines, depths, resource bodies, assay, the deep account.

This is reportfolio of *function*, not of grid label — postcard tetrad intact; *tzur* still names the mountain; portfolio stops twinning Aretzon's surface foundation.

#### Why this works

| | **Aretzon (Earth)** | **Tzuron (Mountains, reframe)** |
|--|---------------------|----------------------------------|
| Layer | **Surface / base** — foundation claim, title, survey of ground you stand on or deed | **Depth / contents** — what the mass *contains*: ore, seam, void, grade, shaft account |
| Question | Is there a founding claim the system can rest on? | Is the **deep entry** true — grade, depth, support, reserve? |
| Failure | Broken title continuity; paper base with no standing | Wrong assay / wrong depth figure; mine that "worked" until the error found its level |
| Doesn't move | Foundation base of ownership | The mountain as **vault** — resource doesn't care about your surface story |
| Customers | Surveyors, land-holders, diaspora title | Miners, quarry-wards, assay masters, delvers, deep-guild engineers |
| Imagery | Boundary marker, deed, first stone of *title* | Adit, lamp, sample, seam map, timbered shaft |
| Parable shape | Perfect Deed (empty surface claim kept) | Wrong grade / thin timber / false reserve that held until it didn't |

**One-line split:** Aretzon = *who holds the ground.* Tzuron = *what the mountain holds.*

Mining **claim law** stays Aretzon (cadastral). **Geological/resource truth** of the hole stays Tzuron. Same ridge: her registry says you own it; Tzuron's column says whether the seam you entered was ever there.

#### Systems Mode (still Diamonds)

Not Clubs abundance (Zerael wants full harvest warmth). Not Spades "mountain kills the soft."  

**Cold object:** the **entered truth of the deep** — assay, reserve, depth, structure of the void — independent of hope, surface deed, or how long the gallery seemed fine.

| Keep from old Tzuron | Drop / rehome |
|----------------------|---------------|
| Late failure of wrong entry (Half-Right energy) | Bridge span as master icon |
| "Almost" not a column | First stone of *building* foundation (→ Aretzon title-stone or Harashon craft) |
| Unhurried severity optional | Mountain sovereign as pure gravity-on-architecture |
| Engineer customers (narrow to **mine/quarry/deep** structure) | General guild-architect of every freestanding span |

**Half-Right Span → portable:** e.g. **The False Seam** / **The Gallery That Held:** every measurement of the adit right except one grade or one timber figure; worked for eleven years; was wrong the day of the assay. Same systems beat, depth skin.

**Staked Span → retarget:** guild stakes youth to assay/mine-measure early; Tza'iron still calls theft of optionality. Shared parable survives with new trade.

#### vs neighbors

| God | Fence |
|-----|--------|
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title vs deep contents/resource figure |
| **Zerael** | Warm surface yield without theory vs entered deep reserve (cold systems) |
| **Rabakhon** | Biological replication process vs mineral/void extract account |
| **Ashiron** | Coin balance after sale vs resource still in the mountain / grade true |
| **Harashon** | Medium of the make (including timber/steel craft) vs whether the *hole's* numbers were true |
| **Chakamon** | Inherited manual copy vs this seam's assay (may use old book wrong) |
| **Ya'aron** | Cover erases formation on surface forest vs under-mountain systems |
| **Bitzon** | Ground that returns/grows vs mountain that is **extracted** (opposite vector) |
| **Taturiel** | First step on ridge vs first lamp in a mapped dark |

#### Personality (under reframe — options)

Depth weather differs from Aretzon's calm surface foundation:

| | Face | Play |
|--|------|------|
| **D1** | **Reserve / vault** | Holds much, reveals by measure; unhurried because the seam is older than the claim |
| **D2** | **Assay-severity** | Sample is sacred; "looks rich" is not a column; contempt for surface optimism |
| **D3** | **Deep-calm** | Quiet of caves; patient; not Aretzon's base-pride — **withholding** pride (the mountain keeps its own counsel until entered true) |

Avoid twinning Aretzon's exact "calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried" package — related still (both solid, both unhurried possible) but **identity** is vault/assay/depth, not foundation-that-doesn't-move.

#### Name

**Tzuron** (*tzur*) can **stay** — rock/mountain-fastness as the body that *contains* the deep, not as a stack of load-bearing stones. Design one-liner: mountains as resource-body / underworld of stone, not architecture.

#### Fit tag

Likely still *(clean)*: Mountains → mines/depths is a natural mortal read; load-span was the more forced pure-optimizer abstract. Optional *(seam)* if old span-cult minority remembers bridge-Tzuron — only if you want that history.

#### Lean

**Strong path:** Keep Mountains; reportfolio to **depths / mines / caves / resources**. Clears Aretzon without leaving the biome row. Rename optional (keep Tzuron). Rewrite worship, parable skin, Staked Span aim; drop foundation-stone collision entirely.

### Personality probe: spelunking wanderlust (Clubs creep) — Lara Croft without monsters

**User:** Allow Tzuron to creep a bit into Clubs — wanderlust, spelunking; loves the adventure; almost Lara Croft feel **without the monsters**.

#### What that face is

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Wanderlust** | Not content at the registry desk; the deep is for *going* |
| **Spelunker** | Caves, mines, adits, unmapped dark as joy, not only workplace |
| **Adventure-love** | Delight in the descent, the squeeze, the first lamp around a bend |
| **Competent delver** | Lara Croft competence/curiosity — **not** combat, tombs full of guardians, or trophy killing |
| **Not** | Monster-slayer; Taturiel's first-step-on-any-ridge; pure optionality abandon (Tza'iron); freedom-as-unclosed question (Derorel) |


#### User correction: not real Clubs theft

**User:** Spelunking only "creeps on Clubs" in a soft sense — **Clubs is not about exploration in specific**, so there is **no real suit-level fence**. Freedom-of-exploration energy is **inferred from uncontained/Sandbox mode**, not a portfolio Clubs monopolizes.

| Clarify | |
|---------|--|
| **Clubs mode** | Uncontained: growth without design, without a stop; optionality, distribution, abandon, first-step *as cards* — not "the adventure suit" |
| **Exploration (specific)** | **Taturiel** alone (Queen) — first step, unmapped, retains nothing |
| **Freedom (specific)** | **Derorel** — open question, not delving |
| **Tzuron adventure** | Personality + method for **Diamonds deep-entry** — does not steal a Clubs domain that doesn't exist as "exploration generally" |

**Implication:** Drop anxiety about "Clubs creep" as suit violation. Keep **card fences** only where real:
- **Taturiel** — mechanism of first-step/forgetting/unmapped, not "you can't enjoy caves"
- **Derorel / Tza'iron / Sharaq** — only if her joy becomes their cold gloss (endless unclosed, abandon-as-theology, no destination)

She can be fully Lara-Croft-delver-joyful **without** being a Clubs god. The uncontained *flavor* of wanderlust is available to any suit's face; Clubs doesn't own it.

**Revised framing:** Not "Clubs-colored seam." Prefer: **delver joy as (seam) on Systems deep-contents** — extra worship built on assay/depths, parallel Chakamon warmth. Optional note that Sandbox *mode* also loves going further, but that's mode rhyme not portfolio theft.

---
#### Why it can still be Diamonds

Clubs creep is real — own it as **face/method**, not domain swap:

| Diamonds core (keep) | Clubs-colored how |
|----------------------|-------------------|
| Deep entry must be true (assay, map of seam, depth figure) | She **wants to go get it** — truth of the mountain is verified by descent, not only by desk |
| Resource/contents systems | Adventure is how mortals *meet* the vault, not abundance-without-theory (Zerael) |
| Wrong entry fails later | She loves the delving *and* still won't bless a pretty lie about the seam |

**Cold gloss add:** present for the **entered deep** — and worshipped as one who is **glad to go down**. Domain is still contents/systems of the mountain; personality is delver-joy.

**If she only loved adventure with no care for the figure:** she'd be soft-Taturiel. Fence: she can love the trip and still be the god of **whether what you brought back (or claimed was down there) was entered true**.

#### Fences (critical)

| God | They hold | She holds |
|-----|-----------|-----------|
| **Taturiel** | First step; unmapped *as such*; discovery creates ground; forgets she's been | **Known mountain body** — depths of *this* mass; return with a true account; not "anywhere unmapped" |
| **Derorel** | Freedom as unclosed question / won't settle the life-question | Adventure as **method toward a deep entry**, not endless refusal to conclude |
| **Tza'iron** | Optionality; abandon the path; stake as theft | May love many descents; still honors a **finished true assay** of a seam (can finish a map) |
| **Sharaq** | Distribution, no destination | She **has** a destination: the deep / the seam / the chamber |
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title | Under the claim, what's held |
| **Yetziron** | Variation for its own sake | Variation of path only to get the deep figure right |
| **Midvaron** | Adventurous desert *story* heat | Adventurous **descent** — lamp, rope, rock dark; different register |

#### Tag implications

| Tag | If |
|-----|-----|
| **(clean)** | "Mountain contents" naturally attracts delver cults; adventure is how you meet assay — domain implies the face |
| **(seam)** | Domain is deep entry; **love of adventure** is extra worship built (nothing in pure assay requires joy) — apprentices describe thrill theology can't require |
| **(miscollapse)** | Older cult was pure vault/assay desk-god; delver joy is later drift |

**Lean:** **(seam)** is honest and good — same family as Chakamon warmth (nothing about transmission requires affection). Adventure-love is the seam that makes her vivid; assay remains the column.

#### vs Aretzon (still clear)

Aretzon: calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried foundation — **stays**.  
Tzuron: **moves into the dark on purpose** — opposite energy, same Diamonds suit. Twin problem dissolves.

#### Risks

1. **Taturiel bleed** — fix with "this mountain's deep," return-with-account, not first-print-anywhere.  
2. **Becomes pulp archaeologist only** — keep Systems: sample, map, grade, false seam parable.  
3. **Monsters/traps drift** — user veto: **no**; wonder of the deep, not bestiary.  
4. **Gender/Lara coding** — fine with she; competence and joy, not pinup checklist.

#### One-line (candidate)

> *She loves the going down — rope, lamp, the bend that isn't on the last survey — and still only blesses the descent that brings back a true account of what the mountain holds.*

#### Personality lock package (candidate)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Wanderlust / spelunker joy** | Lara Croft-without-monsters: competent, curious, glad of the deep |
| **Systems still** | Assay, seam map, depth figure, false entry fails later |

# Rabakhon (R-B-H) — Fertility (Diamonds Queen)

**Sketch status:** Reimagine lean — personality **flighty process-fascinated** (Dr. Ruth Design vibe); someone excited about the system.
**Fit tag (current):** *(miscollapse)* — clinical dynastic completion; harvest-hymn minority. May reassess under new face.
**Root:** R-B-H. Gender: **she/her** (locked). User: male fertility god reads creepy; Dr. Ruth process-face fits she.
**Roster/mechanic hold:** process completed; willingness/feelings not fields; entry before name; cousin Rifon (repair vs replicate).

---

## User direction

**Personality:** flighty **Dr. Ruth** energy — shameless about the detail, absolutely fascinated by the process. *"Did you notice how the zygote split? I wonder if…"*  
**Suit need:** somebody has to be **excited about the system** (Diamonds register fix) — not another cold auditor.

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Excited about the system** | Loves the mechanism of replication — cleavage, timing, successful run |
| **Shameless about detail** | Intimate/biological frankness without embarrassment; process talk in mixed company |
| **Flighty** | Jumps to the next interesting wrinkle mid-thought; curiosity over dignity |
| **Not** | Blank clinical void; warm abundance (Zerael); protective infant guardian (Yaladiel) |
| **Still Diamonds** | Fascination is with **whether/how the process completes**, not romance or the child's personhood |

**Design-only vibe:** Dr. Ruth — not in-world name.

**One-line:** *Shameless and flighty over the machinery of making life — "did you see how it split?" — excited about the system, not the poetry of the house.*

### Mechanic keep / tension

| Keep | Under new face |
|------|----------------|
| Process completed = standing | She cares *how* it runs and *that* it completes |
| Feelings/willingness not fields | Can discuss anatomy shamelessly without treating consent/romance as sacred columns — **watch ethics**: Diamonds cold on willingness is harsher with a cheerful face; Design should note she is fascinated by process, not licensed to ignore persons in cult practice |
| Entry before child named | Still object = run/result entry |
| Harvest hymn minority | Older abundance warmth vs process-nerd orthodoxy |
| vs Rifon | Rifon: repair/fascinated by failing valve; Rabakhon: **replication run**, delighted by successful biology |

**Miscollapse options:**

| Reading | |
|---------|--|
| **A. Keep** | Dynasties wanted heirs-as-entries; she became process-completion god; shameless excitement is how process-love looks |
| **B. Soften** | Cheerful systems face is clean-ish for "fertility as biology system"; harvest hymn is older softer read |
| **C. Dual** | Official dynastic ledger cold; living cult is Dr. Ruth process-fascinated (officers vs midwives?) |

**Lean:** Personality **locked lean** as Dr. Ruth systems-excitement; keep completion mechanic; miscollapse can stay as "person/feelings not fields" + empty-name entry, not as "must be cold-voiced."


### Bridge function (user)

Rabakhon's process-fascinated face **bridges** the birth/new-adjacent goddesses without stealing their jobs:

| Goddess | She holds | He bridges by |
|---------|-----------|----------------|
| **Chadashiel** | New *starts* (season/relation/onset) | New *life as a process that begins* — the run starting, not the social spring |
| **Yaladiel** | Newborn/infant as protected person in the *we* | The **machinery that produces** that person — before she guards the result |
| **Chayelon** | Social health of mother/child after | The **system map of making** (and with Rifon, of body); not the care-graph |
| **Ahavel** | Love-forms that may include making a family | Process that does not require her columns |

So: **newness and birth weather** (Hearts) <-> **replication system** (Diamonds) <-> **repair system** (Rifon) <-> **warm abundance** (Zerael).  
She is the excited technician in the middle of the life-making cluster, not the welcomer, guardian, or nurse.

### Defense / flaw

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** | Will not treat unfinished replication as success; will not substitute love-story for completed process |
| **Flaw** | Shameless process-talk can erase dignity; fascination with the run over the people in it; dynastic "heir entered" still hollow if only the entry matters |

### Fences

| God | vs Rabakhon |
|-----|-------------|
| **Rifon** | Repair map; valve interest | Replication run; zygote-split delight |
| **Zerael** | Warm abundance without theory | Process theory with excitement |
| **Yaladiel** | Protect newborn person | Process before/during making, not guardian of infant |
| **Ahavel** | Love-forms | Not required for his columns |
| **Asaphon** | Close period books | Complete biological process entry |
| **Chayelon** | Social health of mother/child | Not wellness graph; system run |

# Aretzon (ʾ-R-Ṣ) — Earth (Diamonds Jack)

**Sketch status:** Review opened (Diamonds Jack; purest-optimizer / claim-over-ground).
**Fit tag (current):** *(clean — purest optimizer)*
**Root:** ʾ-R-Ṣ (*eretz*, land).
**Roster:** *The Unclaimed Foundation — the deed to land nobody can currently stand on; property law keeping perfect record of ownership over ground untouched by its rightful claimant longer than anyone living remembers.*
**Gender:** **she/her** (locked — Mother Earth / classical feminine).

---

## Process checks

1. **Diamonds = Systems.** Pass — the *entry* (claim/deed) is real; soil use and presence are not required columns.
2. **Jack row:** Fire (Sarphon) / Water (Demahel) / **Earth** / Air (Sharaq). Elemental Jacks; her subject is not dirt-as-Clubs growth but **title persistence**.
3. **Clean tag.** Design already states the hard choice: Earth could sit Clubs (soil/growth/fertility) but the mechanic is pure claim-ledger. That is correctly clean if cult and personality stay on *deed*, not harvest/garden.
4. **Register risk (suit review).** Personality is **absorbed busy bookkeeper of claims** — purest optimizer dialect. Strong mechanic; face can blend with Kevodon/Asaphon/Takanor clinical-or-clerk family unless she has a distinct *counting pleasure* or land-title weather.
5. **Name collision:** **Aretzon** (Earth, ʾ-R-Ṣ) vs **Aritzon** (Tyranny, ʿ-R-Ṣ). Intentional near-root pair; prose and speech must not blur. Different suits, ranks, genders, weather — but reader-adjacent. Design should keep one fence line when either is rewritten.
6. **Shared parable gold:** Unclaimed Ridge (× Taturiel) is load-bearing for Jack/Clubs edge — protect.

---

## What works (protect)

| Piece | Why |
|-------|-----|
| **Claim persists independent of use** | Purest Systems Mode in the suit — entry outranks ground |
| **Perfect Deed** | Absurd beauty of systems piety: renew forever, markers gone, still finest deed |
| **Recopy errors and all** | Accuracy-as-amendment worse than wrong continuity — sibling to Kevodon's unamendable roll, different object (title text not life-name) |
| **Busy / absorbed** | Not cruel; not warm; *occupied by the registry* — temperament = job |
| **Worshipped by land-holders & surveyors** | Correct customers; diaspora claim culture is free story fuel |
| **Design Earth-in-Diamonds** | Explicit Clubs fence already written; keep |
| **Unclaimed Ridge** | Taturiel first-step forgetfulness vs permanent claim — clean clash of modes |

**Default lean:** mechanic + Perfect Deed + Ridge **stand well**. Decision: keep purest-optimizer face as *the point* of clean, or give her a **distinct title-weather** so Diamonds mid-suit isn't one clerk voice.

### Rank 10 principle (user — locked lean)

**Biomes are containers, not substrates.** Rank 10 is not "about the sand / the trees / the rocks / the mud" as stuff. Each suit reads **what that biome holds or does as mode weather**:

| Biome | Suit | Not the stuff | What it **contains / does** (mode) |
|-------|------|---------------|-------------------------------------|
| **Forests** | Spades | Not timber taxonomy | **Predatory cover** — formation erased; ground that hunts the disciplined line (Hard Mode restriction) |
| **Deserts** | Hearts | Not sand grains | **Mystique / sparse address** — stories on the wind, unanswered word, night-fire *we* (Social) |
| **Mountains** | Diamonds | **Not stacks of rock / load-pile architecture** | **Depths & resources** — caves, mines, seams, assay, what the mass holds (Systems entry of the deep) |
| **Swamps** | Clubs | Not mud chemistry | **Unemptiable return** — basin that comes back, growth that won't stay drained (Sandbox) |

So the Mountains reframe is not a special plea — it's **applying the same rule the row already uses**. Old Tzuron (span/gravity/foundation-stone) was treating the mountain as **building material / structural pile**, which is substrate-logic — and that's why she slid into Aretzon (Earth as foundation/base). Correct Diamonds read: mountain as **vault of the deep**, entry for contents.

**Aretzon fence under principle:** Earth Jack is element-as-foundation (base that doesn't move). Mountains 10 is biome-as-contents (what the high mass holds underneath). Different rank axes; no need to share "immovable rock woman" identity.

---
### User probe: Mountains = caves / mines / depths / resources (not stacks of rock)

**Idea:** Keep biome label **Mountains**. Change Systems subject from load-bearing *pile* (spans, gravity, first foundation stone) to what the mountain **holds and yields from within**: caves, mines, depths, resource bodies, assay, the deep account.

This is reportfolio of *function*, not of grid label — postcard tetrad intact; *tzur* still names the mountain; portfolio stops twinning Aretzon's surface foundation.

#### Why this works

| | **Aretzon (Earth)** | **Tzuron (Mountains, reframe)** |
|--|---------------------|----------------------------------|
| Layer | **Surface / base** — foundation claim, title, survey of ground you stand on or deed | **Depth / contents** — what the mass *contains*: ore, seam, void, grade, shaft account |
| Question | Is there a founding claim the system can rest on? | Is the **deep entry** true — grade, depth, support, reserve? |
| Failure | Broken title continuity; paper base with no standing | Wrong assay / wrong depth figure; mine that "worked" until the error found its level |
| Doesn't move | Foundation base of ownership | The mountain as **vault** — resource doesn't care about your surface story |
| Customers | Surveyors, land-holders, diaspora title | Miners, quarry-wards, assay masters, delvers, deep-guild engineers |
| Imagery | Boundary marker, deed, first stone of *title* | Adit, lamp, sample, seam map, timbered shaft |
| Parable shape | Perfect Deed (empty surface claim kept) | Wrong grade / thin timber / false reserve that held until it didn't |

**One-line split:** Aretzon = *who holds the ground.* Tzuron = *what the mountain holds.*

Mining **claim law** stays Aretzon (cadastral). **Geological/resource truth** of the hole stays Tzuron. Same ridge: her registry says you own it; Tzuron's column says whether the seam you entered was ever there.

#### Systems Mode (still Diamonds)

Not Clubs abundance (Zerael wants full harvest warmth). Not Spades "mountain kills the soft."  

**Cold object:** the **entered truth of the deep** — assay, reserve, depth, structure of the void — independent of hope, surface deed, or how long the gallery seemed fine.

| Keep from old Tzuron | Drop / rehome |
|----------------------|---------------|
| Late failure of wrong entry (Half-Right energy) | Bridge span as master icon |
| "Almost" not a column | First stone of *building* foundation (→ Aretzon title-stone or Harashon craft) |
| Unhurried severity optional | Mountain sovereign as pure gravity-on-architecture |
| Engineer customers (narrow to **mine/quarry/deep** structure) | General guild-architect of every freestanding span |

**Half-Right Span → portable:** e.g. **The False Seam** / **The Gallery That Held:** every measurement of the adit right except one grade or one timber figure; worked for eleven years; was wrong the day of the assay. Same systems beat, depth skin.

**Staked Span → retarget:** guild stakes youth to assay/mine-measure early; Tza'iron still calls theft of optionality. Shared parable survives with new trade.

### Personality (locked)

**User:** calm, patient, unyielding. **Not slow** — **unhurried**.

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|

**vs Tzuron:** Both unhurried. Tzuron waits because gravity will make the argument; Aretzon is unhurried because the foundation was never in a race. Related weather, different object (span-math vs base claim).

**vs Qorathiel:** Regal absolute winter; Aretzon is patient base for building, not seasonal decree.

### Jack-row element cross-compare (user: hardest placement)

**User:** Aretzon was one of the hardest to place — needed an "element" for the Spades/Jack tetrad slot; Earth-in-Diamonds is what landed. Cross-compare how the four Jacks treat their element.

| Jack | Element | Suit / Mode | Cold pattern | Distance from substance | Tag | Element still felt? |
|------|---------|-------------|--------------|-------------------------|-----|---------------------|
| **Sarphon** | Fire | Spades Hard | Undiscerning burn; fuel is fuel; never checks | **Near-literal** | clean | Yes — flame, kindling, burn-out rite |
| **Demahel** | Water | Hearts Social | Undertow of *unspoken* feeling; tears private | **Metaphor / miscollapse** | miscollapse | Partial — current, tears, confluence minority |
| **Aretzon** | Earth | Diamonds Systems | Deed persists independent of ground/use | **Highest abstract** | clean purest optimizer | Least — soil almost absent; title is the object |
| **Sharaq** | Air | Clubs Sandbox | Distribution with no destination; seed on current | **Near-literal vector** | clean | Yes — wind/road/seed travel |

#### What each suit *does* to its element

| Suit grammar | Transform applied to element |
|--------------|-------------------------------|
| **Spades** | Element *is* the restriction: fire that will not distinguish (not "fire + rules," the burn *is* the rule) |
| **Hearts** | Element becomes social weather: water → current under a bond / private tears (not rivers-as-terrain) |
| **Diamonds** | Element becomes entry: earth → *eretz* as claimable land → deed text that outlasts dirt |
| **Clubs** | Element becomes unbounded process: air → spread without stop or planned arrival |

#### Asymmetry (why Earth felt hardest)

1. **Fire and Air stay close to classical behavior** filtered through mode (consume without check; move without destination). Easy native fit.
2. **Water jumped early** — oldest cults wanted confluence; grief-worship built unspoken-feeling. Tag owns the jump *(miscollapse)*. Tears keep a bodily water hook.
3. **Earth has no such bodily hook in current cult** — once you refuse Clubs soil/growth (Zerael/Rabakhon/Bitzon pressure) and refuse Spades hard-ground/terrain (Ya'aron/Tzuron-adjacent), what's left for Diamonds is **title**. That is correct Systems Mode and the *least elemental surface* on the row.
4. **Design already admits the Clubs temptation** for Earth; Fire Design admits hearth/craft temptation and strips it to Harashon. Same move, different leftover: Sarphon keeps *fire*; Aretzon keeps *claim*, not *earth*.

#### Parallel inside Diamonds (not only Jack)

Diamonds also abstracts "solid world" at **10 — Tzuron Mountains**: rock → load-bearing *calculation*, not mountain-as-hazard. So Aretzon's dematerialization is **suit-consistent**, not a one-off failure: Diamonds eats matter into entry (deed, figure, column).

Hearts does the same soft dematerialization at **10 — Midvaron**: desert → unanswered address / story weather. Element/terrain as social image is a Hearts habit.

#### Rank 10 terrains vs Rank J elements (adjacent ground)

| | More "place" | More "mode image" |
|--|--------------|-------------------|
| Spades | Ya'aron forests (cover, formation break) | Sarphon fire (restriction incarnate) |
| Hearts | Midvaron deserts (sparse + tales) | Demahel water (undertow of feeling) |
| Diamonds | Tzuron mountains-as-math | Aretzon earth-as-deed |
| Clubs | Bitzon swamps (ground that returns) | Sharaq air (vector spread) |

Jack is not "four nature gods." It is **four mode takes on the classical-element *slot***, with unequal remaining substance.

#### Placement diagnostics for Aretzon

| Question | Read |
|----------|------|
| Is the *domain axis* wrong? | No — Jack = elements tetrad is fixed like Ace seasons |
| Is *suit* wrong? | Clubs would be soil/growth — crowded and less pure systems; Spades would be hard ground/unyielding — overlaps Ya'aron/Tzuron weather; Hearts has no natural earth-claim social graph without forcing |
| Is *Diamonds claim* forced? | It's the **least bad pure mode read** of *eretz* once you won't do dirt-as-Clubs. "Hard to place" is accurate; outcome is still defensible |
| Should all four feel equally elemental? | Currently **no**. Equalizing would mean either re-grounding Aretzon (boundary stones, foundation, survey line as *earthy* systems) or admitting Earth is the abstract member the way Demahel is the miscollapsed member |

#### Options going forward (from cross-compare)

| | Path | Effect |
|--|------|--------|
| **1** | **Accept asymmetry** — Earth is the systems-abstract Jack; Perfect Deed is the point | Honest; purest optimizer stays; face optional B/C |
| **2** | **Re-ground without leaving Diamonds** — keep claim mechanic; restore *earth* texture (markers, foundations, surveyed ridge, soil description in the deed as sacred object) | Still systems; more "element" surface; surveyor delight (C) natural |
| **3** | **Own a Demahel-shaped story** — older earth-cult (standing on soil / foundation under feet) miscollapsed into pure title; tag → seam or miscollapse | Parallel Water's history; dual cult optional |
| **4** | **Reopen suit** (costly) — only if claim-law never felt like *Earth* enough; would force reshuffle of element row | High cost; not recommended unless tetrad is wrong |

**Lean from cross-compare:** Placement difficulty was structural (element tetrad × Systems Mode × crowded soil domains), not a bad call. Prefer **1 + light 2** (re-ground texture, keep claim) over reshuffle. Path 3 available if dual cult desired later.

# Tzuron (Ṣ-W-R) — Mountains (Diamonds 10)

**Sketch status:** Review opened — **overlap worry with Aretzon** (user).
**Fit tag (current):** *(clean)*
**Root:** Ṣ-W-R (*tzur*, rock/mountain-fastness). Renamed from Hararon (spelling vs Harashon).
**Roster:** *The Immovable Ledger — the calculation that doesn't forgive an error in scale; the rock face doesn't collapse for the builder who almost got the math right.*
**Gender:** she/her (sketches).

---

## The problem (user)

Aretzon (Earth Jack) and Tzuron (Mountains 10) risk reading as **the same god twice**: unhurried solid Diamonds women of immovable base / immovable math.

### Where they actually differ (mechanic — protect if kept)

| | **Aretzon** | **Tzuron** |
|--|-------------|------------|
| **Rank axis** | Element (Earth) | Terrain (Mountains) |
| **Systems object** | **Founding claim / title base** — may you rest further entries on this ground-as-property? | **Load-bearing figure** — will the built thing hold weight at this scale? |
| **Failure mode** | Broken continuity of claim; retired "for accuracy"; no base under the stack | Wrong number entered; structure fails when error finds its moment |
| **Doesn't care about** | Use, presence on soil, justice of ownership story | Ambition, urgency, good intention, "almost right" |
| **Customers** | Land-holders, surveyors, diaspora title | Engineers, guild-architects, load work |
| **Parable peak** | Perfect Deed (empty claim maintained) | Half-Right Span (stood eleven years; wrong day one) |
| **Truth type** | **Legal / cadastral** — entry of *right* | **Physical / structural** — entry of *scale* |

**One line if split holds:** Aretzon = *who/what base are we building the system on?* Tzuron = *does the math of what we built survive gravity?*

### Where they blur (surface — the real worry)

| Collision | Detail |
|-----------|--------|
| **Both "foundation"** | Aretzon locked as foundation-that-doesn't-move; Tzuron worship = **first stone of any foundation** + measurements |
| **Both unhurried / patient / ancient rock weather** | Aretzon: calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried-not-slow. Tzuron: no hurry, waits as long as gravity, ancient before first engineer |
| **Both immovable** | Foundation doesn't move / Immovable Ledger / rock face |
| **Both she** | Fine alone; twins when + weather match |
| **Both abstract solid → entry** | Diamonds habit; row adjacency amplifies |
| **Shared parable risk** | Staked Span (Tza'iron × Tzuron) is about early commitment to measure — lives near "base" talk |

**Verdict on worry:** **Legitimate on face and worship language. Not automatic on cold mechanic** — cadastral base vs structural scale is a clean Systems split *if prose stops sharing "foundation stone" and twin patience-as-identity.*

---

## Rank-10 context (what Mountains is for)

| Terrain 10 | Suit read |
|------------|-----------|
| Ya'aron Forests | Hard Mode: formation erased by cover |
| Midvaron Deserts | Social: unanswered address / story weather |
| **Tzuron Mountains** | Systems: scale/load entry that doesn't forgive |
| Bitzon Swamps | Sandbox: ground that returns / won't stay drained |

Mountains → engineering is already a **dematerialization** (like Aretzon earth → claim). Design admits Spades temptation (hostile rock) and refuses it. Good — but leaves two Diamonds "solid abstract" cards adjacent.

---

## Differentiation paths

| | Path | Effect |
|--|------|--------|
| **A. Hard fence only** | Keep both mechanics; strip shared language. Aretzon = claim/title/base **entry**. Tzuron = **load figure / span / scale** — never "foundation" as her word (guild may say pier, keystone, cut, measurement). Worship: not first-stone-of-foundation (Aretzon may keep title-stone); Tzuron = measurements read / proof load / figure entered before cut | Lowest cost; worry addressed in Design + worship |
| **B. Personality split** | Aretzon keeps calm/patient/unyielding/unhurried. Tzuron needs a **different face** so unhurried isn't the whole cast — e.g. impersonal as gravity (no "she waits," the number simply is); or dry engineer contempt for almost; or mountain-sovereign severity without Aretzon's base-pride | Face diversity; mechanic same |
| **C. Re-ground Tzuron toward mountain mass** | More *tzur* presence: vertical, mass, the face that doesn't negotiate — still Systems via the figure for grade/mass/hold, less pure abstract ledger voice | More terrain, less twin of Aretzon abstract |
| **D. Reopen Tzuron's Systems object** | If load-math feels like soft-Aretzon, ask: is Mountains better as something else in Diamonds (e.g. permanence of entered constraint; isolation of high ground as systems boundary)? Risk redesign | Only if A/B/C fail |
| **E. Spades mountains** | Hostile indifferent rock (Ya'aron sibling) — Design already rejected; costs Spades terrain row uniqueness | Not recommended |

**Lean for discussion:** **A + B.** Mechanic split is real; **kill foundation-word overlap**; give Tzuron a face that is not Aretzon's calm base-patience twin (gravity-impersonal or scale-severity). Protect Half-Right Span — it's the distinctive parable.

### Worship language fix (concrete)

| | Avoid | Prefer |
|--|-------|--------|
| **Aretzon** | Load, span, "will it hold" | Claim, title, survey, base entry, renew, markers of *boundary* |
| **Tzuron** | Deed, title, "foundation" as portfolio word, first stone as *claim* | Measurement, scale, load figure, span, cut, gravity's schedule, wrong the day drawn |

If both keep a stone rite: Aretzon = boundary/title stone; Tzuron = **witnessed measurements before the cut** (not "foundation stone" as shared icon).

### Personality options for Tzuron (if B)

| | Face | vs Aretzon |
|--|------|------------|
| **B1** | **Gravity-impersonal** — barely a temperament; the number will speak; no pride in base | Aretzon has base-pride and calm presence; Tzuron is thinner, colder mechanism |
| **B2** | **Scale-severity** — almost offended by "close enough"; mountain sovereign of proportion | Aretzon unyielding about *continuity*; Tzuron unyielding about *magnitude* |
| **B3** | Keep ancient patience | Weakest vs twin; only if A is ironclad |

**Default lean:** B1 or B2 over B3.

## What already works (Tzuron alone)

| Load figure, not intention | Clean Diamonds |
| Customers engineers not surveyors | Distinct if cults stay pure |
| Design vs Spades terrain | Correct refusal |
| Staked Span × Tza'iron | Optionality vs early measure — different from Aretzon × Taturiel |

---

## Cold gloss (candidate under A+B)

> **Mountains — the load figure that does not forgive scale.** Tzuron holds whether the number was entered true for what the structure must bear. Not title to the ground (Aretzon). Not the craft medium (Harashon). Not inherited manual warmth (Chakamon). Ambition and "almost" occupy no column. The span that stood eleven years was wrong the day it was drawn.

### Defense / flaw (current, still good)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Defense** | Will not grade on effort, hope, or delay of collapse |
| **Flaw** | Correct math can still sit inside an unjust project; patience of gravity is not mercy; "wrong the day drawn" erases lived years as evidence |

### Fences (sharpened)

| God | They | She |
|-----|------|-----|
| **Aretzon** | Founding claim / base title; unhurried foundation weather | Load/scale entry; not who owns the ridge |
| **Harashon** | Medium permits the make | Whether the make holds weight once built |
| **Chakamon** | Instruction copied intact | Number true for *this* load, not inherited opacity |
| **Ya'aron** | Cover erases formation (Hard) | Not tactics; structural figure |
| **Qorathiel** | Season absolute | Math absolute on a different axis |
| **Tza'iron** | Optionality / early stake theft | Measure committed too soon (shared parable) |

### Reportfolio option (user): Rank 10 is biomes — not locked to Mountains

**User:** Rank 10 is about biomes, so Diamonds need not be Mountains. Reportfolio Tzuron is on the table.

Diamonds needs a **biome** whose Systems Mode subject is **not** "immovable solid base" (Aretzon already has foundation/claim).

|---|-------|--------------------------------|------------|--------------|---------|-------|

| **1** | **Mines / Caves / Depths** | The entry for what you cannot see: depth, support, ore grade, air — wrong figure fails later underground | **Strong** — she is surface title/base; this is *below* and *assay/structure of the hole* | Not forest/desert/swamp | Yes (leave *tzur*) | Keeps "wrong day drawn, late death" energy; floating-city cliffs/quarries fit |
| **2** | **Rivers / Floodplains** | Measured water-as-system: flood mark, gauge, silt account, canal level — the figure the season must match | Strong (not title) | Not Demahel (feeling-current); not Zerael (yield) if stay on *gauge* not harvest | Yes | Risk soft-Chalufon (river trade) if widened |

#### If reportfolio: what happens to load-math?

Load-bearing engineering need not vanish from the pantheon — it can sit as:
- **Tenant of Harashon** (medium + structure as craft), or
- **Customer practice under new biome god** (mine timbering / harbor wall still "the figure"), or
- **Soft absorb into Aretzon** only for *foundation of building* — **no**, keep Aretzon cadastral; structural load needs a home or becomes anonymous guild skill

Staked Span can retarget: guild stakes youth to **chart-mastery**, **mine-assay**, or **gauge-reading** instead of span-engineering.

#### Decision tree

1. Is **Mountains sacred to the tetrad look** (forest/desert/mountain/swamp postcard)? → prefer **0** or **4**.
2. Is **clearing Aretzon** the priority? → **1** or **3**.
3. Is **Skyfall floating-city** flavor the priority? → **3** coasts/shoals (or **1** if cliff-quarry culture).

---


### Rank 10 principle (user — locked lean)

**Biomes are containers, not substrates.** Rank 10 is not "about the sand / the trees / the rocks / the mud" as stuff. Each suit reads **what that biome holds or does as mode weather**:

| Biome | Suit | Not the stuff | What it **contains / does** (mode) |
|-------|------|---------------|-------------------------------------|
| **Forests** | Spades | Not timber taxonomy | **Predatory cover** — formation erased; ground that hunts the disciplined line (Hard Mode restriction) |
| **Deserts** | Hearts | Not sand grains | **Mystique / sparse address** — stories on the wind, unanswered word, night-fire *we* (Social) |
| **Mountains** | Diamonds | **Not stacks of rock / load-pile architecture** | **Depths & resources** — caves, mines, seams, assay, what the mass holds (Systems entry of the deep) |
| **Swamps** | Clubs | Not mud chemistry | **Unemptiable return** — basin that comes back, growth that won't stay drained (Sandbox) |

So the Mountains reframe is not a special plea — it's **applying the same rule the row already uses**. Old Tzuron (span/gravity/foundation-stone) was treating the mountain as **building material / structural pile**, which is substrate-logic — and that's why she slid into Aretzon (Earth as foundation/base). Correct Diamonds read: mountain as **vault of the deep**, entry for contents.

**Aretzon fence under principle:** Earth Jack is element-as-foundation (base that doesn't move). Mountains 10 is biome-as-contents (what the high mass holds underneath). Different rank axes; no need to share "immovable rock woman" identity.

---
### User probe: Mountains = caves / mines / depths / resources (not stacks of rock)

**Idea:** Keep biome label **Mountains**. Change Systems subject from load-bearing *pile* (spans, gravity, first foundation stone) to what the mountain **holds and yields from within**: caves, mines, depths, resource bodies, assay, the deep account.

This is reportfolio of *function*, not of grid label — postcard tetrad intact; *tzur* still names the mountain; portfolio stops twinning Aretzon's surface foundation.

#### Why this works

| | **Aretzon (Earth)** | **Tzuron (Mountains, reframe)** |
|--|---------------------|----------------------------------|
| Layer | **Surface / base** — foundation claim, title, survey of ground you stand on or deed | **Depth / contents** — what the mass *contains*: ore, seam, void, grade, shaft account |
| Question | Is there a founding claim the system can rest on? | Is the **deep entry** true — grade, depth, support, reserve? |
| Failure | Broken title continuity; paper base with no standing | Wrong assay / wrong depth figure; mine that "worked" until the error found its level |
| Doesn't move | Foundation base of ownership | The mountain as **vault** — resource doesn't care about your surface story |
| Customers | Surveyors, land-holders, diaspora title | Miners, quarry-wards, assay masters, delvers, deep-guild engineers |
| Imagery | Boundary marker, deed, first stone of *title* | Adit, lamp, sample, seam map, timbered shaft |
| Parable shape | Perfect Deed (empty surface claim kept) | Wrong grade / thin timber / false reserve that held until it didn't |

**One-line split:** Aretzon = *who holds the ground.* Tzuron = *what the mountain holds.*

Mining **claim law** stays Aretzon (cadastral). **Geological/resource truth** of the hole stays Tzuron. Same ridge: her registry says you own it; Tzuron's column says whether the seam you entered was ever there.

#### Systems Mode (still Diamonds)

Not Clubs abundance (Zerael wants full harvest warmth). Not Spades "mountain kills the soft."  

**Cold object:** the **entered truth of the deep** — assay, reserve, depth, structure of the void — independent of hope, surface deed, or how long the gallery seemed fine.

| Keep from old Tzuron | Drop / rehome |
|----------------------|---------------|
| Late failure of wrong entry (Half-Right energy) | Bridge span as master icon |
| "Almost" not a column | First stone of *building* foundation (→ Aretzon title-stone or Harashon craft) |
| Unhurried severity optional | Mountain sovereign as pure gravity-on-architecture |
| Engineer customers (narrow to **mine/quarry/deep** structure) | General guild-architect of every freestanding span |

**Half-Right Span → portable:** e.g. **The False Seam** / **The Gallery That Held:** every measurement of the adit right except one grade or one timber figure; worked for eleven years; was wrong the day of the assay. Same systems beat, depth skin.

**Staked Span → retarget:** guild stakes youth to assay/mine-measure early; Tza'iron still calls theft of optionality. Shared parable survives with new trade.

#### vs neighbors

| God | Fence |
|-----|--------|
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title vs deep contents/resource figure |
| **Zerael** | Warm surface yield without theory vs entered deep reserve (cold systems) |
| **Rabakhon** | Biological replication process vs mineral/void extract account |
| **Ashiron** | Coin balance after sale vs resource still in the mountain / grade true |
| **Harashon** | Medium of the make (including timber/steel craft) vs whether the *hole's* numbers were true |
| **Chakamon** | Inherited manual copy vs this seam's assay (may use old book wrong) |
| **Ya'aron** | Cover erases formation on surface forest vs under-mountain systems |
| **Bitzon** | Ground that returns/grows vs mountain that is **extracted** (opposite vector) |
| **Taturiel** | First step on ridge vs first lamp in a mapped dark |

#### Personality (under reframe — options)

Depth weather differs from Aretzon's calm surface foundation:

| | Face | Play |
|--|------|------|
| **D1** | **Reserve / vault** | Holds much, reveals by measure; unhurried because the seam is older than the claim |
| **D2** | **Assay-severity** | Sample is sacred; "looks rich" is not a column; contempt for surface optimism |
| **D3** | **Deep-calm** | Quiet of caves; patient; not Aretzon's base-pride — **withholding** pride (the mountain keeps its own counsel until entered true) |

Avoid twinning Aretzon's exact "calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried" package — related still (both solid, both unhurried possible) but **identity** is vault/assay/depth, not foundation-that-doesn't-move.

#### Name

**Tzuron** (*tzur*) can **stay** — rock/mountain-fastness as the body that *contains* the deep, not as a stack of load-bearing stones. Design one-liner: mountains as resource-body / underworld of stone, not architecture.

#### Fit tag

Likely still *(clean)*: Mountains → mines/depths is a natural mortal read; load-span was the more forced pure-optimizer abstract. Optional *(seam)* if old span-cult minority remembers bridge-Tzuron — only if you want that history.

#### Lean

**Strong path:** Keep Mountains; reportfolio to **depths / mines / caves / resources**. Clears Aretzon without leaving the biome row. Rename optional (keep Tzuron). Rewrite worship, parable skin, Staked Span aim; drop foundation-stone collision entirely.

## Discussion prompts

1. Accept **mechanic split** (claim vs load) as enough if language is cleaned?
2. Personality: gravity-impersonal (B1), scale-severity (B2), or keep twin patience (B3)?
3. Must Tzuron's worship drop "first stone of foundation"?
4. Any desire to put more *mountain* back (path C) without Spades-ifying?

6. **Mountains = depths/resources** (not stacks) — lock this reframe?


### Personality probe: spelunking wanderlust (Clubs creep) — Lara Croft without monsters

**User:** Allow Tzuron to creep a bit into Clubs — wanderlust, spelunking; loves the adventure; almost Lara Croft feel **without the monsters**.

#### What that face is

| Trait | Play |
|-------|------|
| **Wanderlust** | Not content at the registry desk; the deep is for *going* |
| **Spelunker** | Caves, mines, adits, unmapped dark as joy, not only workplace |
| **Adventure-love** | Delight in the descent, the squeeze, the first lamp around a bend |
| **Competent delver** | Lara Croft competence/curiosity — **not** combat, tombs full of guardians, or trophy killing |
| **Not** | Monster-slayer; Taturiel's first-step-on-any-ridge; pure optionality abandon (Tza'iron); freedom-as-unclosed question (Derorel) |


#### User correction: not real Clubs theft

**User:** Spelunking only "creeps on Clubs" in a soft sense — **Clubs is not about exploration in specific**, so there is **no real suit-level fence**. Freedom-of-exploration energy is **inferred from uncontained/Sandbox mode**, not a portfolio Clubs monopolizes.

| Clarify | |
|---------|--|
| **Clubs mode** | Uncontained: growth without design, without a stop; optionality, distribution, abandon, first-step *as cards* — not "the adventure suit" |
| **Exploration (specific)** | **Taturiel** alone (Queen) — first step, unmapped, retains nothing |
| **Freedom (specific)** | **Derorel** — open question, not delving |
| **Tzuron adventure** | Personality + method for **Diamonds deep-entry** — does not steal a Clubs domain that doesn't exist as "exploration generally" |

**Implication:** Drop anxiety about "Clubs creep" as suit violation. Keep **card fences** only where real:
- **Taturiel** — mechanism of first-step/forgetting/unmapped, not "you can't enjoy caves"
- **Derorel / Tza'iron / Sharaq** — only if her joy becomes their cold gloss (endless unclosed, abandon-as-theology, no destination)

She can be fully Lara-Croft-delver-joyful **without** being a Clubs god. The uncontained *flavor* of wanderlust is available to any suit's face; Clubs doesn't own it.

**Revised framing:** Not "Clubs-colored seam." Prefer: **delver joy as (seam) on Systems deep-contents** — extra worship built on assay/depths, parallel Chakamon warmth. Optional note that Sandbox *mode* also loves going further, but that's mode rhyme not portfolio theft.

---
#### Why it can still be Diamonds

Clubs creep is real — own it as **face/method**, not domain swap:

| Diamonds core (keep) | Clubs-colored how |
|----------------------|-------------------|
| Deep entry must be true (assay, map of seam, depth figure) | She **wants to go get it** — truth of the mountain is verified by descent, not only by desk |
| Resource/contents systems | Adventure is how mortals *meet* the vault, not abundance-without-theory (Zerael) |
| Wrong entry fails later | She loves the delving *and* still won't bless a pretty lie about the seam |

**Cold gloss add:** present for the **entered deep** — and worshipped as one who is **glad to go down**. Domain is still contents/systems of the mountain; personality is delver-joy.

**If she only loved adventure with no care for the figure:** she'd be soft-Taturiel. Fence: she can love the trip and still be the god of **whether what you brought back (or claimed was down there) was entered true**.

#### Fences (critical)

| God | They hold | She holds |
|-----|-----------|-----------|
| **Taturiel** | First step; unmapped *as such*; discovery creates ground; forgets she's been | **Known mountain body** — depths of *this* mass; return with a true account; not "anywhere unmapped" |
| **Derorel** | Freedom as unclosed question / won't settle the life-question | Adventure as **method toward a deep entry**, not endless refusal to conclude |
| **Tza'iron** | Optionality; abandon the path; stake as theft | May love many descents; still honors a **finished true assay** of a seam (can finish a map) |
| **Sharaq** | Distribution, no destination | She **has** a destination: the deep / the seam / the chamber |
| **Aretzon** | Surface foundation/title | Under the claim, what's held |
| **Yetziron** | Variation for its own sake | Variation of path only to get the deep figure right |
| **Midvaron** | Adventurous desert *story* heat | Adventurous **descent** — lamp, rope, rock dark; different register |

#### Tag implications

| Tag | If |
|-----|-----|
| **(clean)** | "Mountain contents" naturally attracts delver cults; adventure is how you meet assay — domain implies the face |
| **(seam)** | Domain is deep entry; **love of adventure** is extra worship built (nothing in pure assay requires joy) — apprentices describe thrill theology can't require |
| **(miscollapse)** | Older cult was pure vault/assay desk-god; delver joy is later drift |

**Lean:** **(seam)** is honest and good — same family as Chakamon warmth (nothing about transmission requires affection). Adventure-love is the seam that makes her vivid; assay remains the column.

#### vs Aretzon (still clear)

Aretzon: calm, patient, unyielding, unhurried foundation — **stays**.  
Tzuron: **moves into the dark on purpose** — opposite energy, same Diamonds suit. Twin problem dissolves.

#### Risks

1. **Taturiel bleed** — fix with "this mountain's deep," return-with-account, not first-print-anywhere.  
2. **Becomes pulp archaeologist only** — keep Systems: sample, map, grade, false seam parable.  
3. **Monsters/traps drift** — user veto: **no**; wonder of the deep, not bestiary.  
4. **Gender/Lara coding** — fine with she; competence and joy, not pinup checklist.

#### One-line (candidate)

> *She loves the going down — rope, lamp, the bend that isn't on the last survey — and still only blesses the descent that brings back a true account of what the mountain holds.*

#### Personality lock package (candidate)

| | |
|--|--|
| **Wanderlust / spelunker joy** | Lara Croft-without-monsters: competent, curious, glad of the deep |
| **Systems still** | Assay, seam map, depth figure, false entry fails later |
| **Seam** | Adventure-love not required by domain; cults built it |
| **Not** | Surface title (Aretzon); first step anywhere (Taturiel); abandon as theology (Tza'iron) |

---

### Diegetic tension: portfolio creep + Taturiel's long campaign (user)

**User:** Historical gods often creep toward other portfolios without expressly violating them. Build that tension in — internal friction, and/or **Taturiel has been trying to get Tzuron to change modes for millennia.**

#### Design principle

Seams and soft creeps are not only author mistakes to fence away. They are **how pantheons feel lived-in**: a Systems god who loves delving will *read* as Exploration-adjacent to mortals and to other gods, without her cold column becoming Taturiel's.

| Layer | What lives there |
|-------|------------------|
| **Cold / mode** | Diamonds: deep contents entered true — **does not change** |
| **Worship face** | Spelunker joy, wanderlust — soft creep toward uncontained *feel* |
| **Friction** | Cults, sister-gods, and maybe Tzuron herself disagree how much is the trip vs the account |
| **Taturiel's project** | For millennia: treat Tzuron as a Sandbox god who hasn't admitted it; recruit delvers; reframe every true map as a first step |

#### How hard to play (options)

| | Form | Payoff |
|--|------|--------|
| **F1** | Design-only | Clean; low story fuel |
| **F2** | Cult friction — assay orthodoxy vs delver thrills; Taturiel's scouts claim sisterhood | Diegetic without FC dialogue |
| **F3** | God-to-god campaign — Taturiel at her for millennia to switch modes; Tzuron never converts | Best character; comedy + theology |
| **F4** | F2+F3 | Richest |

**LOCKED: F4** — cult friction **and** god-to-god conversations.

**User refinement:** The conversations are **real**, but **neither side really expects an actual mode change.** Not a conversion campaign with hope of success; a **millennia-long practice** of the argument itself — fond, serious, unserious by turns, always renewed.

| | |
|--|--|
| **Taturiel** | Restarts the pitch (forgetting helps; every age is a first time she "really" means it, or performs meaning it) — without budgeting for Tzuron to flip |
| **Tzuron** | Answers, deflects, enjoys, or endures — without budgeting for herself to leave Diamonds |
| **Cults** | Assay orthodoxy vs delver thrills; scouts claim sisterhood; mountain priests file the talk as known weather, not pending schism |
| **What would break the gag** | Either side *actually* treating flip as imminent policy — keep it lived argument, not plot countdown |

**Asymmetry still works:** Taturiel may not carry the millennia as failure; Tzuron and the cults do. Neither mistakes the talk for a referendum that will pass.

#### Running joke: the suit-even trade (user — locked)

**The pitch's comic form:** not only "become Clubs," but **exchange Tzuron for one of the more boring Clubs gods** so the suits stay even (13/13). Cosmic HR. Portfolio swap as bookkeeping.

| | |
|--|--|
| **Joke structure** | Diamonds loses a fun delver; Clubs gains her; Clubs dumps someone dull into Diamonds to keep the count |
| **Neither expects it** | Still F4 — real talk, zero budget for the trade clearing |
| **Who is "boring"** | **Not fixed in doctrine.** Different ages, different names offered (craft-rule pedant, swamp-patience, harvest-warmth — whoever the speaker finds least Sandbox-*feeling* that century). **Taturiel especially** may offer a *different* Clubs god each time and not recall last millennium's nominee — first-step memory applied to cosmic swap meets |
| **Tzuron's side** | May name who she'd dump *into* Clubs if forced (a clinical Diamond, a desk god) — same unserious seriousness |
| **Cult echo** | Delvers chant "we'd take the trade"; assay priests file it next to other jokes that got written down too carefully |
| **Design** | Suit balance anxiety as in-fiction comedy; author knows modes aren't horse-traded; worshippers and sister-gods play as if they could be |

**One-line add:** *The running joke is a swap to keep the suits even — Tzuron for whichever Clubs god seems boring this age — and after millennia of meetings, nobody has brought a quill to the actual transfer.*

#### Internal friction (Tzuron)

| Pole | Pull |
|------|------|
| **Vault / assay** | True figure; false seam fails later; Systems pride |
| **Lamp / descent** | Joy of going down; the bend not on the survey |

She does not resolve into pure desk or pure Taturiel. Friction *is* personality: glad of the trip, still standing on the account.

#### Taturiel's campaign (sketch lines)

- **Her angle:** Every adit is a first step into dark; caring about the return account is a cage; come to Clubs.
- **Tzuron's non-conversion:** Lamp *and* sample. Millennia of "join us" → still Diamonds. Fond, exasperated, or politely unchanged — not necessarily hostile.
- **Why millennia works:** Taturiel may not experience long failure (no retention). Tzuron does.
- **Optional shared parable:** scout and delver both "first" into a cave; one leaves the first thing untouched; one brings a true grade home.

#### One-line (with friction)

> *She loves the going down and still only blesses a true account of what the mountain holds — a tension she and Taturiel have rehearsed for millennia, including the running joke that Clubs would trade her for whichever of their duller gods keeps the suits even, without either side ever expecting the transfer to file.*
